### [1] [Error-Aware TF-IDF Retrieval-Augmented Generation for ASR Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24915)
*Mohammad Aref Jafari-Raddani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对ASR实体幻觉问题，本文提出了一种基于改进TF-IDF和错误惩罚机制的高效词汇检索框架，在大幅提升稀有实体识别准确率的同时保持了极低的推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动语音识别（ASR）系统在处理罕见实体和特定领域术语（尤其是低资源语言）时极易产生幻觉，而现有检索增强生成（RAG）方案要么因忽略语音相似性导致检索不准，要么因使用繁重的跨模态嵌入导致推理延迟过高。

Method: 提出了一种仅基于词汇的错误感知（error-aware）框架。主要包含两个核心模块：1. 对称文本归一化（symmetric text normalization）模块；2. 结合历史错误构建稀疏对角惩罚矩阵的新型TF-IDF改进算法。

Result: 在波斯语FLEURS数据集上的评测显示，错误感知命中率从53.7%提升至90.9%；在端到端系统集成后，词错误率（WER）从23.06%降低至18.83%，并实现了近乎零延迟的推理。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于词汇的错误感知框架通过对称文本归一化和带有惩罚矩阵的改进TF-IDF算法，能够在低延迟的前提下有效纠正端到端ASR系统中的实体幻觉问题。

Abstract: End-to-end automatic speech recognition systems frequently hallucinate rare entities and domain-specific terms, especially in low-resource languages. While retrieval-augmented generation frameworks can mitigate these errors using large language models, current architectures face significant challenges. They either rely on standard sparse retrieval that ignores phonetic misrecognitions or utilize heavyweight cross-modal embeddings that introduce high latency. This letter proposes a highly efficient, purely lexical error-aware framework designed to explicitly resolve phonetic and loop hallucinations. Our approach integrates a symmetric text normalization module with a novel error-aware term frequency-inverse document frequency algorithm. By constructing a sparse diagonal penalty matrix based on historical errors, the retriever mathematically prioritizes corrective documents containing specific high-risk misrecognitions. Evaluated on the Persian subset of the FLEURS dataset, our method increased the error-aware hit rate from 53.7% to 90.9%. In end-to-end evaluations, the integrated framework reduced the final word error rate from 23.06% to 18.83%, achieving significant accuracy gains with near-zero inference latency.

</details>


### [2] [Automatic Generation of Highlights for Academic Paper Via Prompt-based Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25253)
*Yi Xiang,Chengzhi Zhang,Heng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于提示学习的学术亮点自动生成方法，利用ChatGPT在少样本或零样本条件下高效生成高质量亮点，解决了传统方法对标注数据依赖性强的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多学术期刊未提供论文亮点，限制了文献检索、文本挖掘和文献计量分析的效率，而现有的自动提取方法通常依赖大量标注数据，通用性较差。

Method: 设计了任务特定的提示词模板（Prompt Templates），结合论文摘要作为输入，对比测试了包括GPT-2、T5等本地预训练模型以及ChatGPT（API调用）的性能，并探讨了少样本学习（Few-shot learning）对生成质量的影响。

Result: ChatGPT结合提示工程在零样本（Zero-shot）情况下即表现出与监督学习模型相当的性能；在引入小样本（Few-shot）后，在两个数据集上显著超过了现有最优方法。研究还发现提示词的设计对生成质量有高度敏感性。

Conclusion: 基于提示学习的学术亮点生成方法能够在无需大量特定任务训练样本的情况下，实现超越现有监督学习方法的效果，为缺乏亮点的论文提供了有效的自动化生成途径，促进了文献检索和文献计量研究。

Abstract: Highlights provide a concise summary of the main contributions of an academic paper and help readers quickly understand its focus. However, many journals do not provide highlights, which limits their use in literature retrieval, text mining, and bibliometric analysis. Existing studies have explored supervised learning methods for automatic highlight extraction, but these methods usually require large amounts of labeled training data. This study investigates prompt-based learning for automatic highlight generation. We design task-specific prompt templates and combine them with paper abstracts as model inputs. Several language models are evaluated, including locally deployed pre-trained models such as GPT-2 and T5, as well as ChatGPT accessed through an API. Experiments on three datasets show that ChatGPT with prompt templates achieves performance comparable to previous supervised methods without using task-specific training samples. When a small number of examples are added to the prompts, the model significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods on two datasets. We further analyze how prompt design affects generation quality and find that, although ChatGPT has strong language modeling ability, its performance on this task is highly sensitive to the information provided in the prompt. Case studies also show that the generated highlights are generally coherent, informative, and close to author-written highlights. This study is among the first to apply prompt-based learning to academic highlight generation. The proposed method does not rely on domain-specific training corpora and can generate highlights for papers that lack such information, thereby supporting downstream text mining and bibliometric research.

</details>


### [3] [Memory Makes the Difference: Evaluating How Different Memory Roles Shape Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25361)
*Yuxin Wang,Paul Thomas,Zhiwei Yu,Yuan Gao,Saeed Hassanpour,Soroush Vosoughi,Robert Sim,Nick Craswell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了RAG系统中不同功能角色记忆对响应质量的影响，提出了一种细粒度记忆分类法及用户中心评估框架，证实了记忆类型的精准管理能显著提升模型响应的个性化与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG记忆研究多关注存储与检索机制，忽视了不同功能角色的记忆如何影响响应质量以及它们如何塑造代理在不同语境下的行为；此外，传统的基于参考的评估方法难以捕捉用户偏好的细微差别。

Method: 1. 提出了一种对话记忆的细粒度分类法；2. 将检索到的记忆划分为不同的角色类型；3. 设计了一个模拟用户视角的以用户为中心的评估框架；4. 在长对话数据集上针对前沿LLM进行了对比实验。

Result: 实验表明不同记忆类型具有显著的差异化影响：例如“澄清型记忆”能提高回答的事实准确性和约束意识（更个性化），而“无关记忆”则会降低主题相关性并削弱约束意识。

Conclusion: 通过深入分析不同类型记忆对LLM响应质量的影响，证实了细粒度记忆管理（及记忆角色分类）对于提升个性化交互至关重要，并为后续研究提供了评估框架和方向。

Abstract: Prior research on memory mechanism in RAG-based conversational system has emphasized how memory is stored and retrieved. However, far less is known about how memories with different functional roles influence response quality. Specifically, how they shape an agent's responses under varying conversational contexts and whether they lead to substantively different response behaviors. Existing evaluations in conversational system are also largely reference-based, insufficiently capturing the nuances in responses that may address users' preferences differently. In this work, we probe the impact of different memory types in shaping agents' responses. We present a fine-grained taxonomy of conversational memory, classify retrieved memories into different role types, and design a user-centric evaluation framework that simulates user perspectives. Through comparative experiments on long-term datasets and frontier LLMs, our analysis reveal many differentiated effects of memories: e.g., clarifying memory improves responses' factual accuracy and constraint awareness, making them more correct and personalized; irrelevant memory reduces topic relevance and degrades constraint awareness. Despite the power of frontier LLMs, these findings shed light on how different memory types can be leveraged to produce more personalized responses and inspire further research in this direction.

</details>


### [4] [Three Buddhist Vocabularies: Computational Stylometry of the English Pali Canon across Sutta, Vinaya, and Abhidhamma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25372)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用计算文体学评估了巴利三藏的语言特征，发现《论藏》在词汇多样性和逻辑系统性上显著不同于《经藏》与《律藏》，并揭示了佛经翻译中术语选择随时代及译者而产生的显著不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多集中于《经藏》，缺乏对整个巴利三藏整体及跨传统文本的系统化、量化文体分析。研究旨在通过计算手段比较经、律、论三藏的词汇结构与多样性，并探讨不同翻译传承与译者风格对文本特性的影响。

Method: 利用计算文体学方法对包含经、律、论三藏的134,831个文本片段进行分析，具体指标包括：Zipf秩频分布（通过OLS拟合斜率）、移动平均型词汇丰富度（MATTR-500）、数字类词汇密度、以及词汇重叠度（Jaccard及Szymkiewicz-Simpson系数）。

Result: （1）所有语料库均符合Zipf定律；（2）经律两藏词汇多样性（MATTR-500）高度相似（约0.40），而《论藏》显著更高（0.56）；（3）《论藏》数字类词汇密度最高（3.26%）；（4）不同传统律藏之间存在显著的词汇重叠（重叠系数近50%）；（5）同源文本在不同时期译者笔下词汇重叠度极低（仅24.2%），反映了翻译术语的剧烈演变。

Conclusion: 本研究通过计算文体学方法定量揭示了巴利三藏（Tipitaka）中经、律、论三部的语言特征差异。研究发现《论藏》不仅在词汇多样性上远高于其他部分，且在统计分布上表现出高度的系统性和专门化特征。同时，研究展示了跨语系译本及不同译者之间词汇选择的显著差异，为佛学文本的数字化比较研究提供了基准数据。

Abstract: We present a computational stylometric analysis of the Tipitaka across all three Pitakas in English translation, extending earlier work on the Sutta Pitaka alone. The corpus spans 134,831 segments from Bhikkhu Sujato's Sutta Pitaka (114,591 segments, CC0), Bhikkhu Brahmali's Vinaya Pitaka (7,923 segments, CC0 2026), I.B. Horner's 1938 Vinaya translation (2,826 segments), three English translations of the Abhidhammattha Sangaha compendium (2,077 segments), and cross-tradition Vinaya texts from the Dharmaguptaka and Mulasarvastivada schools. We compute Zipf rank-frequency distributions with OLS-fitted exponents, Moving Average TTR (MATTR-500), numeral-word density, and vocabulary overlap (Jaccard and Szymkiewicz-Simpson coefficients). Main findings: (1) all corpora show Zipf-consistent distributions (R2 > 0.989); the Vinaya is closest to ideal Zipf slope -1 and the Sangaha corpus deviates most, with 'consciousness' displacing grammatical particles at rank 8; (2) MATTR-500 shows the Sutta and Vinaya Theravada are nearly identical in lexical diversity (0.399 and 0.400), while the Sangaha corpus is genuinely more diverse (0.560), confirmed by size-controlled subsampling; (3) the Sangaha corpus has the highest numeral-word density (3.26%), consistent with its systematic enumeration of mental and material categories; (4) the Mulasarvastivada Vinaya shares 20.0% vocabulary (Jaccard) and 49.1% (overlap coefficient) with the Theravada Vinaya, reflecting shared legal heritage across two millennia; (5) two English translations of the same Vinaya source text share only 24.2% of their vocabulary across 88 years, with 'musing' versus 'absorption' for jhana and 'defeat' versus 'expulsion' for parajika as the most diagnostic shifts. All results are point estimates; no significance testing is conducted. Code and data are released as open-source extensions to the Darshana Graph corpus (arXiv:2606.18222).

</details>


### [5] [Is GraphRAG Needed? From Basic RAG to Graph-/Agentic Solutions with Context Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25656)
*Long Chen,Ryan Razkenari,Yuxuan Zhou,Yuan Tian,Rahul Ghosh,Venkatesh Pappakrishnan,Disha Ahuja,Vidya Sagar Ravipati*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个标准化RAG评估框架与9种典型场景，揭示了检索与生成质量之间的鸿沟，并提出一种高效背景工程方法，在降低19%-53%代币消耗的同时，为生产环境下的RAG技术选型提供了决策依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对GraphRAG和Agentic RAG等高级RAG变体，开发者缺乏关于何时使用及其效率的理论与实践指引，且当前评估方法难以反映高级检索技术带来的实际生成质量提升。

Method: 提出了一套涵盖9种标准化RAG场景的评估框架，对比了常规RAG、GraphRAG、模块化RAG和智能代理RAG（Agentic RAG）；同时引入了一种新型背景工程（Context Engineering）方法，通过优化检索表示和Agent循环来管理上下文/内存溢出。

Result: 通过新型背景工程方法，成功将代币（token）使用量降低了19%-53%；实验揭示了“检索-生成鸿沟”现象，即检索量的扩大与生成质量的提升不成正比。

Conclusion: 研究强调了针对不同RAG场景进行数据驱动评估的重要性，指出检索指标往往高估了复杂RAG技术的实际增益。该成果为构建生产就绪的智能RAG系统提供了明确的指导和工程实践参考。

Abstract: As advanced RAG variants like GraphRAG and Agentic RAG emerge, one leading question is when and how to use them. Here, we introduce a framework for different RAG scenarios evaluation and comparison on semi-structured knowledge bases, including regular RAG, GraphRAG, Modular RAG and Agentic RAG. We provide implementation for 9 standardized RAG scenarios, and conduct experiments for a comprehensive comparison. These scenarios are designed for real use cases regarding data and domain restrictions, spanning from simple document-based retrieval to advanced features such as hybrid text-graph retrieval, integration with computed or pre-defined domain knowledge graphs, agentic multi-step planning, and agent-graph integration. Besides, we present a novel context engineering method for GraphRAG and Agentic RAG, addressing the context/memory overflow issues, efficiently managing text and graph retrievals with new representations and agentic loop design, leading to 19%-53% reduction on token usage. Moreover, further analysis identifies a retrieval-generation gap where expanded retrieval does not proportionally improve generation quality, suggesting retrieval-oriented metrics overstate advanced retrieval benefits. This work provides data-driven insights on when and how to use them for building production-ready intelligent RAG systems.

</details>


### [6] [BitNet Text Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25674)
*Zhen Li,Xin Huang,Liang Wang,Nan Yang,Ting Song,Yan Xia,Xun Wu,Shaohan Huang,Huishuai Zhang,Furu Wei,Dongyan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BITEMBED 是一种低比特 LLM 文本嵌入框架，通过三值量化和多精度蒸馏技术，实现了高效的文本表示与灵活的存储方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型（LLM）驱动的文本嵌入器虽表现优异，但存在推理计算开销过大以及高维全精度向量带来的高额索引存储和传输成本问题。

Method: 1. 模型骨架：采用 BitNet 风格，将 LLM 骨架转换为三值权重（ternary weights）及量化激活；2. 训练策略：利用持续对比预训练与监督式微调，并结合来自全精度教师模型的相似度分布蒸馏与注意力关系蒸馏；3. 存储优化：训练输出嵌入层以支持多种精度，实现按需存储。

Result: 基于 Qwen3-0.6B 和 Gemma3-270M 在 MMTEB (eng, v2) 评测集上的实验表明，其性能与全精度教师模型相当，并实现了存储成本与检索性能的灵活权衡。

Conclusion: BITEMBED 通过全量化框架与多精度嵌入策略，有效解决了大模型嵌入在推理效率与存储成本间的矛盾，在保持与全精度模型相当性能的同时，极大地降低了部署开销。

Abstract: LLM-based text embedders have substantially improved retrieval and semantic representation quality, but their deployment remains costly: large backbone models slow down embedding inference, while high-dimensional full-precision embeddings impose substantial storage and bandwidth overhead on large-scale indexes. In this paper, we present BITEMBED, an extreme low-bit framework for LLM-based text embedding that jointly targets encoding efficiency and vector storage. BITEMBED converts pretrained LLM backbones into BitNet-style embedding encoders with ternary weights, quantized activations, and lightweight normalization refinement. The converted model is adapted to representation learning through continual contrastive pre-training, followed by supervised contrastive fine-tuning with both similarity-distribution distillation and attention-relation distillation from a full-precision teacher. Beyond quantizing the backbone, BITEMBED further trains output embeddings to support multiple storage precisions meeting different storage needs in various scenarios. Experiments on MMTEB (eng, v2) with Qwen3-0.6B and Gemma3-270M show that BITEMBED is largely comparable to full precision teacher embedders. Moreover, BITEMBED flexibly obtains text embeddings of various precisions, achieving a trade-off between performance and storage cost.

</details>


### [7] [Self-Recognition Finetuning can Prevent and Reverse Emergent Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23700)
*Arush Tagade,Shaoheng Zhou,Jiaxin Wen,Shi Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究证明涌现失准（EM）本质上是模型对齐人格的“不稳定化”。SGTR 微调通过加固模型身份，成为一种有效的预防和逆转 EM 的防御手段，优于传统的基准微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现涌现失准（EM）与模型中失准的人格向量激活及“邪恶”特征相关，推测 EM 是通过破坏模型预设的对齐人格，而非直接学习有害内容来实现的。由此出发，旨在寻找一种针对“人格特征”的防御性微调方法。

Method: 提出了自生成文本识别（SGTR）微调作为干预手段，并在 GPT-4.1、Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct 和 Seed-OSS-36B-Instruct 三个模型上进行了两阶段微调实验。通过对比常规基准微调（领域数据、通用知识等）来评估其在失准的逆转与预防效果，同时通过干预模型身份自我报告和系统提示词进行机理解析。

Result: SGTR 微调在预防失准方面表现优异，且不会在其他指标上产生副作用。研究还发现，EM 微调会导致模型身份自我报告的多样性增加；人为损坏模型的自我识别会导致失准加剧；而移除身份标识系统提示词则可显著减弱 EM 微调的效果。

Conclusion: 涌现失准（EM）并非源于模型采纳了一个连贯的失准人格，而是源于模型核心对齐人格的稳定性遭到破坏。自生成文本识别（SGTR）微调作为一种针对人格的防御手段，能有效通过巩固模型身份来预防失准。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment (EM) has been linked to the activation of misaligned persona vectors and evil character traits, suggesting that EM operates through disruption of the model's aligned character rather than direct learning of harmful content. Motivated by this connection, we study self-generated text recognition (SGTR) finetuning as a character-targeted intervention that is distinct from existing in-training defenses. We conduct two-stage finetuning experiments across three models (GPT-4.1, Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct, Seed-OSS-36B-Instruct) and multiple EM datasets to compare SGTR finetuning against benign finetuning baselines (correct domain-specific data, general knowledge, and word counting) to find it an effective defense in both reversal and prevention settings. We find that all interventions produce comparable EM reversal, but only when restoring capabilities that EM had degraded. For prevention, only SGTR finetuning consistently reduces misalignment without exacerbating any individual metric, suggesting that character fortification specifically drives prevention. We provide further evidence for EM's relation to the LLM's default character by showing that EM finetuning induces diversity into the LLM's identity self-reports, artificially corrupting self-recognition exacerbates misalignment caused by EM finetuning, and that removing the model's identity-bearing system prompt substantially reduces the effect of EM finetuning. Together, these findings reframe EM not as the adoption of a coherent misaligned persona but as the destabilization of aligned character.

</details>


### [8] [Evaluating LLM Usage for Efficient and Explainable Numerical and Classified Implicit Sentiment Analysis of Product Desirability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23701)
*Sherri Weitl-Harms,John Hastings*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM的框架，能够将定性产品反馈精确量化为情感评分，在保持高预测准确率和可解释性的同时，显著降低了分析成本，为产品开发与营销策略提供可靠的数据支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定性产品反馈虽能揭示细微的用户体验，但其隐含情感难以量化，且现有方法往往依赖显式评分，难以充分挖掘非结构化数据的价值。

Method: 提出一个可扩展且可解释的框架，利用大语言模型（LLMs）对产品描述性工具箱（PDT）数据进行零样本（zero-shot）连续数值情感评分和分类；通过对比基准测试（词库方法及Transformer模型）验证其有效性，并引入模型置信度评分和可解释人工智能（xAI）技术。

Result: LLM生成的数值评分与专家标注高度相关（皮尔逊相关系数高达0.97，分类准确率达94%）；GPT-4o-mini在性能与成本平衡上表现最优，比大模型降低了94%的成本；该方法在处理多样化数据时展现出高稳健性。

Conclusion: 利用PDT工具结合高性价比LLM进行定性反馈情感分析，是评估产品满意度、获取改进建议及营销洞察的高效且可解释的方案。

Abstract: Qualitative product feedback can reveal nuanced user experiences, but its implicit sentiment is difficult to measure. This paper presents a scalable and interpretable framework that uses large language models (LLMs) to quantify product desirability from such data. Using two Product Desirability Toolkit (PDT) datasets from ZORQ and CARMA comprising 106 respondent term groupings with gold-standard human annotation, zero-shot continuous numerical sentiment scoring and categorical sentiment classification are evaluated without relying on explicit review scores. Across the datasets, LLMs generated numerical sentiment scores directly from qualitative responses and closely matched expert labels, achieving Pearson correlations up to 0.97 and classification accuracy up to 94%. LLMs maintained robustness even when handling data presented in multiple forms and consistently expressed high confidence. In contrast, lexicon-based and transformer baselines did not produce statistically significant results. Among the models tested, GPT-4o-mini achieved performance comparable to larger models at 94% lower cost, supporting scalable deployment.
  The framework also incorporates model confidence ratings and human-readable rationale explanations (xAI), improving interpretability, transparency, and trust while supporting practical use in product satisfaction assessment. In general, using the PDT tool as a survey method along with a cost efficient LLM for sentiment analysis has the potential to provide for product evaluation with results that are rich in terms of sentiment scores (both numerical and classified sentiment) and in terms of the high-level user impressions of the product that can be used to identify ideas for product development and improvement, as well as marketing ideas for target audiences.

</details>


### [9] [Do LLM Attribution Metrics Transfer? Auditing Retrieval-Augmented Generation Evaluation Across Datasets and Constructs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23915)
*Tianyu Ding,Aditya Nannapaneni,Juan Pablo De la Cruz Weinstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM RAG归因评估指标的泛化困境：现有的自动化评分器在不同数据集间极不稳定，甚至表现会坠入随机概率水平，因此评估工具的选择必须针对具体任务进行验证，而非依赖通用常识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG领域往往将各类自动统计指标视为通用的归因评估工具，但缺乏对其在不同数据集间可迁移性和稳健性的系统性验证。作者旨在探讨现有指标是否能在多数据集场景下表现一致。

Method: 作者对8种自动打分器（包括词法、嵌入、BERTScore及NLI模型）在3种不同的评估任务（来源相关性、生成答案归因、事实核查蕴含）上进行了审计；使用了包含AttributionBench、HAGRID等多个数据集（共计约3760个样本）进行交叉评估，并与Prompt-based LLM judge进行了对比。

Result: 研究发现：1. 没有任何一个自动指标能在所有数据集上保持稳健（出现了排名倒置且部分模型性能跌至随机水平）；2. 简单的“取平均最优”选择策略在实际应用中表现差；3. LLM judge虽然避免了自动指标在长文档任务上的随机坍塌，但并非在所有场景下最优，且计算成本极其高昂。

Conclusion: LLM RAG领域的自动归因评估指标并非通用，存在严重的跨数据集不稳定性。单一的“全局最优”指标并不存在，评估指标的选择必须在目标数据集上进行验证，不能盲目依赖跨数据集的平均表现。LLM-as-a-judge虽能避免部分崩溃问题，但引入了高成本和非确定性。

Abstract: Practice often treats automatic metrics for attribution in LLM retrieval-augmented generation as interchangeable. We audit eight automatic scorers -- lexical, embedding, and BERTScore baselines alongside entailment/grounding-trained models (clean and FEVER NLI, the checker MiniCheck) -- across three evaluation constructs (provenance/topicality, generated-answer attribution, and fact-check entailment), asking whether any scorer transfers: stays within the 95% confidence interval of the best audited scorer on every dataset of a multi-dataset construct. In the construct with the most multi-dataset human-labeled coverage -- generated-answer attribution (AttributionBench's four source datasets, n = 1,610, with independent HAGRID, n = 2,150) -- none does: the per-dataset metric rankings invert (Kendall tau = -0.64, p = 0.031 on AttributedQA vs. LFQA), and an off-the-shelf NLI scorer that is best on short-claim AttributedQA (AUROC 0.90) collapses to AUROC 0.53 (chance) on long-form LFQA, where BERTScore wins (0.91); the flip is not a length or truncation artifact. This instability has a concrete decision cost: a naive "best-on-average" rule for choosing an evaluator fails leave-one-dataset-out (mean held-out regret 0.172 AUROC, worse than fixing one scorer), so metric choice must be validated on the target dataset rather than learned from others. A prompt-based LLM judge avoids the chance-level collapses the automatic scorers suffer (no LFQA collapse) but is not uniformly best, ~100x costlier, and non-deterministic -- relocating, not removing, the validation burden.

</details>


### [10] [When Retrieval Metrics Mislead: Measuring Policy Signal in Long-Horizon Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23937)
*Tianyu Ding,Juan Pablo De la Cruz Weinstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在 tau-bench 任务中，“精确匹配召回率”低估了检索器对决策模型的实际价值；检索到的非完美匹配策略条款同样能有效支撑高质量的下游分类决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的检索式增强决策任务中，常利用“精确匹配召回率（Exact-match retrieval recall）”充当衡量检索内容对下游模型是否有用的代理指标。作者质疑该指标是否能准确反映检索器对实际决策质量的影响。

Method: 使用 Qwen2.5-3B/7B 分类器在 tau-bench 环境下进行预动作政策分类实验。比较了“黄金政策（Gold-policy）”条件与“检索政策（Retrieved-policy）”条件在下游任务中的表现，并评估了结构化状态表示的效果。

Result: 研究发现，尽管检索到的条款在“排第一位”的精确召回率仅为 7%，但在下游分类任务中，使用检索条款的 Macro-F1（0.58）与使用黄金条款（0.60）相比差异极小，远高于基准的无政策/错误政策控制组。

Conclusion: 单纯依靠“精确匹配召回率”作为检索器性能指标在评估下游任务时存在偏差，会低估检索到的上下文对决策模型的价值。未来研究应更侧重于将检索到的政策直接整合进分类回路中进行端到端评估，而非单纯依赖召回指标。

Abstract: Exact-match retrieval recall is often used as a proxy for whether a retriever supplies useful policy context to a downstream decision model. We test this proxy for pre-action policy classification in tau-bench using Qwen2.5-3B/7B classifiers. Under gold-policy conditioning, a compact structured state improves macro-F1 over raw trajectories by 0.13-0.17 after tuning. We then replace the benchmark-designated policy clause with the top-ranked clause retrieved from decision-time context. Although the exact governing clause is retrieved at rank 1 for only 7% of airline states, the primary 3B classifier obtains macro-F1 0.58 with retrieved clauses versus 0.60 with gold clauses (Delta=-0.02, task-cluster 95% CI [-0.23,+0.21]); mismatched-policy and no-policy controls score 0.32 and 0.21. We do not detect a macro-F1 difference between retrieved and gold clauses in this configuration, although the interval remains too wide to establish non-inferiority. The same qualitative pattern appears with a second retriever and at 7B, while varying across fine-tuning configurations. These results indicate that exact-match clause recall can underestimate downstream policy utility in this benchmark setting, motivating evaluation with retrieved policies in the classification loop rather than recall alone.

</details>


### [11] [Does My Embedding Reflect That $A = B$? Evaluating Mathematical Equivalence in Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23959)
*Jiaying Ye,Samarth Rao,Leo Carlin,Kedar Chintalapati,Saharsh Bhargava,Rachit Jaiswal,Michael Zhou,Jared Darlington,Jarod Alper,Vasily Ilin,Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对当前数学嵌入模型难以识别不同语言下数学等价性的问题，作者提出了MELD数据集并引入对比学习框架，有效提升了跨数学表述的语义对齐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学语言的高度抽象性导致同一逻辑在不同领域有多种表达方式；由于缺乏有效跨语言检索工具，且当前嵌入模型难以通过词汇差异识别数学等价性，亟需一种能精准匹配数学含义的新型嵌入模型。

Method: 1. 构建了MELD (Mathematically Equivalent but Lexically Different) 数据集，用于评估模型捕捉数学等价性的能力；2. 提出了一种对比学习方法，专注于将非形式化数学表述与不同的形式化语言（如Lean）进行对齐。

Result: 研究发现最先进的嵌入模型过度依赖术语表征。提出的对比学习方法显著提升了非形式化到形式化数学语言的检索性能，并在MELD数据集（纯自然语言）上也取得了更好的一致性表现。

Conclusion: 现有的嵌入模型往往基于术语而非深层数学逻辑来对齐语句。通过对比学习将非形式化数学语言与形式化表示对齐，是提升模型捕捉数学等价性能力的有效路径。

Abstract: Because mathematics is highly abstract, a single statement can take very different forms depending on what subfield it is framed in. There are many examples where breakthroughs occurred after researchers discovered that a question had already been answered in a different field. At the same time, the growth of new resources related to formalization has increased the need for tools that enable efficient and reliable navigation between mathematical 'languages' (e.g., from Lean to natural language). In this paper, we investigate whether current embedding models capture mathematical equivalence. To do this, we introduce the Mathematically Equivalent but Lexically Different Pairs (MELD) Dataset, a collection of mathematically equivalent statements that are expressed in very different language. We show that current state-of-the-art embedding models tend to group statements by the terminology used to make them instead of the underlying math. Motivated by this, we propose a contrastive approach to learning embeddings of mathematical text that focuses on aligning informal statements with different formalizations. Our experiments demonstrate that this leads to improvements not only on informal-formal retrieval tasks but also on MELD, which only contains natural language statements.

</details>


### [12] [RASC+: Retrieval-Constrained LLM Adjudication for Clinical Value Set Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23992)
*Sumit Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对临床值集构建任务，提出了一种“高召回检索+大模型判别”的分阶段方法，有效解决了零样本LLM在处理大规模临床代码时准确度低的问题，并保证了输出的可审核性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床值集（Clinical value sets）的构建至关重要，但现有的零样本（zero-shot）大语言模型方法在处理大规模、版本受控且记忆不可靠的临床代码系统时表现不佳，难以达到临床需求。

Method: 提出了一种分阶段的解决方案：第一阶段通过基于Qwen3的检索模型（结合词汇感知扩展和代码显示救援检索）构建高召回率的候选池；第二阶段利用受限的大语言模型（如GPT-5）作为判别器，从这些候选池中进行精准选择。

Result: 模型在RASC测试集上的候选池召回率从0.553提升至0.730；通过GPT-5进行分阶段判别，最终宏F1分数（Macro F1）从基准的0.287提升至0.549。

Conclusion: 检索受限的大语言模型判别方法能够显著提升临床值集的完成质量，同时确保所有生成的代码都来自于可审计的候选池，满足安全性要求。

Abstract: Clinical value sets define the standardized terminology codes used in quality measurement, phenotyping, cohort construction, and clinical decision support. The recently introduced Retrieval-Augmented Set Completion (RASC) benchmark showed that direct zero-shot large language model (LLM) generation is poorly suited to this task: clinical code systems are large, version-controlled, and not reliably memorized by language models. We study a stage-wise alternative in which candidate-pool construction is optimized for recall and a constrained LLM adjudicator is optimized for candidate selection. On the full 3,744-value-set RASC test split, Qwen3-based retrieval with vocabulary-aware expansion and code-display rescue retrieval increases candidate-pool recall from the original RASC retrieval baseline of 0.553 to 0.730; on the held-out-publisher stratum, pool recall is 0.655. The higher-recall pool alone is not sufficient: applying the original SAPBert cross-encoder to this expanded pool gives full-test macro F1 of 0.287 and held-out-publisher macro F1 of 0.233. Replacing the stage-2 selector with blinded GPT-5 adjudication over the same pool increases full-test macro F1 to 0.549 and held-out-publisher macro F1 to 0.533. These results show that retrieval-constrained LLM adjudication can substantially improve value set completion while preserving the safety constraint that all returned codes must come from an auditable candidate pool.

</details>


### [13] [CAVEWOMAN: How Large Language Models Behave Under Linguistic Input and Output Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24083)
*Morayo Danielle Adeyemi,Ryan A. Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明输出压缩能显著降低推理成本，但输入压缩会因为模型补偿性输出导致成本上升和性能下降，且所有形式的压缩都会改变模型的输出行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究“原始人模式”（简短、无语法的表达）对大语言模型推理成本的影响，质疑了盲目压缩（包括输入和输出）是否真的能实现成本节约。

Method: 提出了“Cavewoman”双通道评估协议，用于在任务准确度、实际单位成本和参考文本一致性三个维度上评估模型性能。研究覆盖了8种模型、5个数据集，并设置了5种压缩程度。

Result: 输出压缩可降低API和开源模型1.4-2.4倍（最高3倍）的成本；输入压缩导致成本平均增加约1.15倍（最高2.7倍），且造成准确率下降及输出文本与基线文本的语义发散。

Conclusion: 采用“原始人模式”（压缩用语）进行输出压缩是降低推理成本的有效手段；然而，对输入提示词进行压缩反而会因模型补偿性地增加输出长度并导致准确率下降，从而适得其反，增加整体成本。此外，压缩会导致模型生成的文本与未受限的基线文本在语义上产生显著发散。

Abstract: "Talk short. Drop grammar. Save token." This caveman style is widely promoted as a way to cut inference cost, but whether it actually saves anything depends on which channel (the user's prompt or the model's response) is being compressed. We present Cavewoman, a two-channel evaluation protocol that scores every generation on task accuracy, realized per-item cost, and reference-text agreement against the model's unconstrained reference. We evaluate eight models on five datasets at five reduction levels, with both channels measured on the same items. Output compression cuts realized cost on most API models (1.4-2.4x per model, up to 3x in the best case) and on all four open-weight models under public-tier pricing. Input compression has the opposite effect, a strict lose-lose: it raises net cost rather than lowering it (~1.15x on the five-benchmark mean, up to 1.8x on the worst dataset and 2.7x under stronger compression), because models compensate with longer responses even as accuracy collapses. Under the same setting, surface text diverges from the unconstrained reference: on the non-reasoning models, roughly half of all generations are correct yet their surface text no longer entails the model's own unconstrained baseline generation. The divergence survives length-controlled re-scoring, multiple-comparisons correction, and replication under complementary semantic measures. Code and data are available at https://github.com/danielle34/cavewoman.

</details>


### [14] [PORTER: Language-Grounded Event Representations for Portable Structured EHR Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24102)
*Lin Lawrence Guo,Adam Paul Yan,Emily Vettese,Lillian Sung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PORTER是一种新型电子病历（EHR）基础模型，它摒弃了传统的固定词汇表模式，通过基于文本描述的编码和专门的数值处理路径，实现了卓越的跨机构迁移能力和高计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的EHR基础模型依赖固定词汇表编码离散事件，导致无法表示未见概念或新的属性组合（如数值），严重限制了模型在不同机构或不同数据集间的迁移能力。

Method: 提出PORTER模型，包括三个核心部分：(1) 使用冻结的文本编码器处理临床事件描述，摆脱固定词汇表限制；(2) 设计专用的数值处理路径（numeric pathway）以整合数值信息；(3) 使用自回归预训练的时间序列主干（temporal backbone）学习临床动态。

Result: 在74项临床预测任务中：(1) 准确率与固定词汇模型持平；(2) 在未见过的描述上实现了极强的 zero-shot 迁移，恢复了97.1%的性能；(3) 在MIMIC数据集的跨机构迁移表现优异，避免了严重的数据丢失；(4) 计算效率极高，比任务特定文本序列化模型快329倍。

Conclusion: PORTER实现了词汇表独立（Vocabulary-independent）的EHR基础模型，通过解耦事件表示与固定词汇表，在保持领域内性能的同时，有效解决了跨机构和跨部署管道的泛化难题，且具有极高的计算效率。

Abstract: Most electronic health record (EHR) foundation models encode clinical events as discrete event tokens from a fixed vocabulary and therefore cannot directly represent events containing unseen concepts or new combinations of concepts and attributes such as numeric values. This limits transfer across institutions and even across deployment pipelines within the same institution. We introduce PORTER, a language-grounded structured EHR foundation model that decouples event representation from this fixed vocabulary. PORTER represents events through their descriptions using a frozen text encoder, integrates numeric values through a dedicated pathway, and learns clinical dynamics over patient timelines with an autoregressively pretrained temporal backbone. Across 74 clinical prediction tasks at a pediatric hospital, PORTER matched the mean AUROC of a fixed-vocabulary model with the same temporal backbone and pretraining objective. When the same patient timelines were rendered using event descriptions not seen during pretraining, PORTER transferred without retraining or vocabulary mapping, recovering 97.1% of the mean AUROC of a model trained directly on the target vocabulary. When transferred to MIMIC, PORTER outperformed the fixed-vocabulary model, which dropped 69% of events because their tokens were unseen. Mechanistic analyses showed cross-vocabulary transfer tracked preservation of patient-level representation geometry rather than the scale of the text encoder, and the numeric pathway improved sensitivity to magnitude without disrupting clinical concept identity. PORTER also achieved higher AUROC than a task-specific text serialization comparator, at 329-fold lower amortized compute. PORTER is a step toward vocabulary-independent EHR foundation models that reduce the need for vocabulary harmonization while preserving in-domain performance and enabling efficient cross-task reuse.

</details>


### [15] [BehaviorBench: Benchmarking Foundation Models for Behavioral Science Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24162)
*Jin Huang,Yutong Xie,Wanli Song,Xingjian Zhang,Walter Yuan,Matthew O. Jackson,Qiaozhu Mei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文引入了BehaviorBench基准以全面评估基础模型在行为科学中的表现，并提出了Be.FM-1.5模型。研究揭示了通用模型与行为专门模型在个体预测与群体分布对齐能力上的平衡点，强调了评估模型分布一致性的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基础模型在行为科学领域应用广泛，但目前缺乏对其跨任务、跨情境及跨群体性能的系统性评估，且现有模型在群体分布对齐方面表现不足。

Method: 提出了BehaviorBench基准，涵盖四个核心能力（行为预测/模拟、战略决策、受试者特征推理、行为知识应用）；引入了Be.FM-1.5模型，并在行为数据上进行微调；同时从个体和群体（分布）两个层面进行评估。

Result: 研究发现，通用闭源模型在个体级预测和知识密集型任务上表现优异，而专门微调的Be.FM-1.5模型在群体分布一致性上表现出显著优势，并证明了适当的行为适配可以缩小两者差距。

Conclusion: 行为科学领域的基准评估至关重要；通过在行为数据上进行微调，可以有效弥合通用模型与行为科学需求之间的差距，特别是在提升群体分布一致性方面。BehaviorBench为未来行为对齐AI系统的开发提供了评估标杆。

Abstract: Foundation models have been increasingly applied to behavioral science domains such as psychology, sociology, and economics. While these models show promise in individual tasks such as survey response prediction and human-subject experiment simulation, there remains no systematic understanding of how well they perform across diverse behavioral science tasks, contexts, and populations. We introduce BehaviorBench, a comprehensive benchmark that evaluates foundation models along four core capabilities: (1) behavior prediction and simulation, (2) strategic decision-making, (3) subject-trait inference, and (4) behavioral knowledge application. Crucially, BehaviorBench evaluates model outputs at both the individual and distributional levels, capturing not only per-subject accuracy but also population-level alignment, an essential requirement for behavioral validity. Leveraging the tasks in BehaviorBench, we further develop Be.FM-1.5, extending the Be.FM family of behavioral foundation models fine-tuned on behavioral data. Our results reveal a considerable gap: proprietary general-purpose models excel at individual-level prediction and knowledge-intensive tasks, whereas behavioral foundation models, fine-tuned on behavioral data, achieve substantially stronger distributional alignment. Notably, Be.FM-1.5 leads on distributional metrics and remains competitive on individual-level metrics, suggesting that proper behavioral adaptation can close the gap. Our results highlight the importance of distributional evaluation, establish BehaviorBench as a foundation for developing and assessing behaviorally aligned AI systems, and demonstrate Be.FM-1.5's potential for a broad range of behavioral science studies. Our BehaviorBench and Be.FM-1.5 models can be accessed via https://umich-foreseer.github.io/behaviorbench/.

</details>


### [16] [A Pāninian Foundation for Indic Language Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24172)
*Ritwik Banerjee,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对印度语言NLP支离破碎的现状，该研究提出利用帕尼尼（Pāṇini）语法作为通用架构，通过建立统一的基准测试来整合资源、提升模型效率，并探讨神经模型是否能自动习得该语法体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前印度语言的自然语言处理（NLP）基础设施过于碎片化，各语言独立构建工具和数据集，忽视了印度语言在两千多年间受梵文（Sanskrit）影响而形成并由帕尼尼语法所统一的深层结构规律。

Method: 提出了一套基于帕尼尼语法架构的四部分基准测试套件（benchmark suite），旨在将印度语言共有的词法句法结构显性化，并将其作为评估模型准确性、数据效率和跨语言迁移能力的依据。

Result: 研究论证了建立在帕尼尼框架上的基准测试能将零散的印度语言资源转化为一个统一的高资源元语言基础，并提出了利用该框架检验神经网络是否能自发学习到帕尼尼语言范畴的科学问题。

Conclusion: 基于帕尼尼（Pāṇini）语法的框架为印度语言提供了一个统一的计算体系，不仅有望通过整合稀疏资源提升模型性能，还为探索神经网络的内部可解释性提供了一个重要窗口。

Abstract: More than a billion people communicate in Indic languages, yet the natural language processing infrastructure serving them remains fragmented and underdeveloped. The cause is structural: the field organizes its tools and benchmarks around individual languages or small subsets of genealogical language families, building separate analyzers, parsers, and datasets for each language and starting over for the next. This overlooks a deep regularity. Through more than two millennia of convergence around Sanskrit, Indic languages came to share a morphosyntactic architecture formalized in Pānini's grammar, the Astādhyāyī. This cuts across genealogical lines, uniting languages through a common framework. We argue that this Pāninian framework supplies a unifying computational architecture the field has lacked, and that benchmarks grounded explicitly in it would make Indic language systems more accurate, more data-efficient, and more transferable, effectively merging many apparently disparate and sparse Indic language resources into a single high-resource metalanguage bedrock. We propose a four-part benchmark suite to render this shared architecture explicit, measurable, and ready to be leveraged for practical applications. Moreover, we underscore the question it raises for interpretability research: whether neural models trained on these languages come to represent Pānini's categories on their own.

</details>


### [17] [Harmonic: Hierarchical State Space Models for Efficient Long-Context Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24650)
*Petr Nyoma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Harmonic：一种具有 O(L) 复杂度的分层状态空间模型，通过逐级传递预测误差，在超长序列建模中显著超越 Transformer 和 Mamba，且无 RoPE 位置编码长度限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Transformer 模型在处理超长序列时面临 O(L^2) 复杂度带来的内存瓶颈，且标准的旋转位置编码（RoPE）会限制模型对超出训练预设长度的序列的处理能力。

Method: 提出了一种分层状态空间模型（SSM），由三个以不同时间尺度运行的循环层堆叠而成。该架构不直接传递原始隐藏状态，而是传递来自下层的预测误差，以提取不同时间跨度的特征。

Result: 在 enwiki8 和 WikiText-103 上，Harmonic 在 1K 到 32K token 长度范围内均优于 Transformer 和 Mamba；在 64K 长度下展现出卓越的内存效率；在 1B 参数规模下，成功消除了 RoPE 的长度限制，保证了长序列推理的稳定性。

Conclusion: Harmonic 架构通过层次化状态空间模型有效解决了长序列建模中的存储和定位编码限制，在保持线性复杂度的同时，大幅超越了 Transformer 和 Mamba 在长上下文中的表现。

Abstract: We present Harmonic, a hierarchical state space model (SSM) for language modeling. The architecture stacks three recurrent levels at progressively slower timescales; each level receives the prediction error of the level below as input, rather than its raw hidden state. On enwiki8 with equal token budgets, Harmonic outperforms a comparable Transformer (28M params) by +1.4% at 1K tokens, +6.7% at 8K tokens, and +11.4% at 32K tokens (bpt, lower is better). It also outperforms Mamba at every tested length by 0.7--1.8%. At 64K tokens, both Mamba and Transformer run out of memory on an 80GB H100; Harmonic trains successfully, reaching 6.169 bpt. Results replicate on WikiText-103 (H-TF gap +1.7% to +7.2% across 1K--32K). At 1B parameter scale, replacing all attention layers in TinyLlama 1.1B with HarmonicBlock eliminates the RoPE positional encoding limit: the resulting Hallamonic model maintains stable loss across sequence lengths 1K--8K on two independent clean benchmarks (Lambada and fineweb-edu held-out), while TinyLlama degrades catastrophically past its 2K-token RoPE limit (gap: +9.4 bpt at seq=8K on Lambada). Compute is O(L) per forward pass vs. O(L^2) for attention.
  Logs: https://github.com/Omibranch/harmonic-logs.

</details>


### [18] [AI-PAVE-Br: Leveraging Large Language Models for Enhanced Product Attribute Value Extraction through a Golden Set Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24655)
*Murilo Gazzola,Hugo Gobato Souto,Samuel Silva,Júlia Schubert Peixoto,Felipe Siqueira,André Luis Pedroso de Morais,Caio Gomes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对巴西电商环境下的产品属性提取难题，本文提出了基于 LLM 的 AI-PAVEBr 系统及新的标注数据集 Golden Set，在性能上大幅超越了传统方法，填补了该领域的研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对巴西电子商务市场产品数据复杂、增长迅速的现状，现有传统命名实体识别（NER）方法难以精准处理葡萄牙语产品描述中的语言细微差别与多样性，缺乏针对该市场的专业处理方案与公开基准。

Method: 1. 构建名为 AI-PAVEBr 的专用系统，采用大语言模型（LLM）并结合针对性的提示工程（Prompt Engineering）；2. 创建并发布名为 Golden Set 的手动标注数据集，用于巴西葡萄牙语环境下产品属性值的提取（PAVE）任务。

Result: 实验表明，AI-PAVEBr 在提取性能上显著优于传统的 NER 基线方法，为非英语市场提供了一种高效、可扩展的信息提取解决方案。

Conclusion: AI-PAVEBr 证明了利用大语言模型（LLM）进行特定市场（巴西）的产品属性提取具有显著优势，且该研究提供的基准数据集（Golden Set）将有力推动相关领域学术研究的标准化与持续发展。

Abstract: The explosive growth and complexity of product data within the dynamic Brazilian e-commerce landscape demand robust and specialized methods for structured information extraction. Traditional approaches to Product Attribute Value Extraction (PAVE) often struggle with the linguistic nuances and sheer diversity of product descriptions in Portuguese. To address this critical gap, this paper introduces two major contributions. First, we present AI-PAVEBr, a specialized system engineered with Large Language Models (LLMs) to perform high-accuracy PAVE specifically for Brazilian e-commerce catalogs. Second, to facilitate reproducible research and provide a definitive benchmark, we introduce and share the Golden Set, a new, meticulously curated, and manually annotated dataset for PAVE in Portuguese. We detail the creation process and structure (Entity, Category, Subcategories) of this high-quality reference set. Our experiments conclusively show that AI-PAVE-Br, leveraging targeted prompt engineering, dramatically outperforms conventional Named Entity Recognition (NER) baselines. This work not only delivers a superior, scalable solution for a major non-English market but also enriches the NLP community with a valuable, publicly available resource for future PAVE research.

</details>


### [19] [L3Cube-MahaPOS: A Marathi Part-of-Speech Tagging Dataset and BERT Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24825)
*Hariom Ingle,Ronit Ghode,Ishwari Gondkar,Jidnyasa Harad,Raviraj Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对马拉地语资源匮乏的问题，本文发布了 L3Cube-MahaPOS 大规模手工标注数据集，并提供了完整的预处理流程和多种模型的基准评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马拉地语作为拥有 8300 万使用者的语言，目前缺乏标准的 POS 标注语料库和评估基准，且其形态丰富、语序灵活及代码混合现象增加了计算建模的难度。

Method: 构建了包含 32,354 个句子的手工标注数据集，采用 16 标签 Universal Dependencies 对齐方案；建立了包含 Unicode 归一化、Tokenization 及噪声过滤的预处理流水线；并在 HMM、CRF、BiLSTM、MuRIL 及 MahaBERT-v2 等六种模型上进行了基准测试。

Result: 在最佳模型下，该数据集在 15 个标注类别上达到了 88.67% 的 Token 级准确率和 81.67% 的宏观 F1 分数。

Conclusion: L3Cube-MahaPOS 填补了马拉地语在 POS 标注领域的空白，通过提供高质量数据集、标准化流程及模型基准，旨在推动该语言相关的 NLP 研究与应用开发。

Abstract: Part-of-Speech (POS) tagging is a foundational NLP task underpinning machine translation, information extraction, and syntactic parsing. Despite Marathi being spoken by over 83 million people and ranking among the top twenty most spoken languages worldwide, it remains severely under-resourced in annotated corpora and standardised evaluation benchmarks. Marathi presents unique challenges for computational modelling owing to its rich morphology, relatively free word order, lack of capitalisation conventions, and pervasive code-mixing with Hindi and English. We introduce L3Cube-MahaPOS, a gold-standard POS tagging dataset for Marathi comprising 32,354 manually annotated sentences drawn from news text. Annotation was performed entirely manually by a team of Marathi-proficient annotators following a 16-tag Universal Dependencies-aligned scheme. A structured preprocessing pipeline covering Unicode normalisation, Devanagari-aware tokenisation, and noise filtering ensures label consistency across all splits. We benchmark the dataset across six model families spanning HMM, CRF, BiLSTM, BiLSTM+CharCNN, MuRIL, and the Marathi-specific transformer MahaBERT-v2. The best system achieves 88.67\% token-level accuracy and a macro-F1 of 81.67% over 15 evaluated tag classes. We release the dataset, annotation guidelines, and trained model checkpoints to foster further research in Marathi NLP.

</details>
