### [20] [Above the Inner Loop: Exceeding Accelerate at LLM Prefill GEMM on the M1 AMX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25426)
*Deyvik Bhan*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 研究发现 Apple Silicon 的 AMX 算力瓶颈在于调度而非内核循环。通过优化多线程面板分配与权重预打包，作者实现了优于官方库的各种 fp32 GEMM 运算，并在 LLM 预填充场景中获得了 1.44 倍的端到端吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Apple Silicon (M1-M3) 上的 LLM 预填充阶段高度依赖 GEMM 计算，作者旨在探究为什么手工编写的 kernel 比官方 Accelerate 库性能更高，并试图挖掘 AMX 的性能优化空间。

Method: 1. 通过微基准测试（microbenchmarks）揭示 AMX 内部循环逻辑；2. 针对 M1 的两个 AMX 模块，采用精细的多线程面板拆分策略；3. 实施常量权重预打包；4. 在 llama.cpp 中对全链路预填充（prefill）阶段进行性能验证。

Result: 研制的 direct-AMX kernel 在所有 12 个 LLM prefill GEMM 形状上均超越了 Apple Accelerate 的三种方案（cblas_sgemm, BNNSMatMul, BNNS Graph），且保证了比特完全一致（bit-exact）。在 llama.cpp 中，将端到端的预填充吞吐量从 291 提升至 420 tokens/s（约 1.44 倍）。

Conclusion: M1 AMX 的高性能瓶颈不在于内部循环（inner loop），而在于调度策略。通过精细的面板调度（fine-panel scheduling）和权重预打包（weight pre-packing），可以显著超越 Apple Accelerate 对 AMX 的调用性能，实现更优的吞吐量与比特精确度。

Abstract: On Apple Silicon the fp32 GEMMs dominating LLM prefill are dispatched by Accelerate to a matrix coprocessor (AMX) on the M1-M3. We ask where a hand-written kernel's throughput over Accelerate comes from on the M1 AMX, and reach a structural conclusion: not a faster inner loop. By microbenchmark the inner loop is load-issue bound -- once any operand load interleaves with the FMA32 stream, single-thread throughput falls to a 610-to-680 GFLOPS band, under half the load-free rate. The gain comes from two deployment-level levers Accelerate underuses: fine multi-thread panels filling the M1's second on-chip AMX block (winning the K >= N shapes), and pre-packing the constant weight at load (winning the N > K shapes). A bit-exact direct-AMX kernel using both is the fastest bit-exact fp32 GEMM path we find on the M1: it exceeds all three Accelerate fp32 paths (cblas_sgemm, BNNSMatMul, and the BNNS Graph compiler) at all twelve LLM prefill GEMMs at S = 128 (GPT-2 to Llama-7B scale), leading the fastest, BNNS Graph, by 1.17 -- and by 1.09 at the three shapes where it too holds fp32 -- with geometric means of 1.58 over BNNSMatMul and about 2.0x over cblas_sgemm. Every output is bit-identical to Accelerate, whereas BNNS Graph is bit-exact at only three of twelve shapes, the rest at reduced precision (error up to 1.4e-3). Dropped into llama.cpp for its cblas_sgemm prefill matmuls, it raises measured full-forward throughput from 291 to 420 tokens/s (1.44x, bit-identical) at 128-token prefill -- end-to-end, not a GEMM-only ratio. The contribution is this shape-resolved M1-AMX characterization (microbenchmark, two-block aggregate, per-core occupancy probe), leaving fine-panel scheduling and pre-packing as the only two levers above an inner loop at the hardware limit; mis-tuning the single column-panel width costs nearly 2x.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [21] [TokenMinds: Pretrained User Tokens and Embeddings for User Understanding in Large Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25147)
*Qingyun Liu,Bo Yan,Yang Liu,Yuji Roh,Ekansh Sharma,Likang Yin,Emma Olowo,Min-hsuan Tsai,Yuxuan Li,Diego Uribe,Saksham Aggarwal,Siqi Wu,Yuan Hao,Vikas Kedigehalli,Lukasz Heldt,Lichan Hong,Li Wei,Xinyang Yi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TokenMinds 提出了一种基于语义 ID 的双输出（离散 Token + 稠密 Embeddings）用户建模框架，解决了大模型在工业级推荐系统中的表示瓶颈与成本问题，并在 YouTube 实际生产中实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户建模存在局限性：传统定长稠密 Embeddings 存在表示瓶颈；现有的 LLM 生成式文本表示难以捕捉深层行为动态且对齐困难。研究旨在探索如何利用离散语义 ID（SID）对用户进行建模，以克服上述限制并提升复杂工业场景下的泛化能力。

Method: 提出 TokenMinds 系统，采用基于预训练大型语言模型（LLM）的编码器-解码器架构。该系统设计了双输出机制：一方面生成离散的 SID 用户 Token，另一方面保持生成稠密用户 Embeddings 的能力，并结合异步计算基础设施以适应工业级实时推理。

Result: 在 YouTube 数十亿用户规模的实测中，TokenMinds 表现优异。离散用户 Token 与稠密 Embeddings 在排名系统表现出互补价值，且通过统一跨场景建模，大幅降低了模型的训练和部署成本。

Conclusion: TokenMinds 通过引入基于语义 ID（SID）的离散用户表示，证明了其在工业级推荐系统中的实用性。该双输出架构（离散 Token + 稠密 Embeddings）不仅提升了模型性能，还通过统一语义空间实现了跨场景建模，显著优化了计算成本。

Abstract: User modeling in industrial recommender systems typically produces dense embeddings, which suffer from representational constraints inherent to fixed-dimensional vectors. An emerging alternative for discrete user representation -- using LLMs to generate text-based user tokens -- captures topical co-occurrences rather than deep sequential behavior dynamics and produces outputs that are difficult to ground to item attributes. Meanwhile, Semantic ID (SID) based item tokenization has proven effective for improving generalization in generative recommendation, yet discrete SID-based representations for users remain largely unexplored. We propose TokenMinds, an industrial-scale system that extends the PLUM framework from item retrieval to user modeling, generating both discrete SID-based user tokens and dense user embeddings via an encoder-decoder architecture adapted from pre-trained LLMs. This dual-output design provides the complementary benefits of discrete, semantically grounded user representations while maintaining compatibility with existing downstream models that rely on dense embeddings. Additionally, the shared SID vocabulary naturally extends to cross-scenario modeling: by unifying long-form and short-form video behaviors into a single model, we substantially reduce training and serving costs. We validate TokenMinds through extensive offline experiments and live launches on multiple YouTube surfaces, served on full user traffic (billions of users) via an asynchronous infrastructure that decouples representation generation from downstream scoring. Focusing on ranking as the primary downstream use case, our results confirm the practical viability of SID-based user tokens at industrial scale and demonstrate that tokens and dense embeddings provide complementary value across different production ranking systems.

</details>


### [22] [Extreme Meta-Classification for Large-Scale Zero-Shot Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25237)
*Sachin Yadav,Deepak Saini,Anirudh Buvanesh,Bhawna Paliwal,Kunal Dahiya,Siddarth Asokan,Yashoteja Prabhu,Jian Jiao,Manik Varma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对大规模检索任务中零样本项难以处理的问题，本文提出了EMMETT框架及其实际应用算法IRENE，通过即时合成分类器平衡了检索效率与表达容量，并经实验证明大幅度提升了检索精度及业务指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Siamese双塔模型虽支持新项快速检索但编码器表达能力有限；极端分类方法虽表达能力强但无法应对频繁出现的新项（零样本问题）。需要一种既能保持高效检索又能具备高表示容量的方案。

Method: 1. EMMETT框架：利用已知项的分类器通过合成方式为新项生成即时分类器；2. IRENE算法：EMMETT的高效实现，适用于大规模部署；3. 理论框架：为大规模零样本检索的泛化性能提供了数学分析与指导。

Result: 在多种基准测试中，IRENE的Recall@10提升高达15%；在大型搜索引擎的广告检索实测中，点击率（CTR）提升了4.2%。

Conclusion: 提出了一种名为IRENE的框架，能够通过合成零样本项的分类器，有效解决大规模检索任务中新项快速涌入带来的挑战，并显著提升了检索精度和业务指标。

Abstract: We develop accurate and efficient solutions for large-scale retrieval tasks where novel (zero-shot) items can arrive continuously at a rapid pace. Conventional Siamese-style approaches embed both queries and items through a small encoder and retrieve the items lying closest to the query. While this approach allows efficient addition and retrieval of novel items, the small encoder lacks sufficient capacity for the necessary world knowledge in complex retrieval tasks. The extreme classification approaches have addressed this by learning a separate classifier for each item observed in the training set which significantly increases the representation capacity of the model. Such classifiers outperform Siamese approaches on observed items, but cannot be trained for novel items due to data and latency constraints. To bridge these gaps, this paper develops: (1) A new algorithmic framework, EMMETT, which efficiently synthesizes classifiers on-the-fly for novel items, by relying on the readily available classifiers for observed items; (2) A new algorithm, IRENE, which is a simple and effective instance of EMMETT that is specifically suited for large-scale deployments, and (3) A new theoretical framework for analyzing the generalization performance in large-scale zero-shot retrieval which guides our algorithm and training related design decisions.
  Comprehensive experiments are conducted on a wide range of retrieval tasks which demonstrate that IRENE improves the zero-shot retrieval accuracy by up to 15% points in Recall@10 when added on top of leading encoders. Additionally, on an online A/B test in a large-scale ad retrieval task in a major search engine, IRENE improved the ad click-through rate by 4.2%. Lastly, we validate our design choices through extensive ablative experiments. The source code for IRENE is available at https://aka.ms/irene.

</details>


### [23] [Adaptive Re-Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25249)
*Ata Cinar Genc,Emir Kaan Korukluoglu,James Allan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种“自适应重排序”框架，通过自动评估查询难度并动态选择不同代价的检索处理策略，在显著降低系统延迟和计算成本的同时，保持了与重型单一模型相近的检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的“检索后重排序”管线对所有查询一律使用昂贵的重排序模型，导致简单查询产生不必要的延迟和计算资源浪费。

Method: 提出了一种基于效用的标签框架，通过训练一个路由分类器，根据查询复杂度动态选择三种处理策略之一：稀疏检索（BM25）、轻量级重排序（MiniLM-L6-v2）或重型神经网络重排序（BGE-v2-m3）。

Result: 在多个数据集上，与单一重排序模型（BGE）相比，该方法实现了1.15-53倍的延迟降低，同时保持了具有竞争力的检索效果（nDCG@10指标波动在-17.5%至+4.0%之间）。

Conclusion: 基于效用函数的自适应重排序（Adaptive Re-Ranking）是一种有效的降低信息检索系统计算成本和延迟的方案，表现出极高的可扩展性。

Abstract: Modern Information Retrieval (IR) systems typically use a "retrieve-then-rerank" pipeline, where a computationally expensive, pre-determined cross-encoder re-ranks the top results from a fast initial retriever. While effective, this approach often applies heavy re-ranking models regardless of query complexity, resulting in high latency and wasted computational resources on simple queries. We propose Adaptive Re-Ranking, an utility-based labeling framework for cost-aware routing and present empirical evidence (via oracle analysis and a trained baseline router) that per-query routing offers large potential gains but is non-trivial to learn from limited supervision. We train a routing classifier with 3 strategies: sparse retrieval (BM25), dense re-ranking (MiniLM-L6-v2), and heavy neural re-ranking (BGE-v2-m3). Compared to BGE our method achieves 1.15-53x lower median latency and 1.11-5.22x lower mean latency across all datasets we have tested, while delivering -17.5% to +4.0% nDCG@10, which is competitive in some datasets. Our findings show that routing queries based on our novel utility function offers a scalable solution for reducing computational costs and latency in a variety of IR systems.

</details>


### [24] [TheoremGraph: Bridging Formal and Informal Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25363)
*Simon Kurgan,Evan Wang,Eric Leonen,Sophie Szeto,Luke Alexander,Artemii Remizov,Jarod Alper,Giovanni Inchiostro,Vasily Ilin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TheoremGraph 是一个连接非正式论文数学与正式化定理证明（Lean）的统一依赖图，旨在通过大语言模型和图结构支持更强大的数学知识检索、归因及检索增强推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学知识的结构（陈述及其依赖）在非正式论文（宏观引用）和正式数学库（细粒度依赖）之间存在割裂，缺乏统一的依赖图结构来支撑高效的数学搜索和推理。

Method: 1. 非正式数学：解析 arXiv 上 1170 万个定理环境，提取 1830 万个依赖关系。 
2. 正式数学：开发 LeanGraph，从 25 个 Lean 4 项目中提取 38.8 万个节点和 1130 万条类型化边。 
3. 跨域对齐：利用 LLM 将自然语言描述嵌入共享语义空间，进行跨文档和非正式-正式数学的匹配。

Result: 成功构建了 TheoremGraph；确认了 47,952 个非正式与正式陈述的匹配对；在概念检索任务中，提出的表示方法在没有 LLM 重排序的情况下达到了与顶级工具（LeanSearch v2）相当的性能（Recall@10 为 0.775）。

Conclusion: TheoremGraph 通过统一非正式与正式数学的依赖关系，为数学搜索、归因和检索增强推理（RAG）提供了强有力的基础设施。它证明了跨文档和跨模态实现细粒度数学知识链接的可行性。

Abstract: Mathematical knowledge is organized around statements and their dependencies, but this structure is exposed unevenly: informal papers cite mostly at the document level, while formal libraries record fine-grained dependencies over a much smaller body of mathematics. We introduce TheoremGraph, a unified statement-level dependency graph spanning both informal and formal mathematics. On the informal side, we parse 11.7M theorem-like environments from mathematics arXiv and recover 18.3M candidate directed dependencies, each labeled by the extractor that proposed it so downstream users can trade coverage for precision. On the formal side, we release LeanGraph, a Lean 4 elaborator-level extractor producing 388,105 declaration nodes and 11.3M typed edges across 25 Lean projects. We bridge the two graphs by embedding generated natural-language slogans into a shared semantic space, linking related statements across papers and across the informal/formal divide; an LLM judge affirms 47,952 such matches above a 0.8 cosine floor, with the judge-acceptance rate rising from 48% across the floor to 87% in the >=0.9 tier. On formal concept retrieval, our name-and-signature representation with graph expansion comes within 0.5pp of LeanSearch v2's reranked Recall@10 (0.775 vs. 0.780) without an LM reranker. We release the dataset, extractors, HTTP API, and MCP interface as infrastructure for mathematical search, attribution, and retrieval-augmented reasoning, available at theoremsearch.com and huggingface.co/datasets/uw-math-ai/theorem-matching.

</details>


### [25] [S2-CAR: Segmentation-Supervised Complexity-Adaptive Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25415)
*Linjiang Guo,Nitin Bisht,Shiqing Wu,Xianzhi Wang,Guandong Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对序列推荐中意图异质性难以捕捉的问题，本文提出S2-CAR框架，利用基于隐状态能量的动态分割和自适应多意图聚合，显著提升了推荐准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理复杂异质用户行为序列时，往往采用统一建模或不准确的硬分窗，导致意图干扰及对短期信号的过度依赖，难以捕捉真实的意图边界和变化。

Method: 1. 提出Context-Aware Soft Temporal Point Process (Soft-TPP)：基于隐状态能量衰减的动态分割机制，摆脱固定时间窗口的硬性约束；2. 提出Segment-Count-Adaptive Multi-Intent Extraction模块：对分割后的片段进行分层聚合，提取紧凑的多意图表示。

Result: 在电影、电商和游戏领域的3个基准数据集上，S2-CAR在所有指标上均优于13种先进基线；同时，所提出的基于能量的分割模块可作为即插即用的组件提升现有模型性能。

Conclusion: S2-CAR通过基于能量的连续意图分割和层次化多意图聚合，有效地解决了序列推荐中意图异质性和序列复杂性带来的挑战。该框架具有良好的通用性和可插拔性。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation aims to predict user preferences from interaction histories, yet existing models often struggle when behavior patterns become complex and heterogeneous. A key reason is that interaction histories are rarely uniform: users' interests shift in a latent way over time, yet existing models either treat the full sequence as a homogeneous context or rely on rigid time-window segmentation that misaligns with true intent boundaries. This mis-segmentation not only introduces cross-intent interference at intermediate sequence positions but also leads to over-reliance on short-term interest signals. To address this, we propose S2-CAR, a segmentation-supervised and complexity-adaptive framework for sequential recommendation that models user intent as a continuous latent energy state. Specifically, it uses the Context-Aware Soft Temporal Point Process (Soft-TPP) to segment boundaries triggered by the natural decay of latent-state energy rather than fixed intervals, enabling intent segmentation without fixed time-gap rules. Next, upon this segmentation, a Segment-Count-Adaptive Multi-Intent Extraction module hierarchically aggregates intent-coherent segments into a compact set of multi-interest representations. Extensive experiments on 3 representative public benchmark datasets spanning movie, e-commerce, and gaming domains across 13 baselines demonstrate that S2-CAR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across all datasets and metrics. Further analysis shows that the proposed energy-based segmentation serves as a plug-and-play module, yielding consistent improvements when integrated into existing sequential recommendation backbones.

</details>


### [26] [Recommendation as Generation: Unifying Personalized Video Generation and Recommendation at Industrial Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25496)
*Yanhua Cheng,Bo Wang,Haotian Zhang,Xinyuan Gao,Zhihui Yin,Ben Xue,Yongzhi Li,Jieting Xue,Ye Ma,Minquan Wang,Jiahui Li,Tianyu Xu,Zhiqiang Liu,Xiao Lin,Shiyang Wen,Changcheng Li,Liu Liu,Quan Chen,Peng Jiang,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该文提出了一种“推荐即生成”（RaG）的新范式，通过语义ID解耦和视频生成代理，实现用户兴趣的精准建模与视频按需生成，显著提升了工业级推荐系统的商业效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统短视频推荐受限于预制视频库，难以捕捉用户细粒度与动态兴趣；旨在打破现有推荐系统的匹配范式，通过按需生成个性化视频来满足用户需求。

Method: 提出Recommendation-as-Generation (RaG) 范式，通过共享语义ID (SIDs) 解耦视频内容与风格；开发视频生成代理 (VGAs) 实现分层规划与创作；引入跨域奖励学习机制，联合优化兴趣对齐、用户反馈与视频质量。

Result: 在拥有4亿日活的工业级平台上通过A/B测试验证，RaG在广告场景下比现有的强生成式推荐模型（GRM）基线实现了高达1.87%的广告收入增长。

Conclusion: RaG框架通过将个性化视频生成与推荐系统深度融合，构建了一个闭环生成系统。实验证明，该范式不仅能有效捕捉用户细粒度兴趣，还能通过按需生成视频显著提升商业指标，为推荐系统从“匹配”转向“创造”提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Traditional short-video recommendation systems match user interest to a fixed pool of pre-produced videos, which limits their ability to capture fine-grained and dynamic preferences. We propose Recommendation-as-Generation (RaG), a new paradigm that generates personalized videos on demand from inferred user interest. Our framework unifies generative recommendation and video generation through shared semantic IDs (SIDs), which disentangle video representation into content semantics and creative style semantics, enabling both fine-grained modeling of user interest and controllable generation of interest-aligned videos. We further develop Video Generation Agents (VGAs) that are conditioned on inferred SIDs to drive hierarchical planning and refinement for video creation, including visual composition, audio alignment, and artistic effect enhancement. To optimize the framework, we effectively introduce a synergistic cross-domain reward learning mechanism that jointly enforces interest alignment, user feedback, and video quality assessment.
  We deploy RaG on an industrial-scale platform with over 400 million daily active users and evaluate it in a revenue-critical advertising scenario. Online A/B tests show up to 1.87% ad revenue improvement compared to a strong production GRM baseline, demonstrating its effectiveness in driving further revenue gains beyond generative recommendation. Our results highlight a closed-loop generative system as a promising paradigm for integrating personalized video generation into recommendation.

</details>


### [27] [A Stochastic Epidemiological Model of Latent Tuberculosis in a Radiation Exposed Mars Colony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25728)
*Teddy Lazebnik*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究建立了一个模拟框架，量化了辐射驱动的潜伏结核病在火星殖民地爆发的风险，并提出了利用人工智能进行动态防控的优化方案，为火星任务的医疗健康保障提供了理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着火星殖民计划向工程实践推进，火星环境特有的辐射、免疫改变及封闭空间限制，使得对潜在传染病（如潜伏性结核病）传播风险的评估与管理变得至关重要。

Method: 开发了一个随机宿主-辐射-病原体-栖息地模型，模拟潜伏性结核病的再激活过程；将辐射对免疫力的影响、免疫力对潜伏结核再激活的控制、以及封闭环境下的空气传播机制整合在一起；通过强化学习（近端策略优化，PPO）构建了部分可观测的序贯决策模型，以优化隔离和药物治疗策略。

Result: 模拟显示，即便初始无感染病例，火星殖民地仍可能因环境因素导致结核病内源性爆发；风险受潜伏库大小、辐射-免疫耦合强度及再激活敏感度的显著影响；基于PPO的自适应控制策略在降低感染率和死亡率的同时，避免了不必要的过度干预。

Conclusion: 本研究建立的框架能够对火星任务中的结核病防控策略（筛查、监测、屏蔽和治疗）进行任务特定的“压力测试”，并证明自适应控制策略在减轻感染负担与死亡率方面的优势。

Abstract: Plans to establish a sustained human presence on Mars have moved from speculative ambition toward concrete engineering programmes, making the biological consequences of settlement an increasingly practical question. A Mars colony would place a small, closed population in an environment combining chronic radiation, altered immunity, constrained medical autonomy, and engineered indoor air. Latent infections are especially important because clinically silent carriers may become sources of transmissible disease when host control deteriorates. In this study, we develop a stochastic host-radiation-pathogen-habitat model of latent tuberculosis reactivation in a Mars colony. The model links galactic cosmic radiation to immune competence, immune competence to latent-tuberculosis reactivation, and reactivation to airborne transmission in a closed habitat. We also formulate countermeasure allocation as a partially observable sequential decision problem in which isolation and medication are selected by fixed baselines or by a proximal policy optimization policy trained on an agent-based simulator. Our simulations show that active tuberculosis can emerge endogenously despite no initial infectious cases, and that risk is most sensitive to latent reservoir size, radiation-immune coupling and reactivation sensitivity. Adaptive control reduced infectious burden and mortality while limiting unnecessary intervention. This framework supports mission-specific stress testing of screening, monitoring, shielding and treatment strategies before launch.

</details>


### [28] [How Large Language Models Source Brand Reputation Across Languages and Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25787)
*Dmitrij Zatuchin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究分析了 AI 在回答品牌问题时的引用来源，发现其极度依赖第三方权威中介（如维基百科），表现出明显的长尾分布特性，且在不同市场呈现出特定的来源偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对 AI 品牌可见性的研究多关注回答文本本身，本研究旨在深入溯源，探究 AI 在回答品牌相关问题时的信息来源（引用端）构成及其地理/语言分布规律。

Method: 整合 Rankfor.AI 的三个数据集，覆盖 128 个品牌、12 个母市场、13 种语言，分析了 167,551 条基于 URL 的引用数据。采用分类法对来源域和类型进行定性与定量统计。

Result: 1. 第三方来源占比极高 (85.7%)，自有媒体占比仅 14.3%；2. 来源分布遵循 Zipf 定律（长尾效应），头部的 18% 域名贡献了 80% 的引用；3. 维基百科在 12 种语言中的 11 种里均位列首位；4. 存在显著的市场特异性（例如波兰品牌更多引用 YouTube 及招聘门户）。

Conclusion: AI 检索增强生成的品牌来源高度依赖第三方平台（尤其是维基百科），且呈现出长尾效应。这种来源分布不仅受全球通用平台影响，还表现出一定的本土化和行业特性（如特定市场的职业网站）。

Abstract: When a large language model (LLM) answers a question about a company, it grounds the answer in retrieved web sources, and those sources decide what the model says. Most analysis of AI brand visibility looks at the answer text. This study looks one step earlier, at the citations. We merge three Rankfor.AI datasets covering 128 brands across 12 home markets and 13 languages, and analyse 167,551 URL-grounded citations (189,974 total attribution rows). We classify each citation by domain and source type and measure where AI gets its brand information, by language and by market. Four patterns hold. First, AI grounds brand answers overwhelmingly in third-party sources: 85.7% of citations point to sites the brand does not own, against 14.3% owned. Second, the source base is concentrated and long-tailed: 80% of citations come from about 18% of domains, fitting a Zipf law (alpha = 0.86, R^2 = 0.983). Third, one reference site dominates almost everywhere: Wikipedia is the most-cited domain in 11 of 12 languages, the exception being Lithuanian, where the business daily vz.lt edges it (4.38%). Fourth, the source mix is market-specific at the margin: for 46 Polish national brands the most-cited domain is YouTube, and four HR and careers portals supply 637 citations against 297 for Polish Wikipedia, about twice as many.

</details>


### [29] [AutoRelAnnotator: Calibrated Model Cascades for Cost-Efficient Relevance Evaluation in Sponsored Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25871)
*Md Omar Faruk Rokon,Shasvat Desai,Hong Yao,Kuang-chih Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对搜索相关性标注的高昂成本与精度挑战，论文提出了一种结合微调、模型级联与等渗校准的系统性方案，实现了成本与精度的双重优化，并在1.5亿次标注任务中验证了其工业实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决传统手动标注存在的高成本、高延迟问题，以及通用大模型在领域特定搜索相关性任务中精度不足的缺陷，旨在构建一种高效、可扩展且精确的离线标注流程。

Method: 提出了一种“校准模型级联”框架，通过路由机制将查询请求分配给不同规模的微调分类器；利用领域特定微调提升基准精度，利用多级级联降低计算成本，并引入按类等渗校准来微调输出概率。

Result: 在六个实际生产场景中处理了超过1.5亿条标注任务。实验结果表明：微调提升了20个百分点的准确率；级联结构在保持精度的前提下将计算成本降低了一半；按类等渗校准较现有最强Baseline进一步提升了0.6个百分点的准确率。

Conclusion: 本研究证明通过校准模型级联（calibrated model cascade）可以在保持高精度的同时显著降低标注成本。微调侧重提升精度，级联侧重降低开销，而按类等渗校准（per-class isotonic calibration）提供了进一步增量优化，这一方案已在工业级大规模应用中得到验证。

Abstract: How can we generate high-quality relevance annotations at scale without the cost and delays of human labeling? Relevance annotations are the backbone of search ranking systems which is needed for training data preparation, NDCG evaluation, and root cause analysis. However, human annotation is slow and off-the-shelf LLMs suffer from accuracy on domain-specific tasks. We propose a calibrated model cascade, a systematic approach for cost-efficient offline relevance annotation by routing queries through progressively larger fine-tuned classifiers. Our central insight is that accuracy and cost are orthogonal optimizations: domain-specific fine-tuning drives accuracy, cascading drives cost, and per-class isotonic calibration adds a small but reliable gain on top. Our contribution is threefold: (a) we decompose the gains and show that fine-tuning contributes 20 accuracy points while cascading is approximately accuracy-neutral but halves compute cost, (b) we introduce per-class isotonic calibration as one component of the cascade, contributing a small but statistically significant gain (+0.6 points over the strongest calibration baseline), and (c) we validate the system in production across six offline use cases, processing 150M+ annotations and enabling faster experimentation cycles. Our work is a building block for scalable, high-quality offline annotation pipelines in search and advertising systems.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [30] [Minimax PAC Bounds for Learning in Exogenous Contextual MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25170)
*Corentin Pla,Hugo Richard,Marc Abeille,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了存在外生上下文的马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），通过方差缩减算法实现了在某些场景下与上下文空间大小无关的PAC学习，并为完全未知环境下的策略评估提供了最优的样本复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在具有外生i.i.d.上下文的折扣MDP中，如何有效处理上下文对环境（奖励和转移）影响的问题，旨在寻找样本复杂度最优的PAC学习算法，并探索是否能摆脱对大规模上下文空间 $|\mathcal{Z}|$ 的依赖。

Method: 提出了基于方差缩减（variance-reduced）的算法，并分别针对“上下文分布未知”和“上下文分布与转移概率均未知”两种机制进行了样本复杂度的分析与界定。

Result: 在已知回报和转移概率、仅采样上下文分布的条件下，实现了 $\widetilde O(1/((1-\gamma)^3\varepsilon^2))$ 的样本复杂度且与 $|\mathcal{Z}|$ 无关；在完全未知的机制下，推导出了关于 $|\mathcal{X}|$ 和 $|\mathcal{Z}|$ 相关的匹配上下界。

Conclusion: 该研究为带有外生上下文的表格型折扣MDP提供了PAC学习框架，证明了在特定条件下（如评估PE）样本复杂度可以实现与上下文空间大小 $|\mathcal{Z}|$ 无关，并给出了匹配的上下界，确立了理论上的最优性。

Abstract: We study PAC learning in tabular discounted Markov decision processes with exogenous i.i.d. contexts, with discount factor $γ$, finite state space $\mathcal X$, action space $\mathcal A$, and context space $\mathcal Z$. At each time step, a context is drawn independently from an unknown distribution $μ$ and revealed before the agent acts. This context may affect both rewards and transitions, while remaining uncontrolled by the agent. Depending on the regime, the learner has access either to a sampling oracle for $μ$, to a sampling oracle for the transition kernel conditioned on state-context-action tuples, or to both. Oracles can be accessed before and during policy execution. The sample complexity is measured by a couple $(n,m)$, where $n$ is the number of calls to the sampling oracles before execution and $m$ is the number of calls to the sampling oracles during execution. When rewards and transitions are known and only the context distribution $μ$ is sampled, we give a variance-reduced algorithm that solves policy evaluation (PE), best-value estimation (BVE), and best-policy extraction (BPE) with $\left(\widetilde O\left(1/((1-γ)^3\varepsilon^2)\right), 0 \right) $ sample complexity. The rate is independent of $|\mathcal Z|$ and minimax optimal up to logarithmic factors. As a corollary, we also obtain tight rates in the case of one-step perfect look-ahead, improving upon the existing guarantees. In the fully unknown regime, where both $μ$ and P must be learned, we show that PE remains $|\mathcal Z|$-free, with matching upper and lower bounds $\bigl(\widetilde O(|\mathcal X|/((1-γ)^3\varepsilon^2)),\, \widetilde O(1/((1-γ)^2\varepsilon^2))\bigr)$.

</details>


### [31] [Stabilizing black-box algorithms through task-oriented randomization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25269)
*Yali Wang,Zhaojun Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于任务导向的自适应随机化方法，旨在解决黑盒模型在面对复杂、非结构化输入时的稳定性难题，并为Top-k排序任务提供了理论保障与实证分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒模型在现代研究中应用广泛，但其输出稳定性直接关系到人工智能的可信度，尤其是在面对高维多样的输入数据（从高斯分布到未知结构复杂数据）时，如何平衡稳健性与信息利用是一个亟待解决的挑战。

Method: 提出了一种任务导向的随机化（task-oriented randomization）方法，能够根据输入数据的生成机制（特别是针对非结构化复杂性）自适应地调整策略；并建立了相应的稳定性理论保障框架，将其扩展至Top-k排序问题。

Result: 通过详尽的数值模拟和真实数据集应用验证，证明了该方法能够有效提升黑盒模型输出的稳定性，并量化了稳定性与模型探索能力之间的关键权衡。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种针对黑盒模型输出稳定性的任务导向随机化方法，通过理论与实践验证了其在复杂数据输入下的有效性，并揭示了稳定性与探索性之间的折衷关系。

Abstract: As black-box models become foundational to modern research, ensuring their stability is paramount for the realization of trustworthy artificial intelligence. The inherent diversity of inputs - ranging from structured Gaussian distributions to complex data with unknown structures - poses a significant challenge: how to stabilize black-box outputs while effectively leveraging available prior information. This paper introduces a task-oriented randomization methodology that adaptively tailors its strategy to the underlying generative mechanisms of the input data, specifically addressing unstructured complexities. A comprehensive suite of stability guarantees is proposed. Beyond establishing rigorous theoretical foundations for stability, the research provides a detailed analysis of the intrinsic trade-off between stability and exploration. Motivated by the architecture of Large Language Models, the framework is further extended to top-k ranking problems. The validity and effectiveness of the proposal are demonstrated through extensive numerical simulations and applications to the real-world dataset.

</details>


### [32] [Statistically Valid Hyperparameter Selection: From Tuning to Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25601)
*Amirmohammad Farzaneh,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该专论提出了一种基于“学习后测试 (LTT)”范式的统一统计框架，为人工智能系统超参数的选择提供了具有形式化统计保证的可靠性方案，有效控制了模型错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的超参数调优方法（如网格搜索、贝叶斯优化）多为经验性的“尽力而为”策略，缺乏对模型可靠性和安全性的形式化统计保证。

Method: 基于多重假设检验（Multiple Hypothesis Testing），利用 p-值 (p-values)、e-值 (e-values) 和集中不等式 (concentration inequalities) 等统计工具，对候选超参数集合进行严格的统计推断与控制。

Result: 该框架能够从有限样本中明确控制错误概率，确保证选出的超参数满足特定的应用需求（如平均风险约束、分位数风险或信息理论约束）。

Conclusion: 该专论提供了一种基于“学习后测试 (LTT)”原则的统一统计框架，能够形式化并保证超参数选择过程中的可靠性与安全性，填补了当前缺乏形式化统计保证的行业空白。

Abstract: Hyperparameter selection is a critical step in the deployment of modern artificial intelligence systems, given the need to tune degrees of freedom such as inference-time parameters, implementation-level settings, and thresholds driving decision rules. Despite its practical importance, hyperparameter selection is typically performed using best-effort empirical methods such as grid search or Bayesian optimization, which provide no formal statistical guarantees on reliability or safety.
  This monograph presents a unified statistical framework for reliable hyperparameter selection, centered on the learn-then-test (LTT) paradigm, which formulates the problem as multiple hypothesis testing over a candidate set of hyperparameters. The framework enables the selection of hyperparameters that provably satisfy application-specific reliability requirements -- such as bounds on average risk, quantile risk, or information-theoretic constraints -- with explicit, finite-sample control of error probabilities. The supporting statistical machinery, namely p-values, e-values, and concentration inequalities, is developed from first principles in a dedicated appendix.

</details>


### [33] [Gaussian Mean Field Variational Inference can Overestimate Predictive Variance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25745)
*James Odgers,Ben Riegler,Siddharth Swaroop,Vincent Fortuin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究指出MFVI在低估参数方差的同时，会导致训练分布区域预测方差的过估，这一发现解释了“冷后验”现象的部分成因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MFVI通常被认为会低估后验方差，但这一结论在预测性能上并不全面。论文旨在揭示MFVI在预测方差估计上的系统性偏差及其背后的数学机制。

Method: 以共轭贝叶斯线性回归（BLR）为分析对象，从理论上推导MFVI在参数空间与预测空间下方差表现的差异，并验证了其在训练分布内的预测方差特性。

Result: 证明了MFVI并非总是低估预测方差，在训练数据覆盖的方向上，MFVI的期望预测方差反而可能超出精确后验；在极端情况下，MFVI甚至无法在训练数据点上降低对先验的预测不确定性。

Conclusion: MFVI在参数空间倾向于低估方差，但在预测空间的方向上会存在系统性的方差过估，特别是在训练数据分布区域。这种过估现象与“冷后验效应”（Cold Posterior Effect）存在内在联系，调整温度参数有助于缓解该问题。

Abstract: Mean Field Variational Inference (MFVI) is widely understood to underestimate posterior variance. By analysing conjugate Bayesian Linear Regression (BLR), we show that this characterization is incomplete: while MFVI underestimates the variance in parameter space, it can overestimate the predictive variance compared to the exact posterior. We show that if the MFVI posterior underestimates predictive variances in some directions, it necessarily overestimates them in others. Crucially, this overestimation occurs in directions where the training data concentrates. This leads to the surprising result that, for a test point drawn from the training distribution, MFVI's expected predictive variance exceeds that of the exact posterior. We demonstrate a pathological case of this effect, where the MFVI posterior fails to reduce predictive variance compared to the prior on in distribution data. We connect these results to the Cold Posterior Effect, arguing that varying the temperature can correct this overestimation, yielding predictions closer to those of the exact posterior. We validate our theory on synthetic and real-world regression tasks.

</details>


### [34] [FedReLa: Imbalanced Federated Learning via Re-Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26037)
*Guangzheng Hu,Patricia Menéndez,Feng Liu,Mingming Gong,Guanghui Wang,Liuhua Peng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对联邦学习中异构性和类别不平衡共存的挑战，本文提出的 FedReLa 通过特征依赖的重标签机制校正决策边界，在无需全局分布知识与额外通信开销的情况下，有效提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习中，全局类别不平衡与跨客户端数据异构性常同时存在，且在缺乏全局分部信息（agnosticism）的情况下，现有的数据级处理方法难以应对严重的类别缺失问题，导致聚合模型性能下降。

Method: 提出 FedReLa 框架，核心在于引入了一个基于特征的标签重分配器（feature-dependent label re-allocator），通过对样本进行重标签（re-labeling）以校正有偏差的全局决策边界。该方法可与现有的算法级方法兼容集成。

Result: 在逐步不平衡（stepwise-imbalanced）和长尾（long-tailed）数据集上的实验表明，FedReLa 显著提升了少数类别的识别精度及整体预测准确率，效果优于现有主流方法。

Conclusion: FedReLa 是一种模块化、模型无关的数据级联邦学习方法，能够有效缓解全局类别不平衡与本地数据异构性共存带来的性能衰减，且无需获取全局数据分布信息，也不引入额外的通信开销。

Abstract: Federated learning has emerged as the foremost approach for decentralized model training with privacy preservation. The global class imbalance and cross-client data heterogeneity naturally coexist, and the mismatch between local and global imbalances exacerbates the performance degradation of the aggregated model. The agnosticism of global class distribution poses significant challenges for data-level methods, especially under extreme conditions with severe class absence across clients. In this paper, we propose FedReLa, a novel data-level approach that tackles the coexistence of data heterogeneity and class imbalance in federated learning. By re-labeling samples with a feature-dependent label re-allocator, FedReLa corrects biased global decision boundaries without requiring knowledge of the global class distribution. This modular, model-agnostic approach can be integrated with algorithmic methods to deliver consistent improvements without additional communication overhead. Through extensive experiments, our method significantly improves the accuracy of minority classes and the overall accuracy on stepwise-imbalanced and long-tailed datasets, outperforming the previous state of the art.

</details>


### [35] [When Does Synthetic Data Augmentation Improve Score-Based Imbalanced Classification?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26053)
*Zhengchi Ma,Pengfei Lyu,Anru R. Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了合成数据增强在分类任务中的双重性：在模型设定正确时其收益极低甚至有损，但在模型设定错误时，它能通过调整类别权重显著提升AUROC/AUPRC等指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管合成数据增强在处理类别不平衡问题中应用广泛，但其在基于分数的分类任务中的理论影响尚不明确，缺乏对AUROC、AUPRC等常用指标影响的系统性量化分析。

Method: 构建了一个分析合成少数类增强的统一理论框架，将增强效果分解为“有效类别加权改变”与“合成分布偏差”两部分；推导了极小极大下界（Minimax lower bounds）以评估指标极小遗憾值（regret rate），并分析了在模型正确与错误设定下的表现差异。

Result: 在模型正确设定下，原始估计器已达到最优统计效率；在模型错误设定下，合成增强可有效调节类别投影，补偿由模型偏差引起的排序问题，且给出了包含近似误差、有限样本估计误差及分布误差的量化改进界限。

Conclusion: 通过理论及模拟证明，在模型设定正确（Well-specified）时，合成数据增强无法从总体层面优化分类指标且可能引入偏差；但在模型设定错误（Misspecified）时，通过调整类别权重，增强手段能够修正排序误差，从而提升指标表现。

Abstract: Synthetic data augmentation is widely used to mitigate class imbalance, but its theoretical effects on score-based classification remain poorly understood. This paper develops a framework for characterizing when synthetic minority augmentation can improve threshold-integrated and threshold-optimized metrics, including AUROC, AUPRC, best-threshold balanced accuracy, and best-threshold \(\F_1\) score. We separate the effect of augmentation into two components: a change in effective class weighting and a discrepancy between the synthetic and true minority distributions. Under well-specified score models, the raw estimator already targets the likelihood-ratio ordering, which is population-optimal for the metrics considered. Consequently, augmentation cannot provide a fundamental population-level improvement beyond possible finite-sample variance reduction, and may introduce additional bias through synthetic distributional error. We further establish minimax lower bounds showing that the raw estimator already achieves the optimal metric-regret rate in the well-specified regime. Under misspecification, however, augmentation can play a qualitatively different role: by changing the effective class balance, it can alter the restricted-class projection and correct ranking errors induced by the raw imbalanced objective. We provide explicit improvement bounds quantifying the roles of approximation error, finite-sample estimation error, and synthetic distributional error. Simulation studies corroborate the theory, demonstrating limited gains under well-specification and nontrivial but nonmonotone improvements under misspecification.

</details>


### [36] [Automated Residual Plot Assessment With the R Package autovi and the Shiny Application autovi.web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24236)
*Weihao Li,Dianne Cook,Emi Tanaka,Susan VanderPlas,Klaus Ackermann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究针对传统残差图人工诊断的主观与低效问题，开发了一款利用计算机视觉技术自动评估残差图的工具（R包及Shiny应用），通过计算视觉信号强度（VSS）协助分析师更高效、客观地诊断线性模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性模型的残差评估主要依赖手工查看图表，存在主观性和不一致性，且在大规模数据场景下效率低下；现有的“排列组合协议”（lineup protocol）虽能降低主观性，但耗费大量人力资源，亟需自动化工具来优化该诊断过程。

Method: 开发了一种基于计算机视觉（Computer Vision）的模型，旨在自动评估残差图；提供配套的R包及Shiny交互式应用程序；通过算法预测“视觉信号强度”（Visual Signal Strength, VSS）来量化残差特征。

Result: 该工具能够有效量化残差图的视觉信号，为分析师提供客观的评估辅助指标，实现对模型拟合情况的自动化初步诊断。

Conclusion: 提出了一套基于计算机视觉的自动化工具（R包及Shiny应用），通过量化残差图的视觉信号强度，有效缓解了传统残差诊断中依赖人工评估的主观性、效率低及难以扩展的问题，为模型诊断提供了更为客观的辅助手段。

Abstract: Visual assessment of residual plots is a common approach for diagnosing linear models, but it relies on manual evaluation, which does not scale well and can lead to inconsistent decisions across analysts. The lineup protocol, which embeds the observed plot among null plots, can reduce subjectivity but requires even more human effort. In today's data-driven world, such tasks are well suited for automation. We present a new R package that uses a computer vision model to automate the evaluation of residual plots. An accompanying Shiny application is provided for ease of use. Given a sample of residuals, the model predicts a visual signal strength (VSS) and offers supporting information to help analysts assess model fit.

</details>


### [37] [Model selection with proper scoring rules on data sets of time series: prefer the mean scaled score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24715)
*Giorgio Corani,Stefano Damato,Dario Azzimonti,Lorenzo Zambon*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对多时间序列预测，该研究分析了模型选择中聚合指标产生的矛盾，指出分数分布的偏度是主因，并证明平均缩放得分法（Mean Scaled Score）在测试集规模充分时是最稳健且可靠的模型选择方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在针对多时间序列进行概率预测模型选择时，现有的聚合指标（如平均缩放分、平均排序、胜率）往往得出相互矛盾的结论，缺乏统一的理论依据说明哪种方法更优及其失效条件。

Method: 1. 通过理论分析证明了分数分布的偏度（skewness）是导致不同聚合指标（如平均排序、胜率、中位数与平均分）产生决策分歧的主因，特别是在短时间序列测试集中。2. 利用间歇性需求数据集及M5竞赛数据进行实验，验证了不同聚合准则在不同测试集规模下的表现。

Result: 1. 理论揭示了偏度问题会导致非均值聚合指标在测试集较短时选择错误模型。2. 测试集规模越大，各指标决策越趋于统一。3. 实验验证平均缩放得分法表现最为稳健，不易受异常值或偏度影响。

Conclusion: 平均得分（mean score）是模型选择中最可靠的标准。实验发现，在测试集足够大时，不同聚合指标趋于一致，而平均缩放得分（mean scaled score）即使在不同缩放因子下也能保持决策的一致性，是评估多时间序列预测模型的最稳妥方案。

Abstract: We study the problem of model selection among probabilistic forecasting models evaluated on datasets of multiple time series. The performance of a model on a single time series is quantified by the average value (score) of a proper scoring rule over a test set, but extending model selection to data sets of time series requires aggregating these scores. Common approaches either rely on scaling scores and averaging them (mean scaled score) or avoid scaling by using alternative statistics such as mean ranks or win rates. However, these approaches can yield conflicting conclusions. We show that such discrepancies arise from the skewness of the distribution of the scores, which is particularly pronounced when test sets are short. The skewness can cause non-mean criteria (e.g., mean rank, median, win rate) to select misspecified models. In contrast, the mean score is immune from this problem. We further show that, as the size of the test sets increases, all aggregation criteria converge to the same model selection decision, mitigating these discrepancies. Our experiments on intermittent demand time series, including data from the M5 competition, highlight the importance of sufficiently large test sets; the mean scaled score appears to be the more reliable approach, also because empirically we found its decision to remain consistent when different scaling factors are adopted.

</details>

### [38] [ActPlane: Programmable OS-Level Policy Enforcement for Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25189)
*Yusheng Zheng,Tianyuan Wu,Quanzhi Fu,Tong Yu,Wenan Mao,Wei Wang,Dan Williams,Andi Quinn*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: ActPlane 是一种利用 eBPF 在操作系统内核层强制执行 AI 智能体策略的系统，它解决了传统工具拦截和沙箱方案的局限性，实现了高层语义策略与底层系统执行的安全对接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 AI 智能体开发框架（Harnesses）在策略强制执行方面存在不足：工具层面的防护机制容易被绕过，而 OS 层的沙箱又缺乏语义感知，导致智能体难以理解错误原因；亟需一种既能覆盖所有执行路径，又能理解高层语义意图的底层强制执行方案。

Method: 构建名为 ActPlane 的策略引擎，利用 eBPF 技术在操作系统内核层实施强制管控；引入一种基于信息流控制（IFC）的领域特定语言（DSL）来定义跨事件路径的复杂策略，并提供具备语义反馈的隔离机制。

Result: ActPlane 在经验性研究、编程任务基准测试及安全性基准测试中表现优异，能够有效捕获工具调用拦截无法触及的间接执行路径，且仅带来 1.9%-8.4% 的性能开销。

Conclusion: ActPlane 提供了一种在操作系统内核层面强制执行 AI 智能体策略的范式，通过 eBPF 技术实现了语义感知反馈和隔离，成功弥合了高级意图与底层系统行动之间的鸿沟，在提升合规性的同时保持了低性能开销。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly run in production through harnesses, the software around the LLM, including an engine that enforces safety and effectiveness policies, e.g., 'run tests before committing.' Enforcing these policies requires bridging a semantic gap: policy intent is expressed in underspecified natural language, while enforcement must act on concrete system actions, e.g., which test to run. Many policies also define event ordering or data flow actions. Yet existing approaches fall short. Tool-call guardrails miss system actions that bypass the tool layer, while OS sandboxes control resource access instead of actions, returning opaque errors that confuse the agent. Our key insight is that policy context lives within the agent closest to the task, while enforcement must happen at the OS to cover all execution paths. We introduce ActPlane, a policy engine that lets agents declare policies and enforces them in the OS kernel with semantic feedback and isolation. ActPlane uses a simple information-flow control (IFC) DSL to support cross-event policies. We implement ActPlane with eBPF and evaluate it on policies from the empirical study, coding-task benchmarks, and safety benchmarks. ActPlane improves policy compliance, including on indirect execution paths that tool-call interception cannot observe, with 1.9%-8.4% overhead. ActPlane is at https://github.com/eunomia-bpf/ActPlane

</details>

### [39] [Power-Flexible AI Data Centers: A New Paradigm for Grid-Responsive Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25098)
*Chris Williams,Philip Colangelo,Ayse Coskun,Ethan Levine,Andy Neale,Ciaran Roberts,Shayan Sengupta,Nikhil Shirolkar,Varun Sivaram,Sarah Soares,Ethan Tiao,Scott Underwood,Daniel Wilson,Frank Sharp,Luke Wainwright,Harry Petty,Scott Wallace,Brandon Records*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种AI数据中心电网交互架构，通过智能负载调度和实时遥测，将GPU集群转变为灵活的电网资源，有效平衡了计算需求与电网稳定性之间的冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI基础设施的快速扩张导致电力需求激增，传统电力规划将数据中心视为不可控的峰值负荷，造成电网基础设施成本高昂且互联延迟严重。需探索如何让AI数据中心作为电网交互资产动态响应电力系统需求。

Method: 提出了一种整合电网信号、工作负载调度和电源遥测的系统架构，通过在130 kW的GPU集群上进行实地部署，验证了负载快速削减、持续限流、碳感知运行以及跨地理区域的性能感知负载迁移等能力。

Result: 实验证明该架构能实现精细化的集群功率控制，在不牺牲优先级任务服务质量的前提下，实现动态响应电网负载需求、降低碳排放并缓解电网压力。

Conclusion: 通过软件编排和智能调度，AI数据中心可从传统的“静态耗电负荷”转变为“电网交互资源”，在保证计算任务服务水平的同时，显著提升电网弹性、降低基础设施成本并增强计算可持续性。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of artificial intelligence (AI) infrastructure is driving unprecedented growth in electricity demand from data centers. Traditional power-system planning treats large computing facilities as inflexible peak loads, leading to costly infrastructure upgrades and long delays in grid interconnection. Recent work has shown that AI clusters can reduce electricity consumption during peak demand through software-based workload orchestration. This article explores how modern GPU-based AI data centers can operate as grid-interactive assets that respond dynamically to power system conditions. We describe an architecture integrating grid signals, workload scheduling, and power telemetry for fine-grained cluster power control. Experimental results from a real-world deployment on a 130 kW GPU cluster demonstrate multiple forms of flexibility, including rapid load reduction, sustained curtailment, and carbon-aware operation while preserving service levels for priority jobs. We further demonstrate performance-aware load shifting across geographically distributed clusters, enabling workloads to migrate toward regions with lower grid stress. Together, these capabilities transform AI infrastructure from static electricity consumers into flexible resources that support grid reliability, accelerate interconnection, and improve computing sustainability.

</details>


### [40] [Programmable Probabilistic Computer with 1,000,000 p-bits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25313)
*Navid Anjum Aadit,Xiuqi Zhang,Shuvro Chowdhury,Kevin Callahan-Coray,Kyle Lee,Saleh Bunaiyan,Sanjay Seshan,Clayton Thomas,Jason Twigg,Andrew Seawright,Forrest Brewer,Tathagata Srimani,Kerem Y. Camsari*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文通过互联多个 FPGA 构建了百万比特级的概率计算机，解决了单芯片计算能力的瓶颈，并揭示了分布式采样系统中通信频率与计算精度的通用权衡规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于 p-bit 的概率计算机受限于单芯片容量和内存带宽，无法处理更大规模的问题。作者旨在突破这一硬件物理限制，探索分布式架构在概率采样和组合优化任务中的可行性。

Method: 通过网络化 FPGA 组网构建分布式 Ising 机；利用 1-bit 边界状态通信机制降低带宽需求；通过时间比率 eta (通信频率/更新频率) 分析分布式系统行为，并建立集群平均场 (cluster mean-field) 模型。

Result: 成功实现了一个拥有 100 万 p-bit 的可编程概率机；Gibbs 采样速度超过每秒 1 万亿次翻转；定量确定了性能与通信频率的权衡关系，当 eta 高于特定阈值时，分布式系统性能等同于单片机。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种可扩展的百万比特概率计算架构，确立了分布式采样系统性能与通信频率之间的普遍权衡规律，证明了将概率计算机扩展至单芯片限制之外的可行性与设计准则。

Abstract: Probabilistic computers built from p-bits have been proposed as hardware accelerators for sampling and optimizing Ising models, but existing systems have been confined to a single chip, capped by its capacity and memory bandwidth. Here we break this limit by networking FPGAs into a single Ising machine far larger than any one device could hold, realizing a programmable probabilistic computer with one million p-bits. The machine performs Gibbs sampling at over a trillion flips per second while keeping every coupling weight in local on-chip memory. During execution, devices exchange nothing but 1-bit boundary states. This architecture exposes a question fundamental to any distributed sampler: how frequently boundary information must be refreshed for a partitioned machine to behave as an unpartitioned one. Using three-dimensional Edwards-Anderson spin glasses, we show that the answer is set by a single timing ratio, eta = f_comm/f_p-bit, of the boundary-exchange frequency to the local p-bit update frequency. Above a topology-dependent threshold, the distributed machine matches a monolithic GPU reference. Below it, residual energy still decays as a power law but with a reduced exponent, turning parallelism into a quantifiable throughput-accuracy tradeoff. A theoretical cluster mean-field model reproduces the same behavior, showing that this tradeoff is a universal property of partitioned stochastic dynamics. These results provide a programmable million-p-bit platform, demonstrated across spin glasses, Max-Cut, and Boolean satisfiability, together with a quantitative design rule for scaling probabilistic computers beyond the single-chip limit.

</details>


### [41] [EmuGEMM: Fused Tensor Core Kernels for Precision Emulation in Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25453)
*Denghui Lu,Alexander Maeder,Mathieu Luisier,Alexandros Nikolaos Ziogas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: EmuGEMM 通过融合计算内核消除了 Ozaki 方案在 GPU 上的内存访问瓶颈，显著提升了高精度矩阵乘法的计算性能，并在多种 GPU 架构上超越了 cuBLAS 的基准测试结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 GPU 中低精度运算单元与高精度运算单元的吞吐量差距日益扩大。Ozaki 方案虽能利用低精度单元实现高精度计算，但现有实现方法存在严重的内存读写瓶颈。

Method: 提出 EmuGEMM 算法，通过融合低精度 Tensor Core 内核，消除 Ozaki 方案在实现过程中对全局内存的冗余读写（intermediate materialization），优化了数据移动效率。

Result: EmuGEMM 在 Hopper 和 Blackwell 架构上表现卓越：Scheme I 实现了高达 83% 的 INT8 峰值吞吐量；对于大矩阵，Scheme I 性能优于 cuBLAS TF32（最高 1.7 倍），Scheme II 在复数运算上优于 cuBLAS ZGEMM（最高 5.5 倍）。

Conclusion: EmuGEMM 通过融合内核优化 Ozaki 方案，有效解决了内存瓶颈，在现代 NVIDIA GPU 上实现了超越 cuBLAS 的高精度矩阵乘法性能。

Abstract: Modern GPUs devote an increasing silicon budget to low-precision matrix-multiplication units, widening the precision-throughput gap for scientific computing workloads. Ozaki Schemes I and II offer an alternative by reconstructing high-precision general matrix multiplication (GEMM) from low-precision operations, yet existing implementations leave substantial performance untapped. In particular, intermediate results are repeatedly materialized in global memory, making data movement the dominant bottleneck. We present EmuGEMM, fused integer Tensor Core kernels for NVIDIA Hopper and Blackwell GPUs that eliminate redundant memory round-trips in both Ozaki schemes. Using Scheme I, EmuGEMM sustains up to 1,639 Top/s on Hopper (83% of INT8 peak) and 3,654 Top/s on Blackwell (81%). For large matrices, EmuGEMM surpasses cuBLAS TF32 throughput by up to 1.4x on Hopper and 1.7x on Blackwell, at comparable accuracy. Using Scheme II, EmuGEMM extends to complex arithmetic and outperforms cuBLAS ZGEMM by up to 2.3x on Hopper and 5.5x on Blackwell.

</details>


### [42] [CrossPool: Efficient Multi-LLM Serving for Cold MoE Models through KV-Cache and Weight Disaggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24506)
*Zhuoren Ye,Tianyu Wo,Dinghao Xue,Mingming Zhang,Yuchen Teng,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CrossPool 是一种专门针对混合专家模型（MoE）的冷模型推理服务引擎，通过解耦权重与 KV-Cache 内存池，并引入层级化调度与轻量化控制机制，有效解决了多模型并行服务中的内存浪费和长上下文处理瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在服务多个冷启动（低并发）MoE 模型时，预留最坏情况的 KV-Cache 容量浪费巨大且会导致内存利用率低下，传统的单片 GPU 内存管理方式无法支持突发性长上下文需求。

Method: 提出 CrossPool 架构，主要包括三项关键技术：1. 物理内存分离：将 FFN 权重与 KV-Cache 分离为两个独立的内存池；2. 层级流水线调度：通过层级化调度隐藏隐藏状态（hidden-state）传输开销；3. 持久化内核与控制下沉：利用控制下沉降低 CPU-GPU 间的交互开销。

Result: 相较于现有的基于 KV-Cache 的多模型服务系统，CrossPool 在低并发/冷模型场景下的 P99 每个 Token 延迟（TBT）降低了高达 10.4 倍。

Conclusion: CrossPool 通过从物理层面将权重（Weights）与 KV-Cache 解耦并分别管理，实现了高效的内存池化。这一方案显著提升了 GPU 内存利用率，支撑了突发性的长上下文请求，并在冷模型场景下大幅降低了延迟。

Abstract: Emerging LLM services increasingly host many sparse MoE models, yet most models receive sparse requests and remain cold. This creates a GPU memory problem: model weights are stable and model-determined, while KV-cache is transient and demand-determined. Because cold models rarely reach peak KV-cache demand at the same time, reserving worst-case KV capacity per model wastes memory; a shared KV-cache pool can instead provision aggregate active demand. However, KV-cache sharing is not sufficient when weights and KV-cache remain in a monolithic GPU memory pool. Static weights compete with dynamic KV-cache, and KV-head-limited attention under cold, low-concurrency traffic exposes only a fraction of replicated KV capacity, leading to low GPU memory utilization and weak long-context support. We present CrossPool, a serving engine for cold MoE models that separates FFN weights and KV-cache into two GPU memory pools: a weights pool that consolidates FFN weights across cold models, and a KV-cache pool that dynamically serves active requests while keeping attention local to KV-cache. CrossPool combines a KV-cache planner and virtualizer, a layer-wise pipeline scheduler that hides hidden-state transfers, and persistent kernels with control lowering to reduce CPU-GPU control overhead. With efficient GPU memory pooling, CrossPool underpins bursty long-context requests and outperforms the state-of-the-art kvcached-based multi-LLM serving system, reducing P99 TBT by up to $10.4\times$.

</details>

### [43] [BFMTrack: Latent Sequence Optimization for Physics-Based Motion Tracking with Behavioral Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25056)
*Thomas Rupf,Agon Serifi,David Müller,Sammy Christen,Ruben Grandia,Espen Knoop,Moritz Bächer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对行为基础模型在运动跟踪任务中的局限性，作者提出了一种潜在序列优化（LSO）框架，通过策略梯度与时间相关噪声对潜在变量进行序列化优化，实现了无需奖励工程的高精度动态动作跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的行为基础模型（BFM）虽能处理静态任务，但在处理需要精确时间序列跟踪的动态任务时，现有的启发式方法（如移动窗口平均）效果不佳，无法捕捉精细的运动动态。

Method: 提出了一种“潜在序列优化”（Latent Sequence Optimization, LSO）方法。该方法结合模拟演练（simulation rollouts）与策略梯度更新，在BFM的潜在空间中对一段潜在序列进行优化，并利用时间相关噪声（temporally correlated noise）来确保生成路径的平滑性与连贯性。

Result: LSO在密集跟踪、稀疏关键帧生成以及真实人形机器人部署等任务中表现优异，显著提升了控制的精确度与动作的自然连贯性，克服了传统方法对繁琐奖励工程的依赖。

Conclusion: 通过引入LSO，作者成功扩展了基础行为模型（BFMs）在处理时间依赖型任务（如精确动作跟踪）上的能力，证明了该方法无需复杂的激励函数工程即可实现高质量的运动控制，并具备跨仿真环境与真实机器人平台的通用性。

Abstract: Behavioral Foundation Models (BFMs) offer a promising path toward universal physics-based character control by organizing a rich repertoire of physically plausible behaviors into a latent space, guided by a large-scale motion dataset. While these models excel at time-invariant tasks, such as goal-reaching and state-based reward optimization, their latent space does not directly support time-varying objectives, such as tracking a motion sequence. For tracking, existing heuristics rely on moving-window-averaging that fails to capture the nuances of highly dynamic motions. In this work, we propose a novel Latent Sequence Optimization (LSO) to address these shortcomings. Our approach combines simulation rollouts with a policy gradient update to optimize over a sequence of latents, extending the capabilities of BFMs toward precise motion tracking without requiring reward engineering and tuning. To guide the optimization toward smooth, coherent latent trajectories, we model the latent sequence using temporally correlated noise. We validate our approach across dense tracking, sparse keyframing, and direct deployment onto a real humanoid robot.

</details>


### [44] [Invariant Kalman filtering for extended pose estimation in multi-IMU articulated rigid-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25083)
*Sven Goffin,Cédric Schwartz,Silvère Bonnabel,Olivier Brüls,Pierre Sacré*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了用于解析铰链式刚体系统位姿估计的IterIEKF框架，通过引入相对L-扩展位姿和伪观测约束，解决了传统IEKF难以处理运动学约束的问题，显著提升了估计精度与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（IEKF）在大规模铰链式刚体系统（如机器人或人体）的位姿估计中面临挑战，主要原因是难以直接处理物体间的位姿耦合及关节运动学约束。

Method: 引入“相对L-扩展位姿”（relative L-extended pose）Lie群表示来描述运动学树系统；将关节运动学约束转化为不变形式，并作为无噪声伪观测（noise-free pseudo-measurements）集成到迭代不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（IterIEKF）中。

Result: 在UR5e机器人和人体腿部运动实验中，该方法表现出更快的收敛速度和更低的运行变异性，且相比现有最佳基准算法，RMSE降低了至少50%。

Conclusion: 提出了一种针对运动学树系统的相对L-扩展位姿表示，通过迭代不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（IterIEKF）将关节约束作为无噪声伪观测纳入框架。该方法在收敛性、一致性及精度上均优于现有的EKF系列基准，实现了至少50%的RMSE降低。

Abstract: Accurate extended pose estimation (orientation, velocity, and position) for IMU-instrumented articulated rigid-body systems is a key challenge in robotics and human motion analysis. The invariant extended Kalman filter (IEKF) addresses this problem for a single rigid body with convergence guarantees and consistency under unobservability, but extending these properties to articulated systems is nontrivial: inter-body pose coupling prevents a direct application, and incorporating joint kinematic constraints within the invariant framework remains an open problem. To address this gap, we introduce the relative L-extended pose, a Lie group representation for kinematic-tree systems. With one IMU per body, it yields group-affine dynamics and allows joint constraints to be expressed in invariant form. We incorporate these constraints as noise-free pseudo-measurements within an iterated IEKF (IterIEKF), thereby preserving the convergence and consistency guarantees of invariant filtering. Validated on both a UR5e robot and a human leg, the proposed IterIEKF outperforms all EKF, IterEKF, and absolute-pose IterIEKF baselines. It converges faster, exhibits lower run-to-run variability, and consistently achieves the lowest RMSE, with reductions of at least 50% compared to the second-best filter across all scenarios considered in this work.

</details>


### [45] [ADM-Fusion: Adaptive Deep Multi-Sensor Fusion for Robust Ego-Motion Estimation in Diverse Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25111)
*Hasan Moughnieh,Ibrahim Ghaddar,Hadi Elham,Imad H. Elhajj,Daniel Asmar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ADM-Fusion 是一种端到端多传感器融合方法，通过自适应混合专家架构与跨任务注意力机制，动态优化不同传感器输入，有效解决了复杂和退化环境下的机器人自我运动估计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶等自主系统中，传感器可靠性会随环境剧烈波动。现有的固定权重融合方法难以应对传感器退化，因此需要一种能自适应平衡互补信息以提高运动估计鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于端到端深度学习的 ADM-Fusion 方法，核心包括：1. 适应性传感器混合专家（MoE）框架及内容感知路由（用于动态分配权重）；2. 旋转与平移分离的分支结构；3. 用于任务间信息共享的跨任务注意力机制。

Result: 实验表明，ADM-Fusion 在 CARLA-LOC 和 KITTI 数据集上表现优异，在面临传感器降级等挑战性环境下，其鲁棒性和精度均优于或媲美现有主流方法。

Conclusion: ADM-Fusion 证明了在利用自适应混合专家架构和跨任务注意力机制后，能够有效应对传感器退化环境，在保持鲁棒性的同时实现高性能的自我运动估计，并具备良好的仿真到现实（sim-to-real）迁移能力。

Abstract: Robust multi-sensor fusion is essential for reliable autonomy in diverse and degraded environments, where sensor reliability can fluctuate rapidly. Because different modalities fail in distinct ways, effective fusion should adaptively balance complementary cues rather than rely on fixed weighting. This adaptability is particularly important for ego-motion estimation, since accurate updates depend on the consistent integration of complementary sensor information. We propose ADM-Fusion, an end-to-end deep learning based multi-sensor fusion method designed to adapt to environmental changes and sensor degradation. ADM-Fusion employs an adaptive sensor mixture-of-experts framework with content-aware routing to dynamically assign weights to sensor inputs in real time. The system further incorporates separate translation and rotation branches, coupled through a cross-task attention mechanism to preserve task-specific specialization while enabling information sharing. ADM-Fusion is trained on the CARLA-LOC simulated dataset and subsequently fine-tuned on KITTI real-world data, demonstrating effective simulation-to-real transfer. Experiments show that ADM-Fusion remains robust under degraded conditions while maintaining competitive performance against existing methods.

</details>


### [46] [SurveilNav: Collaborative Object Goal Navigation with Robot and Surveillance System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25119)
*Ming-Ming Yu,Qunbo Wang,Rongtao Xu,Yanghong Mei,Yirong Yang,Longteng Guo,Wenjun Wu,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 SurveilNav 框架及相关数据集，利用机器人动态感知与多视角监控系统的协同，解决了复杂室内环境下的导航效率瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航研究大多局限于单机器人场景，难以在复杂、大规模环境中利用多视角监控信息，导致感知范围受限及盲区问题，影响任务执行效率。

Method: 提出了 SurveilNav 框架，包含主动相机调度（active camera scheduling）、联合 2D/3D 映射、基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的值估计，以及协同目标验证机制。同时构建了基于 Habitat-Sim 的大规模室内协同对象导航数据集（包含 74 层楼，206 个摄像头）。

Result: 在 HM3D 数据集上的实验表明，SurveilNav 在探索效率和导航成功率方面均优于现有方法，实现了新的 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: SurveilNav 通过协同机器人动态感知与监控静态全景，有效解决了单一智能体感知局限与监控盲区问题，在探索效率和导航成功率上达到 SOTA，证明了该协同架构在大规模任务中的应用价值。

Abstract: With the growing deployment of surveillance systems in factories, offices, and homes, integrating them with robots offers a promising direction for collaborative and efficient task execution. However, existing approaches largely focus on single-robot scenarios and struggle with multi-view collaboration in large-scale environments. In this paper, we present a novel indoor collaborative object navigation dataset built on Habitat-Sim, featuring 206 cameras across 74 floors. The dataset enables systematic evaluation of an agent's ability to exploit multi-view surveillance information. To address the limitations of single-robot perception, we propose SurveilNav, a collaborative navigation framework that integrates active camera scheduling, joint 2D/3D mapping, VLM-based value estimation, and collaborative target verification. By synergizing the robot's dynamic local perception with the static global view of surveillance, this architecture effectively overcomes both the limited perception range of single agents and the inherent blind spots of fixed cameras, resolving inefficient exploration. Experimental results on the HM3D dataset demonstrate that SurveilNav substantially outperforms existing methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance in both exploration efficiency and navigation success rate. Moreover, the system shows strong potential for applications in large-scale search, home environments, and rescue missions.

</details>


### [47] [AeroCast: Probabilistic 3D Trajectory Prediction for Non-Cooperative Aerial Obstacles via Transformer-MDN Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25122)
*Syed Izzat Ullah,Jose Baca*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AeroCast 是一种针对共享空域非合作障碍物的高效概率轨迹预测框架，通过 Transformer 和混合密度网络实现多模态分布预测，在提升精度的同时满足实时机载部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有确定性预测方法难以建模非合作障碍物（如鸟类、失控无人机）的多模态复杂运动；现有的循环神经网络方法难以捕捉长期运动特征，且无法为下游规划器提供分布信息。

Method: 提出了一种结合 Transformer 编码器与混合密度网络（MDN）的概率轨迹预测框架；采用平移不变的连续位移编码（consecutive displacement encoding）和校准导向的训练目标（calibration-oriented training objective）。

Result: 在九类混合真实与合成四旋翼数据集上，将 ADE 和 FDE 降低了约 50%；在负对数似然（NLL）和连续排名概率分数（CRPS）指标上优于对比方法；推理延迟低至 0.1ms/样本（100Hz）。

Conclusion: AeroCast 能够高效、准确地对非合作无人机障碍物进行多模态轨迹预测，其低延迟特性使其完全满足机载实时避障需求。

Abstract: Autonomous aerial vehicles operating in shared airspace must predict the future positions of non-cooperative obstacles to plan evasive maneuvers before a collision becomes unavoidable. Unlike cooperative systems that share intent, non-cooperative obstacles such as birds, uncontrolled drones, or debris exhibit multi-modal motion that deterministic predictors cannot adequately represent. Existing methods either rely on recurrent encoders that propagate temporal information sequentially, limiting their ability to capture long-range kinematic precursors of maneuver initiation, or produce point forecasts that provide no distributional information to downstream planners. This paper presents AeroCast, a probabilistic trajectory prediction framework that combines a Transformer encoder with a Mixture Density Network output head to predict per-timestep Gaussian mixture distributions over future three-dimensional displacements. A translation-invariant consecutive displacement encoding and a calibration-oriented training objective address the input design and mode-degeneracy challenges specific to mixture-based aerial trajectory prediction. On a hybrid real-and-synthetic quadrotor corpus spanning nine motion categories, AeroCast reduces Average Displacement Error and Final Displacement Error by approximately 50% relative to the baselines over a five-second horizon, and achieves the lowest negative log-likelihood and Continuous Ranked Probability Score among all compared methods. Ablation analysis identifies velocity input and model capacity as the primary contributors to prediction quality, and positional encoding as essential for long-horizon trajectory coherence. AeroCast inference completes in 0.1ms per sample, compatible with real-time onboard deployment at 100Hz.

</details>


### [48] [RGB: RL Guided Whole-Body MPPI for Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25123)
*Yunsoo Seo,Sol Choi,Euncheol Im,Myo Taeg Lim,Yisoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种 RL 引导的 MPPI 框架，通过在预训练 RL 策略上增加由 MPPI 实现的在线反馈闭环，实现了人形机器人兼具强鲁棒性与高任务精度的灵活控制，无需重训练即可执行新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习 (RL) 虽然鲁棒性强，但其行为高度依赖训练目标和指令接口，导致难以在不重新训练的情况下引入新的控制需求（如特定反馈目标），缺乏灵活性。

Method: 提出了一种 RL 引导的全身模型预测路径积分 (MPPI) 控制框架。该方法将预训练的 RL 策略作为 MPPI 的采样先验（Prior），引导 MPPI 在动态可行空间内进行动作采样；通过模块化的 MPPI 代价函数（Cost terms）实时在线纠正 RL 策略的偏差，以满足特定的约束或任务目标。

Result: 在 29 自由度的 Unitree G1 仿真实验中，系统实现了平均 280Hz 的高频稳定控制；相比纯 RL 基线，该方法显著提升了步行直行精度，并能有效跟踪额外的全身参考信号。

Conclusion: 通过将预训练的 RL 作为 MPPI 的采样先验，本文提出的框架成功实现了在保持 RL 鲁棒性的同时，通过 MPPI 提供灵活的在线任务修正能力，无需重训练即可提升复杂任务的精度。

Abstract: Humanoid robots require whole-body controllers that are both robust and precise in contact-rich environments. While deep reinforcement learning (RL) achieves robust stability, its behavior is tightly coupled to the training objective and command interface, making it difficult to add new feedback objectives without retraining. In this study, we propose an RL guided whole-body model predictive path integral (MPPI) framework that acts as an add-on feedback controller on top of a pretrained RL policy. Instead of using RL policy as the final controller, we use it as a sampling prior that biases MPPI rollouts toward dynamically feasible behaviors. Task objectives are specified through modular MPPI cost terms, and MPPI closes the loop by continuously correcting the RL prior online to satisfy these objectives without retraining the policy. Simulations on a 29-DoF Unitree G1 humanoid in MuJoCo demonstrate stable high-rate control (average 280~Hz). The proposed method improves task-level precision over a pure RL baseline under the same command interface. This is achieved by correcting systematic drift during straight walking and tracking additional whole-body reference signals imposed through the cost.

</details>


### [49] [Causality-Based Parametric Control Barrier Function for Safe Multi-Vehicle Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25134)
*Yiwei Lyu,Caleb Chang,John M. Dolan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对多车交互中的安全控制问题，提出了一种结合因果推理的参数化控制障碍函数方法，有效解决了因果识别困难与系统保守性过高的问题，实现了在复杂多智能体环境下的高效安全控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全驾驶控制器在多车交互场景下存在局限：一是难以处理车辆间的相互影响导致的行为因果模糊；二是基于最坏情况的分析方法导致车辆行为过于保守，降低了驾驶效率。

Method: 1. 引入因果推理（Causality Inference）机制来明确建模多车交互中的相互影响；
2. 扩展参数化控制障碍函数（Parametric-CBF）以纳入因果信息；
3. 设计了一种自适应安全关键控制器，基于学习到的行为预期对周围车辆做出响应。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在多种交互密集型场景下，相较于传统方法，能够更准确地对周围车辆建模，并在保持安全性的同时显著提高了任务处理的效率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于因果推理的参数化控制障碍函数（Causality-based Parametric-CBF）能够有效处理多车交互中的影响，在确保安全的前提下，通过学习周围车辆的行为预期，显著提升了任务执行的效率，避免了传统最坏情况分析的过度保守性。

Abstract: Safe control has been widely studied in various safety-critical applications, for instance, autonomous driving. In order to ensure the autonomous vehicle does not collide with other vehicles, it is essential to obtain an accurate expectation of surrounding vehicles' behavior and react adaptively. Instead of assuming fully cooperative and homogeneous vehicles using the same safety-critical controllers, recent works have been exploring different data-driven approaches to model the neighboring vehicles' underlying controllers with observed data. However, existing works either suffer from 1) the inter-vehicle influence during the multi-vehicle interaction, which makes it hard to determine the causality of surrounding vehicles' behavior in controller modeling, or 2) being dominated by the worst-case analysis, which may lead to overly conservative behavior. In this paper, we extend the prior work on Parametric-Control Barrier Function (Parametric-CBF) to multi-robot interactions with embedded causality inference to explicitly reason over the inter-vehicle influence. Given the learned Causality-based Parametric-CBF, we present an adaptive safety-critical controller that allows the ego vehicle to safely react to surrounding vehicles with the learned expectation. We demonstrate that by leveraging the motion flexibility among multi-vehicle systems, task efficiency can be greatly improved in various interaction-intensive scenarios.

</details>


### [50] [Memory Retrieval in Visuomotor Policies for Long-Horizon Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25136)
*Rutav Shah,Yisu Li,Femi Bello,Yuke Zhu,Roberto Martín-Martín*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HALO 是一种利用 VLM 先验引导和稀疏注意力机制的机器人控制策略，解决了离线模仿学习中长时记忆带来的虚假相关和误差累积挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人需要在部分可观测环境中利用记忆以实现长时程自主任务，但将 Transformer 架构整合进离线模仿学习时面临两大难题：一是容易学习到虚假关联，二是环境交互中的预测误差累积会导致模型漂移和级联故障。

Method: 提出了 HALO（一种带有注意力机制记忆检索的视觉运动策略）：1. 利用视觉语言模型（VLM）先验生成记忆相关的问答对，通过联合训练指导模型关注任务相关信息，抑制虚假相关性；2. 采用稀疏注意力机制限制检索范围至历史中最相关的片段，缓解自动闭环控制中的误差累积。

Result: 模型能够从长达 8 分钟的历史经验中检索任务相关信息，并在长时程控制任务中表现出更高的可靠性。

Conclusion: HALO 通过结合 VLM 先验引导检索和稀疏注意力机制，成功克服了离线模仿学习中长记忆带来的虚假关联和误差累积问题，显著增强了机器人在长时程任务中的控制可靠性。

Abstract: General-purpose robots operating in partially observable environments, such as homes, require memory to support autonomy. They must recall diverse information from the past, such as where objects were placed, which tasks a human partner has completed, and when an appliance was turned on. Achieving this versatility requires a general memory retrieval mechanism. Transformer architectures that use attention over long contexts for memory retrieval provide a promising approach, as they learn retrieval from data rather than relying on task-specific or hand-designed rules. However, directly incorporating them into imitation learning from offline data introduces two key challenges: (1) the policy may learn spurious correlations between past information and predicted actions, and (2) errors accumulate in memory due to prediction inaccuracies and their compounding interactions with the environment, causing model drift and cascading failures. To address both challenges, we introduce HALO, a visuomotor policy with an attention-based memory retrieval mechanism for long-horizon control. First, to suppress spurious correlations, HALO distills vision-language model (VLM) priors into the policy. It generates memory-dependent question--answer pairs from demonstration trajectories and trains jointly with a video question--answering objective, steering retrieval toward task-relevant information. Second, to reduce the impact of accumulated errors in memory during closed-loop control, HALO uses sparse attention that restricts retrieval to only the most relevant parts of the history. Together, these components enable more reliable long-horizon control by guiding the policy to retrieve task-relevant information from up to eight minutes of past experience. Project website: https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/HALO

</details>


### [51] [SwarmFly: A simulation platform for UAV swarm experiment design and validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25146)
*Abhishek Phadke,Karthik Kumar Vasudeva,Abhishek Joshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SwarmFly是一个新型开源MATLAB无人机集群仿真平台，通过模块化架构和多种协调模式，解决了现有工具扩展性差、场景固化的问题，并提供了一套完整的集群行为分析与压力测试方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源无人机集群仿真工具存在维护匮乏、学习曲线陡峭或场景单一的问题，缺乏一个综合性的、模块化的仿真与测试平台，限制了集群控制机制的深入研究。

Method: 开发了一个基于MATLAB的模块化仿真平台SwarmFly；集成了四种集群协调模式（领航-跟随、去中心化、异构中继、异构速度）；设计了包含八个插件的架构，允许研究人员在不修改核心代码的情况下扩展行为、故障模型和分析工具；并通过八项针对编队精度、抗风、故障恢复、能量续航等维度的实验进行验证。

Result: SwarmFly成功实现了对多智能体无人机集群的仿真，能够实时分析集群在内部和外部干扰下的状态；在八项系统性实验中表现出良好的稳定性与功能性，并支持向硬件在环（HIL）测试及更大规模集群扩展。

Conclusion: SwarmFly是一个功能全面、模块化且易于扩展的MATLAB仿真平台，通过八种受控实验证明了其在无人机集群行为分析、故障容忍和性能评估方面的有效性，为无人机集群研究提供了强有力的开源工具。

Abstract: The initial development phase of UAV swarms largely depends on simulation for experimental design and validation, yet existing open-source tools are often unmaintained, have steep learning curves, or are built around a single fixed scenario. The need for a comprehensive, modular simulation platform is a recognized research gap. This paper presents SwarmFly, a MATLAB-based simulation and test platform for multi- UAV swarms that addresses these gaps. SwarmFly combines a real-time operational map, four swarm coordination modes (leader-follower, decentralized, heterogeneous relay, and heterogeneous speed), simulated IMU telemetry, and IP-based geolocation with a plugin architecture that lets researchers add behaviors, fault models, and analysis tools without touching the core code. Eight bundled plugins extend the base simulator into a full test harness. The SwarmFly platform exposes multi-agent aerial swarms to a wide range of internal and external disruptions, enabling observation and quantification of underlying swarm control and behavioral mechanisms. This study verifies and characterizes each subsystem through eight experiments that measure formation accuracy, wind tolerance, fault recovery, energy endurance, and airspace compliance. The platform runs entirely in MATLAB. Its modular design supports straightforward extension toward hardware-in-the-loop testing, larger swarms, and higher-fidelity dynamics. An open-source release is available at [https://github.com/abhishekphadke/SwarmFly.git]

</details>


### [52] [Toward Low-Latency Vision-Language Models with Doubly-Correct Predictions in Egocentric Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25160)
*Qitong Wang,Fan Du,Pranav Maneriker,Jihui Jin,Christopher Rasmussen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人领域VLM剪枝中“效率与安全性/可解释性”的权衡问题，本文提出了一种理性启发式剪枝策略，在提升推断效率的同时，确保模型预测不仅准确而且具备可验证的证据基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协作任务对VLM的实时推断能力要求极高，而传统剪枝技术虽能降低模型复杂度，却往往牺牲了模型的“双重正确性”（即预测准确且证据定位准确），这在安全敏感的机器人领域不可接受。

Method: 提出了一种“理性启发（rationale-informed）”的剪枝策略，旨在通过强化证据与决策之间的对齐，解决现有剪枝方法在压缩时导致模型准确性下降或证据一致性缺失的问题。

Result: 在自我中心视角（egocentric）视频数据集上的基准测试表明，该方法不仅实现了最高水平的预测准确度，还在保持“双重正确性”方面显著优于现有的剪枝方案。

Conclusion: 本文呼应了高效且可靠的视觉语言模型（VLM）研究需求，强调在追求计算效率的同时，必须兼顾准确性与证据可解释性，以满足人机协作安全性与透明度的要求。

Abstract: The rapid rise of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in egocentric visual understanding has made low-latency inference in human-robot collaborative (HRC) tasks increasingly critical. Weight pruning techniques developed for VLMs to shrink model size and computation can be readily applied to satisfy the efficiency demands of on-board processing and real-time interactive robotics. Moreover, safe human-robot interaction demands pruning strategies that preserve doubly-correct predictions; outputs must be both accurate and evidentially grounded to mitigate risks and ensure user trust. In this paper, we present a new study of VLM pruning through the lens of doubly-correct prediction. Our experiments surprisingly show that existing pruning methods often preserve the right evidence localization but undermine correct prediction. To address this, we propose a rationale-informed pruning strategy that better aligns evidence with decisions. Benchmark results on egocentric video datasets demonstrate that our method not only achieves the highest prediction accuracy but also outperforms existing approaches in attaining doubly-correct predictions. We aim to stimulate research on efficient and reliable VLMs, ensuring accuracy-driven advances align with the transparency, auditability, and safety required for responsible human-robot interaction and embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [53] [fARfetch: Enabling Collocated AR-HRC in Large Visually Diverse Environments with VLM-Driven AR Content Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25162)
*Christian Fronk,Hanting Ye,David Hunt,Miroslav Pajic,Maria Gorlatova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: fARfetch 是一个专为大规模户外人机协作设计的 AR 系统，通过引入语义地标映射、空间缩略图及 VLM 驱动的视效自适应优化，显著提升了操作效率并降低了用户负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在大型、视觉环境复杂的户外场景中，AR 系统信息展示可读性差以及跨距离/跨视线人机协作交互困难的问题。

Method: 1. 共享语义环境映射：利用 AR 头显与机器人间的协同感知，通过地标可视化支持基于地标的指令与导航；2. 上下文感知空间缩略图（World-in-Miniature）：提供环境的全局视图，支持精细化路径规划；3. 基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的 AR 视图管理：根据环境视觉复杂度，动态调整虚拟内容的色彩、大小和朝向，确保可读性。

Result: 在 30.5 米规模的户外巡检任务中，与非 AR 基线相比：任务完成时间显著缩短（66%）；用户心理负荷（-43%）、时间压力（-34%）和挫折感（-66%）显著降低； legibility 调查证实了其在户外环境下的虚拟内容展示效果。

Conclusion: fARfetch 成功解决了大型多样化户外环境下的 AR 人机协作难题，通过语义映射、空间缩略图和视觉自适应机制，验证了在复杂户外环境下提升交互效率与可读性的可行性。

Abstract: Augmented Reality (AR) can improve collocated human-robot collaboration by making robot state and intent visible and enabling intuitive control, yet large, visually diverse environments like the outdoors challenge both interaction and content legibility, especially at long distances and beyond visual line of sight. We present fARfetch, an AR-HRC system that integrates (i) shared semantic environment mapping across an AR headset and robot that visualizes detected landmarks in AR to support landmark-grounded go-to commands, (ii) a context-aware world-in-miniature representation of the shared environment for fine-grained path authoring, and (iii) vision-language-model driven AR view management that jointly adapts virtual content color, size, and orientation to maintain legibility in large visually diverse environments. We implement fARfetch with a Meta Quest 3 headset and Unitree Go2 quadruped robot, and conduct a within-subjects user study (N=13) on a real-world large-scale (30.5m) outdoor inspection task. fARfetch yielded significantly faster completion times than a non-AR baseline (66%) and significantly lower workload in mental demand (-43%), temporal demand (-34%), and frustration (-66%). A custom legibility survey indicated fARfetch effectively maintained virtual content legibility in the large outdoor environment.

</details>


### [54] [Learning Perceptive Platform Adaptive Locomotion Controllers for Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25179)
*David Rytz,Kim Tien Ly,Ioannis Havoutis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于MorAL的形态感知强化学习架构，通过实验证明将感知信息整合至Critic层比完全感知（Actor-Critic）更适合且鲁棒，有效地提升了多形态足式机器人的地形适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人运动控制难以跨形态泛化，且全感知策略容易受到噪声干扰，而盲策略缺乏地形感知能力，缺乏一种在通用性、感知鲁棒性与形态适应性之间取得平衡的框架。

Method: 基于MorAL架构，结合多种参考四足机器人形态进行训练，并引入自适应地形课程学习（Adaptive terrain curricula）。对比了盲策略baseline、Critic感知的MorAL+方案以及完全感知（Actor-Critic）的PPAL方案，并在仿真与ANYmal实体机器人上进行验证。

Result: 结果表明，Critic感知方案在保持运动跟踪一致性和鲁棒性方面优于盲策略，且在感知噪声下比完全感知方案更加稳定。

Conclusion: 感知信息的融合位置（Critic-only vs Actor-Critic）对强化学习策略的鲁棒性至关重要。研究证明，在多形态四足机器人控制中，Critic感知方案比完全感知方案（Actor-Critic）更能平衡地形感知能力与噪声稳定性。

Abstract: Universal quadrupedal locomotion remains limited by the difficulty of integrating perception across diverse robot morphologies. State-of-the-art controllers rely on single-robot training or blind policies that omit real-time perception, leading to poor cross-embodiment generalization. Designing locomotion policies that remain robust across related quadruped morphologies while incorporating perception is challenging. Moreover, fully perceptive policies are often sensitive to noise, whereas blind controllers lack terrain awareness. In this work, we study how perception should be integrated into morphology-aware reinforcement learning architectures for deployable quadrupedal control. Building on MorAL, we train morphology-specialized universal controllers on multiple reference quadrupeds using adaptive terrain curricula. We compare a blind baseline, a critic-perceptive variant (MorAL+), and a fully perceptive actor-critic (PPAL). Policies are evaluated in simulation on flat and rough terrains, and deployed on ANYmal hardware. Results show that critic-only perception improves robustness and tracking consistency over blind baselines while remaining more stable than fully perceptive policies under perception noise. These findings highlight that perception placement and curriculum design are key factors for scalable, morphology-aware locomotion.

</details>


### [55] [RAVEN: Long-Horizon Reasoning & Navigation with a Visuo-Spatio-Temporal Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25206)
*Yixun Hu,Zhicheng Zheng,Lihan Zha,Chunwei Xing,Rajdeep Singh,Omar Hossain,Antonio Loquercio,Dhruv Shah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAVEN 是一种新型机器人代理记忆系统，通过直接存储视觉嵌入而非文本描述，实现了更精准、更高效的长视距语义空间检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长期部署机器人面临的痛点：现有的内存系统难以平衡精细视觉语义保留、时空接地与存储检索效率之间的矛盾，且传统的基于图片描述的方法存在信息丢失问题。

Method: 提出 RAVEN 代理记忆系统，利用向量数据库存储带有位姿和时间戳的视觉嵌入（visual embeddings），通过空间地图对检索进行接地（grounding），直接操作视觉嵌入而非通过图片转文字（captioning）过程。

Result: 在多个模拟和真实场景下的视频问答基准测试中，RAVEN 表现优于基于标注的记忆系统，性能比肩前沿 VLMs，检索成本降低了 10 倍，并在 Unitree Go1 机器人上验证了长视距导航任务的有效性。

Conclusion: RAVEN 提供了一种高效、可扩展且无需文本标注的代理记忆系统，在保持细粒度视觉语义的同时，显著降低了长视距机器人任务的检索成本，并成功在真实机器人系统上完成验证。
