Abstract: Long-term robot deployment requires a compact and scalable memory that preserves fine-grained visual semantics, grounds observations in space and time, and enables efficient storage and retrieval. In this paper, we propose RAVEN, an agentic memory system for long-horizon robotic question answering and navigation. RAVEN stores visual embeddings with pose and time in a vector database, and grounds retrieval in a spatial map to answer queries and navigate to goals. By operating directly on visual embeddings, RAVEN avoids lossy image-to-text captioning and enables accurate semantic, spatial, and temporal retrieval at scale. Across several simulated and real-world video question-answering benchmarks, RAVEN consistently surpasses caption-based memory systems and matches frontier VLMs on long-horizon tasks at 10$\times$ lower retrieval cost. Finally, we instantiate RAVEN on a Unitree Go1 robot for the task of long-horizon navigation for natural language goal-reaching, and show successful deployment over several large indoor environments.

</details>


### [56] [Wear-Clearance-Impact Coupling in the Jansen Linkage: A Gait-Durability-Optimized Design Slows Joint Loosening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25208)
*Jichao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文建立了一种考虑间隙、碰撞与非线性磨损的Jansen步行机构动力学模型，研究发现忽略关节间隙会极大低估负载与磨损，并证明了经耐用性优化的设计在考虑混沌动力学反馈后，依然具备显著的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期研究发现经典Jansen连杆设计在耐用性上不占优，但该研究将关节理想化为无间隙模型，无法反映真实的在役退化。本文旨在通过放松此假设，深入探索真实动力学环境下的关节磨损与动力学性能。

Method: 构建了Jansen腿的向前动力学模型，将理想铰链替换为含间隙的关节（Lankarani-Flores接触定律与Ambrosio摩擦），并通过Archard磨损定律形成“磨损-间隙-冲击”反馈循环，基于集合模拟（ensemble simulation）对不同设计进行统计对比。

Result: 1. 忽略间隙会严重低估负载（理想模型48N vs. 间隙模型104N-426N）；2. 耐用性对冲击高度敏感，设计对比需基于统计平均，文中优化设计在统计意义上显著优于传统设计（磨损降低约7-9倍）；3. 磨损高度非均匀，集中于约10度载荷弧，传统均匀间隙假设低估了局部磨损36倍。

Conclusion: 通过耦合考虑间隙、碰撞和磨损的动力学模型，证实了Jansen步行机构在考虑真实关节磨损后的设计耐用性优势。研究建立了首个耦合模型，并提出了实验验证协议。

Abstract: A companion study introduced joint durability into the dimensional design of the Theo Jansen walking linkage and found its classical "holy numbers" Pareto-dominated, but it modelled the revolute joints as ideal, clearance-free pins, so its wear figures were relative rankings, not a prediction of in-service degradation. Here we relax that idealization. We build a forward-dynamic model of the Jansen leg in which a revolute joint becomes a clearance joint with a continuous normal contact law (Lankarani-Flores, hysteresis-damped) and Ambrosio friction, integrated as a constraint-stabilized differential-algebraic system, and couple it to the Archard law in a wear->clearance->impact feedback loop. Three findings emerge. First, neglecting clearance underestimates the peak joint load: the clearance model gives a peak contact force of ~104 N at the load-bearing pin against ~48 N for the ideal joint (~2x amplification), rising to ~426 N when two joints carry clearance at once. Second, the coupling is strongly impact-sensitive--single trajectories are non-monotonic and can reverse the design ranking, a chaos consistent with the literature--so designs must be compared statistically; over an ensemble of 16 randomized phases the optimized joint is robustly more durable, with per-cycle wear ~9-7x lower (peak force ~4x lower) at one clearance joint and still ~1.7x lower on both with two (p<0.01 throughout). Third, the wear is strongly non-uniform--it concentrates on a ~10 deg load arc--so assuming uniform clearance growth underestimates local clearance growth by ~36x. The clearance-free durability advantage thus survives the chaotic, multi-joint, non-uniformly-worn coupling in the ensemble mean. We deliver the first clearance-coupled forward-dynamic model of the Jansen leg and specify a falsifiable protocol to test each prediction.

</details>


### [57] [RigPI: Dynamic Parameter Identification of Rigid Body via VLM-Seeded Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25212)
*Xincheng He,Rongrong Zhang,Wei Jiang,Wenqiang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RigPI是一个整合VLM先验与可微仿真技术的两阶段优化框架，能够在噪声环境下稳健地辨识复杂刚体（含多连杆）的动力学物理参数，提升了机器人交互的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人操纵与数字孪生应用中，由于传感器噪声、建模误差及先验知识匮乏，从真实世界交互中准确辨识物体的物理参数（惯性与摩擦属性）依然是一个极具挑战性的问题。

Method: 提出名为RigPI的系统框架：1) 利用视觉语言模型（VLM）提供初始化与限制搜索空间；2) 构建包含视觉、力矩与运动观测的可微仿真流水线；3) 采用两阶段优化策略，通过梯度信息对物理参数（惯性与摩擦）进行高效精炼。

Result: 在真实世界中针对具有旋转和移动关节的物体进行了广泛实验，结果表明该框架能实现准确、稳定的参数估计，并能通过所辨识参数成功复现机器人操纵轨迹，具备良好的预测真实性。

Conclusion: RigPI是一个稳健且有效的物理参数辨识框架，通过结合视觉语义先验与可微仿真，成功解决了复杂机器人交互场景下的物理参数估计难题，并验证了其在实际机器人任务中的预测有效性。

Abstract: Accurate physical parameter identification of manipulated objects is fundamental to advanced robotic manipulation and the construction of faithful digital twins. However, acquiring physically consistent inertial and frictional properties from real-world interactions remains challenging due to sensing noise, modeling errors, and limited prior knowledge. This paper presents \textbf{RigPI}, a systematic framework for identifying dynamic parameters of both unconstrained rigid bodies and multi-link rigid bodies during robot-object interaction. RigPI integrates vision-based semantic priors, force-torque measurements, and motion observations within a differentiable simulation pipeline. A vision-language model (VLM) provides informed initialization and a constrained search space, while gradient information from a differentiable physics simulator enables efficient and stable parameter refinement. The proposed two-stage optimization strategy alleviates sensitivity to noise and avoids physically implausible solutions. Extensive real-world experiments on objects with revolute and prismatic joints demonstrate that RigPI achieves accurate and stable parameter estimates, and successfully reproduces manipulation trajectories on a real robot with parameter-aware predictive validity. These results highlight the effectiveness and robustness of RigPI for real-world robotic system identification tasks.

</details>


### [58] [Swazure: Swarm Measurement of Pose for Flying Light Specks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25222)
*Hamed Alimohammadzadeh,Shahram Ghandeharizadeh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Swazure协同定位算法，旨在解决多无人机3D显示阵列中传感器有效距离限制导致的定位数据丢失问题，并优化了遮挡处理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在由微型无人机（FLS）构成的3D多媒体显示阵列中，FLS必须精确获取彼此的相对位姿，但传感器通常存在有效的精度范围，当FLS超出此范围时会导致定位数据缺失，该研究旨在解决这一通信与定位限制。

Method: 提出了一种名为Swazure的新技术，通过FLS之间的协同定位来补全缺失的传感器数据；采用数据抽象技术实现物理数据独立性；针对传感器遮挡问题，设计了“Move Obstructing”和“Move Source”两种启发式算法。

Result: 实验结果表明，在适当规模下，Swazure能够实现FLS邻居100%覆盖定位；在最坏情况下的复杂遮挡场景中，“Move Obstructing”启发式算法能够解决约30%的遮挡问题。

Conclusion: Swazure通过协同定位解决了微型飞行器（FLS）在传感器有效范围外的数据缺失问题，并利用“Move Obstructing”启发式算法有效提升了复杂场景下定位的覆盖率与抗遮挡能力，实现了数据与传感器硬件的解耦。

Abstract: One may construct a 3D multimedia display using miniature drones configured with light sources, Flying Light Specks (FLSs). Swarms of FLSs localize to illuminate complex 3D shapes and animated sequences consistent with the coordinates of points in a point cloud. This requires FLSs to accurately measure their pose relative to one another using sensors such as cameras. Such sensors have a sweet range in which they provide the highest accuracy. A challenge is how an FLS tracks another FLS outside its sensor's sweet range, dictated by the point cloud data. We address this challenge by proposing a novel technique called Swazure that solves the missing sensor data using cooperation among FLSs. It implements physical data independence by abstracting the physical characteristics of the sensors, making point cloud data independent of the sensor hardware. The size of an FLS relative to the minimum distance between points of a point cloud is an important parameter. With medium-sized FLSs, Swazure is able to position 100% of the FLS's neighbors. Larger FLS sizes may result in potential obstructions that prevent Swazure from quantifying relative pose. We present two heuristics, Move Obstructing and Move Source, to address this limitation. Our experimental results show the superiority of the Move Obstructing heuristic which resolves approximately 30% of obstructions in the worst case scenario.

</details>


### [59] [Spatio-Temporal Retrieval-based Priors for Adaptive Computational Teaching in Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25224)
*Deepak Edakkattil Gopinath,Xiongyi Cui,Jonathan DeCastro,Avinash Balachandran,Guy Rosman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模仿学习的自适应教学模型，通过最近邻检索与交叉注意力机制有效捕捉师生交互的历史时间特征，在低数据环境下实现了高性能运动任务的有效辅导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化辅导系统多依赖局部、上下文相关的推理，无法处理长期的时间依赖性和师生多次交互的累积效应，且在复杂运动任务（如高性能驾驶）中面临数据稀缺的挑战。

Method: 提出了一种基于模仿学习的计算模型，包含一个专门的时间推理模块。该方法利用最近邻检索（Nearest Neighbor Retrieval）和交叉注意力先验（Cross Attention Prior）来处理交互历史，并在编码器-解码器架构中实现协同教学。

Result: 通过半合成闭环纵向数据集和小型真实模拟器竞赛数据集验证，该模型在一致性与自适应能力上显著优于非自适应基准及其他现有的自适应模型。

Conclusion: 该模型通过结合最近邻检索和交叉注意力机制，成功解决了复杂运动任务中自适应教学的时间推理难题，在低数据环境下表现优于非自适应及其他对比基准模型。

Abstract: Learning-based automated coaching systems for complex motor tasks such as high-performance driving remain limited in the ability to be adaptive by their reliance only on local, context-dependent reasoning, failing to account for the long-term temporal nature of student learning and the cumulative impact of repeated teacher-student interactions. In this paper, we propose an imitation learning based computational model for adaptive teaching with a dedicated temporal reasoning module that can reason over the interaction history under low-data regimes. To compensate for limited amounts of interactive training data, and based on the repetitive nature of the teaching process, the model relies on a nearest neighbor retrieval and cross attention prior, reasoning only on a narrowed-down set of semantically similar past interactions with an encoder-decoder based concurrent teaching model. We validate our approach with (i) a novel semi-synthetic closed-loop longitudinal student-teacher interaction dataset based on Waymo Open Motion Dataset and (ii) a small-scale real-world naturalistic simulator race coaching dataset. Our results reveal the consistent advantage of our adaptive teaching model with the nearest neighbor retrieval and cross-attention prior over a non-adaptive baseline as well as a suite of adaptive models that differ in their choice of priors and temporal fusion mechanisms.

</details>


### [60] [GRAFT: Graph-Based Affordance Transfer via Part Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25241)
*Mengying Lin,Utkarsh Mishra,Ajay Mandlekar,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRAFT 是一个几何感知的零样本机器人操作迁移框架。它通过构建基于部件的图表征，结合语义检索与细粒度几何匹配，仅需单次演示即可将操作技能精准迁移至未见过的物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的机器人操作方法在面对未见过的物体时泛化能力较差，且通常需要大量演示数据。现有的语义检索方法忽略了对操作至关重要的几何相似性。

Method: 提出 GRAFT 框架，将物体建模为基于部件的图（part-based graphs）。方法包含两个层级：1. 部件级描述符：负责全局实例检索及部件对齐；2. 顶点级描述符：负责细粒度的接触点匹配。通过检索最相似的演示实例，将接触点传播到新物体上。

Result: 该方法成功实现了仅通过单次演示（one-shot）即可将操作能力迁移至全新物体，在维持高操作精度的同时，克服了单纯语义检索带来的几何缺失问题。

Conclusion: GRAFT 通过结合语义和几何特征，利用基于部件的图表征，实现了在仅需单次演示的情况下，将操作技能零样本迁移至未见过的目标物体，有效解决了机器人操作泛化难题。

Abstract: Generalizing robotic manipulation to unseen objects remains challenging, as learning-based approaches require many demonstrations and fail in few-shot settings. Prior work transfers affordances through semantic retrieval, but semantics alone neglect geometric similarity, which is critical for manipulation. We propose GRAFT, a geometry-aware correspondence framework for zero-shot manipulation transfer using only one demonstration per object. Objects are represented as part-based graphs, where part-level descriptors support global instance retrieval and part correspondence, and vertex-level descriptors enable fine-grained contact point matching. For an unseen object, our method first retrieves the most functionally and geometrically similar instance from the demonstration buffer with aligned functional parts, and finally propagates the contact points through point-wise correspondence.

</details>


### [61] [An Integrated Hardware-Software Design for Low-Data Spatial Defect Detection in Robotic Visual Inspection with Hybrid Optoelectronic Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25277)
*Chaoqing Tang,Jiaxuan Li,Huanze Zhuang,Guiyun Tian,Chao Wang,Yihao Ouyang,Wenzhong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种光电融合计算架构，通过硬件光学卷积和压缩感知减少数据冗余，并利用CLIP指导实现无需人工标注的缺陷定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人视觉检测中数据过载问题，以及传统模型对海量shape-level像素级人工标注的严重依赖。

Method: 1. 硬件级：引入基于数字微镜器件（DMD）的光学卷积层，通过光子域进行特征提取；2. 数据压缩：采用分块压缩感知策略，将空间信息编码为低维时间信号；3. 标注策略：利用CLIP模型的多模态对齐能力，通过自然语言引导实现无像素级标注的缺陷定位；4. 评估指标：提出定位注意准确度（LAA）指标。

Result: 在透明材料缺陷检测实验中，相比传统影像，数据量减少90%，CNN计算量降低60%，并验证了压缩率、块大小等参数对精度的影响。

Conclusion: 该架构通过软硬件协同设计，在不牺牲检测精度的前提下，显著降低了数据量（90%）和计算负载（60%），为边缘计算和高数据流工业场景提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: To address data overload and inefficient shape-level annotation in robotic visual inspection, this paper proposes a hardware-software integrated optoelectronic architecture. A non-imaging, low-data paradigm is established to minimize annotation dependency. First, a sensor-in-the-loop strategy reconfigures a Digital Micromirror Device (DMD) as a physical optical convolutional layer, enabling photonic-domain feature extraction that unifies sensing hardware and processing software. To suppress data volume at the source, a block-based compressed sensing strategy encodes spatial information into low-dimensional temporal signals, drastically reducing redundancy. Subsequently, to bypass laborious manual defect shape annotation, natural language descriptions guide the network to align with highly generalizable features from Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP), steering the attention maps of the optoelectronic neural network toward defect shapes. Furthermore, a Localization Accuracy for Attention (LAA) metric is proposed to quantify shape-level defect localization performance. Experiments on transparent material defect detection validate the system's effectiveness. Parametric analysis reveals how measurement matrices, compression ratios, and block sizes affect accuracy. Results show that, compared to traditional imaging, the proposed architecture maintains equivalent accuracy while reducing data volume by 90% for Vision Transformers and computational workload by 60% for Convolutional Neural Networks. This low-data paradigm offers an efficient solution for industrial automation scenarios involving massive data streams, high acquisition costs, or constrained edge resources.

</details>


### [62] [DynaMOMA: Instantaneous Prediction of Grasp Poses for Mobile Manipulation of Dynamic Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25295)
*Zhinan Yu,Junyan Xu,Jiazhao Zhang,Zheng Qin,Yijie Tang,Yuhang Huang,Yihan Cao,Zhiyuan Yu,Yongjun Wang,Renjiao Yi,Chenyang Zhu,Kai Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于处理动态物体移动抓取的框架 \ours{}，通过结合“基于锚点的扩散模型轨迹预测”与“全身强化学习控制策略”，成功实现了对动态目标的高效、精准抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决移动机器人对动态物体进行抓取时，移动基座与机械臂协调困难，以及由于物体状态快速变化导致难以准确预测抓取轨迹的关键挑战。

Method: 提出了一种名为“\ours{}”的框架，包含两个核心部分：1. 基于锚点的扩散模型（Anchor-based Diffusion Model），用于预测时间序列上一致的短时抓取轨迹；2. 基于全身强化学习（Whole-body RL）的控制策略，利用预测的轨迹特征进行动作决策，并引入了“预期导向奖励”（Anticipation-guided reward）来优化抓取表现。

Result: 在Isaac Gym模拟实验中，该方法在多种动态抓取任务中表现优异；在现实世界的实验中，也证明了该框架具备极强的泛化性能，能够稳定应对复杂的动态环境。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架有效地解决了动态移动操作中轨迹预测与全身控制的协同问题，通过动作导向的奖励机制和锚点扩散模型实现了对动态目标的高效抓取，且在模拟和现实世界中均表现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation is a fundamental robotics task and has advanced rapidly in recent years, enabling robots to navigate, reach, and interact with objects in complex environments. However, mobile manipulation of dynamic objects remains highly challenging, as robots must coordinate the mobile base and arm while adapting to continuously evolving target poses. A key challenge lies in predicting temporally consistent short-horizon grasp trajectories from dynamic observations. In this work, we propose \ours{}, a dynamic mobile manipulation framework that couples instantaneous grasp trajectory prediction with whole-body control policy. Our predictor uses an anchor-based diffusion model to generate temporally consistent short-horizon grasp trajectories conditioned on historical observations. The predicted trajectories are then encoded as compact features and fed to a whole-body reinforcement learning policy, which controls the mobile manipulator for dynamic grasping. We further introduce a anticipation-guided reward that equips the policy with an anticipatory grasping horizon by adaptively shifting the target from the current grasp observation to the instantaneously predicted grasp trajectory. Through extensive experiments in Isaac Gym simulation, we show that our method achieves strong performance in mobile manipulation of dynamic objects across diverse settings and grasping metrics. Furthermore, our predictor and policy demonstrate strong generalizability in real-world experiments.

</details>


### [63] [WaveForward: An Omnidirectional Passive Wheeled Quadruped Robot with Casters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25299)
*Chuanlin Zhao,Qifeng Zheng,Shuhan Wang,Tiancheng Ma,Weixian Lin,Xin Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于标准万向轮的低成本被动轮足机器人，利用非对称强化学习与特定的肢体扭转策略，在实现全向移动的同时，相比纯足式运动将运输效率大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主动轮足机器人硬件设计极其复杂且成本高昂，而足式移动在长距离运输中能效较低，旨在寻找一种低成本、高效率的轮足全向运动实现方案。

Method: 1. 硬件：在足端安装标准万向轮；2. 控制策略：采用非对称Actor-Critic强化学习框架，利用被动轮的角度与速度信息；3. 运动学：设计了基于速度指令的万向轮基座姿态调整策略，并通过特定驱动方式触发万向轮扭转振荡产生推进力。

Result: 通过绕桩测试与模态切换实验验证，机器人具备了优异的全向移动灵活性；在运输性能上，相比纯足式运动，运输成本（COT）降低了89.1%。

Conclusion: 提出了一种低成本的被动轮足机器人方案，通过简单的万向轮与特有的驱动策略，显著提升了全向运动能力，并将物料搬运成本（COT）降低至原先的10.9%（降低幅度达89.1%）。

Abstract: Wheeled-legged robots possess both agile mobility for traversing complex terrains and high efficiency, making them suitable for long-distance transportation applications. Conventional actuated wheeled robots require specialized hardware and electrical design due to the incorporation of wheel components. We propose a novel and low-cost passive wheeled legged robot equipped with standard casters on each leg to obtain omnidirectional mobility. The control method employs an asymmetric actor-critic structure, enabling the utilization of the privileged information of the passive caster's angles and velocities. We develop a caster base posture adjustment strategy based on velocity commands, utilizing actuated joints to modify the caster base joint axis posture and thereby adjust the propulsion direction of the casters. Moreover, we implemented multiple propulsion modes to achieve varying degrees of caster twisting oscillation, converting these into propulsive force. We conducted a slalom test and mode switch experience, which shows the passive wheeled quadruped could achieve omnidirectional movement versatility, and reduce the cost of transport (COT) by up to 89.1% with respect to legged motion.

</details>


### [64] [AI Coaching for Accelerating Human Skill Development with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25337)
*Wei Wang,Enlin Gu,Antonio Loquercio,Haimin Hu,Rahul Mangharam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于非合作动态博弈的AI教练框架，通过智能动态调整辅助力度与“生产性失败”机制，有效促进人类运动技能的学习，避免了传统过度辅助导致的技能退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的AI副驾驶虽然能提高任务表现，但往往导致人类产生过度依赖并引致技能退化（skill atrophy）。研究旨在开发一种AI教练，通过动态调整辅助力度，引导人类自主掌握运动技能而非单纯完成任务。

Method: 1. 将教练-受学者交互形式化为非合作动态博弈；2. 开发了一种结合自适应共享控制与训练者因果影响概率模型的强化学习框架；3. 实施了包含33名参与者的第一人称视角（FPV）无人机竞速用户研究。

Result: 在FPV无人机竞速的用户研究中，该方法在人类技能习得效果上显著优于现有的AI教练基准模型。

Conclusion: 通过将AI教练建模为博弈论中的非合作动态博弈，本研究证明了战略性脚手架（scaffolding）和允许特定比例的“生产性失败”对于提升人类技能习得至关重要，优于单纯的辅助控制。

Abstract: AI copilots can substantially boost human performance through shared control, but excessive assistance can induce over-reliance and skill atrophy. This paper studies how an embodied AI agent can act as a coach that accelerates human motor-skill development. We argue that effective coaching requires strategic scaffolding and stepping back that are aligned with the learner's capability, allowing productive failures that drive learning. We formalize the interactive AI coaching process as a non-cooperative dynamic game in which the learner optimizes task performance while the coach targets the learner's independent competence. Building on this formalism, we develop a reinforcement learning framework combining adaptive shared control with probabilistic models of the coach's causal influence on skill evolution, enabling tractable training of coaching policies. A comprehensive user study (N=33) on first-person-view drone racing shows significant gains in human learning outcomes over state-of-the-art AI coaching baselines.

</details>


### [65] [Self Capacitive Tactile Sensor System designed for Companion Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25348)
*Mohsin Ali,Hidenobu Sumioka,Shuhei Ikemoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自电容原理的简单、可扩展类人机器人触觉感知系统，通过简化导电架构并利用FPGA进行边缘端实时处理，实现了高效、低延迟的全身触觉感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统触觉传感系统存在结构复杂、布线繁琐、成本高、扩展性差以及对机器人主处理器计算负载过大等问题，难以满足类人机器人全身触觉感知的需求。

Method: 提出了一种基于自电容原理的触觉传感系统；采用单一导电织物层和简化的导电织物布线架构；使用FPGA实现决策树分类器进行实时边缘计算。

Result: 成功构建了基于FPC的100点传感器阵列；实验验证了100Hz及以上采样频率可准确捕捉并区分多种接触类型（轻触、慢拍、快拍、击打）；验证了FPGA端处理方案有效降低了主处理器压力及系统延迟。

Conclusion: 该系统为类人机器人提供了一种简单、可扩展且硬件友好的触觉感知方案，通过FPGA板载处理实现了低延迟、低功耗的实时交互识别，非常适合全身触觉覆盖。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is essential for humanoid robots to achieve safe physical interaction, dexterous manipulation, and truly human-like responsiveness. However, the design of such systems remains challenging. Conventional approaches often suffer from complex multilayer structures, intricate wiring, high cost, and poor scalability, making it difficult to realize full-body tactile sensing with real-time, low-latency detection while maintaining minimal computational load on the robot's main processor. In this work, we present a simple, scalable and hardware friendly tactile sensing system for a companion humanoid robot based on the self-capacitance principle. The proposed sensor system employs a single conductive fabric layer with a conductive fabric wire architecture and does not require intricate electrode patterning. Scalability was demonstrated by fabricating a 100-point sensor array on a flexible printed circuit (FPC). Evaluation across sampling frequencies showed that 10 Hz is insufficient and misses transient events, whereas 100 Hz and 1000 Hz reliably capture and clearly distinguish all interaction types: gentle touch, slow tapping, fast tapping, and hitting. A decision-tree classifier was implemented directly on the FPGA, offloading real-time inference from the Raspberry Pi 4 with minimal latency and negligible power overhead. This design fully meets the tactile sensing requirements of the HIRO-chan robot and is well-suited for full-body tactile sensing in HIRO-chan and other companion robots.

</details>


### [66] [Decoupling Semantics and Geometric Grounding: Spatial Visual Prompts for Language-Conditioned Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25360)
*Yanzhe Tang,Xinyu Shao,Yuxuan Hu,Siyu Chen,Bowen Yang,Yajun Gao,Tongtong Cao,Xiu Li,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对现有VLA模型在机器人操作中语义与空间控制耦合导致的对齐困难，本研究提出了SVP-IL，通过引入显式空间视觉提示（SVP）作为先验知识，有效解决了小样本学习下的精度与数据效率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端VLA模型将语义推理与空间控制耦合，导致在数据受限的模仿学习中存在严重的对齐瓶颈，难以实现精确的目标区分。

Method: 提出了SVP-IL架构，通过视觉语言基础模型（VLMs）解析语言指令生成零样本几何掩膜（作为空间视觉提示），并通过轻量级特征融合机制将这些空间先验注入连续动作生成器中，实现语义与空间控制的解耦。

Result: 在少样本（50-100示例）训练中表现优异，模糊语言任务成功率从24.0%大幅提升至39.5%，标准基准测试成功率达到67.8%，并在真实物理环境中验证了其实用性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SVP-IL通过空间视觉提示（SVP）成功解耦了VLA模型中的语义推理与空间控制，显著提升了机器人在样本受限环境下的数据效率与任务成功率，证明了显式空间引导在机器人模仿学习中的有效性。

Abstract: While end-to-end Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models show promise in robotic manipulation, their monolithic paradigm inherently couples semantic reasoning and spatial control. This creates a severe alignment bottleneck, limiting precise target disambiguation in data-constrained imitation learning. To overcome this, we propose SVP-IL, a decoupled architecture that explicitly extracts spatial visual grounding from the action generation loop. By leveraging vision-language foundation models, we parse instructions into zero-shot geometric masks, translating language into explicit Spatial Visual Prompts (SVP). These priors are injected into a continuous action generator via a lightweight direct feature-level fusion mechanism. This integration provides explicit and uncorrupted spatial gradient guidance while ensuring highly stable optimization under low-data regimes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SVP-IL significantly outperforms state-of-the-art VLAs and pure visuomotor baselines. Trained on as few as 50 to 100 demonstrations, SVP-IL improves average success rates on highly ambiguous language-conditioned tasks from 24.0% to 39.5%, achieving 67.8% on standard benchmarks. Real-world robotic experiments further validate its robustness and data efficiency in unstructured physical environments.

</details>


### [67] [Reliability-Asymmetric Spacecraft Autonomy: Co-Designing a Capable Learned GNC Stack with a Verified, Adaptation-Aware Runtime Shield](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25366)
*Alireza Shojaei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AMPLE-GNC 是一种用于深空任务的三层自主架构，通过大模型指令规划、故障自适应控制和基于线性时序逻辑（LTL）的运行时安全护盾，成功在大规模任务自主性与严格的形式化安全保障之间取得了平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深空任务需要同时具备高能力的自主性（能够处理复杂任务）和可认证性（必须保证操作安全）。现有的基于规则的系统（可认证但脆弱）与学习型系统（有能力但难以验证）之间存在鸿沟，需要一种既能利用人工智能处理复杂性，又能通过形式化验证确保安全性的架构。

Method: 1. 设计了包含基础模型指挥官（将自然语言映射为 PDDL+）、约束筛选验证器和故障自适应控制器的三层架构；2. 利用 Kind 2 模型检查器对九种线性时序逻辑（LTL）不变式进行机器验证，构建运行时安全护盾；3. 基于 Basilisk 测试平台，采用语法约束解码进行大模型推理，采用快速电机适应（Rapid Motor Adaptation）技术处理在线故障。

Result: 1. 指挥官模块在语法约束解码下实现了 84% 的规划器可执行动作率；2. 故障自适应控制器在面临多种执行器故障时，恢复成功率远超经典基准（97.8% 及 94.4% vs 55%）；3. 安全护盾在维持 94.5% 自主性的同时，能够有效拦截不安全操作。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 AMPLE-GNC 架构成功平衡了深空任务对自主性（Capability）与可认证性（Certifiability）的需求。通过结合基础模型指挥官、故障自适应控制和形式化验证的运行时护盾，证实了在保证系统安全性的前提下，利用学习型组件提升任务自主能力的可行性。研究还指出，针对鲁棒性的泛化能力主要取决于训练数据的分布广度而非数量。

Abstract: Deep-space missions need onboard autonomy that is both capable and certifiable. Rule-based autonomy is certifiable but brittle, while learned autonomy is capable but hard to verify. We present AMPLE-GNC, a three-tier guidance, navigation, and control stack. Its capability path combines a small foundation-model commander that maps natural language to PDDL+, a constraint-screening verifier, and a fault-adaptive controller. All three are bounded by a runtime shield with nine linear-temporal-logic invariants whose predictor soundness is machine-checked by the Kind 2 model checker. On a 6-DOF Basilisk testbed, we make three contributions. First, we deploy an edge commander. Fine-tuning a pretrained 360M model with grammar-constrained decoding gives a hard output-validity guarantee and 84% planner-executable actions. On a de-leaked test, novel-phrasing generalization is 38% exact and 51% action, rising to 48% exact after phrasing-diversity re-finetuning; we separate syntactic validity from semantic accuracy. Second, we introduce a fault-adaptive controller. Rapid Motor Adaptation infers latent actuator faults online and recovers 97.8% of actuator-sign faults and 94.4% of continuous-gain faults within the training randomization envelope. Fault-unaware PD and from-scratch end-to-end RL both score 0%, while the strongest classical-adaptive baseline reaches 55% on continuous gain. Beyond the envelope, a split-conformant retrain scores 57-67%, and adding 4x more in-regime data worsens performance, showing that randomization breadth, not data volume, drives generalization. Robustness is flat under star-tracker noise to 0.005. Third, we show that a latching safe-hold shield can suppress even a capable controller. A split-conformal recovery-deadline certificate with adaptation-aware engagement reconciles safety and recovery, keeping the controller 94.5% autonomous while still catching non-recovery.

</details>


### [68] [Commerge: Communication-Efficient, Robust, and Fast LiDAR Map Merging Framework for Multi-Robot Coordination in Resource-Constrained Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25386)
*Hogyun Kim,Jiwon Choi,Juwon Kim,Geonmo Yang,Seokhwan Jeong,Hyungtae Lim,Younggun Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Commerge 是一种通信高效的多机器人地图合并框架，通过图论优化筛选关键扫描数据进行交换，在保证地图对齐精度的前提下，将数据传输量减少了近 5000 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人进行 LiDAR 地图合并时，交换海量传感器数据会因通信带宽限制成为性能瓶颈，尤其在资源受限的环境中。需在保证配准精度的前提下，显著降低通信开销。

Method: 提出了一种三阶段级联优化方法：在交换图（Exchange Graph）中，通过筛选具有时序重叠、传输成本平衡以及几何与感知维度最优的关键帧扫描数据，仅交换极少量关键数据而非全量扫描点云，从而实现带宽的极度优化。

Result: 在五个公开数据集和四个自研数据集上验证，数据传输量最高减少至原有的 0.02%（约 5000 倍），在维持与全量数据传输相当的对齐精度下，将通信需求从 GB 级降低到了 MB 级。

Conclusion: Commerge 框架通过基于图论的选择性数据交换，成功解决了多机器人协作地图合并中的通信瓶颈问题，在大幅度降低通信开销的同时，保证了高精度的地图配准，适用于各种受限环境。

Abstract: By maintaining global consistency across robot teams, multi-robot LiDAR map merging enables faster exploration and efficient area coverage. However, map merging requires exchanging massive sensor data between the server and robots, making communication the bottleneck, especially in communication-constrained environments. Therefore, we present Commerge, a communication-efficient map merging framework that achieves bandwidth reduction through graph-theoretic selective data exchange. By doing so, our Commerge reduces inter-robot communication by up to 5,000x while maintaining alignment accuracy. Our key insight is that only a small subset of carefully selected scans is sufficient for robust map merging. We formulate this as a three-stage cascaded optimization problem on an exchange graph, where vertices represent robot keyframes and edges denote candidate inter-robot loops. Through three cascade stages, we select a sequentially overlapped, balanced-transmission-cost, and geometrically-perceptually optimal scan subset that preserves alignment quality while reducing communication. Unlike existing approaches that either transmit whole scans, which require GB-scale data exchange, or employ naive downsampling, our approach exchanges only MB-scale data while achieving comparable alignment accuracy. Extensive evaluation on five public datasets and four in-house datasets covering cave, planetary-analog, indoor, and outdoor campus environments shows up to 99.98% reduction in data exchange (e.g., from 7,000MB to 1.3MB on the HeLiPR dataset), while maintaining alignment performance across embedded to desktop platforms. The supplementary materials are available at https://sparolab.github.io/research/commerge.

</details>


### [69] [Large-Scale Tunnel Air--Ground Collaboration With FLISP: Fast LiDAR-IMU Synchronized Path Planne](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25393)
*Fenghe Guo,Runjie Shen,Chenyang Sun,Junrui Zhang,Quanxi Zhan,Yongchun Wang,Junjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLISP是一种用于水电隧道巡检的无地图机器人协同规划框架，通过统一的LiDAR-IMU传感方案和优化算法，实现了比传统基于地图的方法快数倍至数千倍的实时路径生成，且具备高成功率和运动学可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统水电隧道巡检高度依赖人工，效率低下且存在安全风险；而现有的基于地图的机器人规划方法（如Fast-LIO2+A*或LIO-SAM+RRT*）在长距离线性环境中面临计算开销大、采样不稳定及结构瓶颈等问题。

Method: 提出FLISP（快速激光雷达-惯导同步路径规划器），包含三个核心贡献：1. 统一架构：基于单套安装在UGV上的LiDAR-IMU实现UGV与UAV的协同路径生成；2. 专用求解器：针对UGV采用改进的萤火虫算法进行避障，针对UAV采用动态迭代优化器；3. 分层细化策略：通过分层优化确保运动学的可行性并避免状态估计漂移，无需预先构建全局地图。

Result: 在1.2公里运营隧道的实验中，FLISP表现优异：路径生成延迟仅为~7ms，成功率达100%；相比栅格化方法（Grid-based）速度提升7倍，相比采样方法（Sampling-based）效率提升三个数量级，且成功规避了状态估计漂移和地图构建开销。

Conclusion: FLISP通过无地图（mapless）规划框架有效解决了水电隧道等特征退化环境下的机器人巡检难题，实现了高效、实时且稳定的协同路径规划，为线性基础设施巡检提供了一种可扩展的工业化方案。

Abstract: Hydropower tunnel inspection is critical for infrastructure integrity yet remains inefficient and hazardous using manual methods. We propose FLISP (Fast LiDAR-IMU Synchronized Path Planner), a mapless planning framework for cooperative UGV-UAV inspection. Unlike traditional map-based paradigms, FLISP features three core contributions: (1) a unified architecture where a single UGV-mounted LiDAR-IMU suite drives synchronized path generation for both platforms; (2) platform-specific solvers utilizing an enhanced Firefly Algorithm for UGV obstacle avoidance and a dynamic iterative optimizer for UAV flight; and (3) a hierarchical refinement strategy ensuring kinematic feasibility without state estimation drift. Benchmarks in a 1.2 km operational tunnel demonstrate that FLISP circumvents structural bottlenecks of map-based methods, eliminating map rasterization overhead (Fast-LIO2 + A*) and sampling instability (LIO-SAM + RRT*). FLISP achieves a 100% success rate with ~7 ms latency, representing a 7-fold speedup over grid-based and three-order-of-magnitude improvement over sampling-based baselines. Validated in operational hydropower tunnels, this approach offers a scalable solution for robotic inspection in feature-degraded linear infrastructure. A demonstration video is available at https://youtu.be/Y_ezs1PfLJ4, and the code at https://github.com/ArchibaldGuo/FLISP.git.

</details>


### [70] [MAPL: Multi-Objective Preference Learning for Robot Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25398)
*Xiyue Chen,Muhan Lin,Shuyang Shi,Joseph Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAPL 提出了一种基于大模型的多目标偏好学习框架，将自然语言目标转化为机器人运动奖励，免去了复杂的手工奖励设计，并实现了高性能的运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强化学习中传统奖励函数设计繁琐、难以平衡多目标的问题，旨在消除人工任务特定的奖励工程，利用大模型的语义理解能力进行偏好引导。

Method: 提出 Multi-Objective AI-Informed Preference Learning (MAPL) 框架：1. 使用大模型（LLM）针对不同语义标准（如稳定性、能效等）对轨迹进行独立评估；2. 训练多头偏好评分模型；3. 将多头输出聚合为标量奖励，用于强化学习策略优化。

Result: 在四个四足机器人运动环境中的测试表明，MAPL 仅利用 LLM 生成的偏好，即可获得与专家设计的奖励函数相当甚至更优的策略性能。

Conclusion: MAPL 证明了通过将复杂的运动任务分解为多目标、基于自然语言的偏好学习，可以有效替代人工设计的奖励函数，实现高效且鲁棒的机器人运动控制策略训练。

Abstract: Reward design remains a major bottleneck in reinforcement learning for robot locomotion, where successful policies often depend on carefully tuned, task-specific reward functions. Preference-based reinforcement learning offers an alternative, but existing LLM-based methods typically ask for a single overall judgment between behaviors, making it difficult to capture the multiple competing objectives that underlie high-quality locomotion. We present Multi-Objective AI-Informed Preference Learning (MAPL), a framework that learns locomotion rewards from high-level natural language objectives rather than manually engineered reward equations. MAPL prompts a large language model to compare trajectories independently along semantically meaningful criteria, using generic language descriptions that are terrain-invariant and require little domain expertise. These objective-wise preferences are used to train a multi-head preference scoring model, whose outputs are aggregated to form a scalar reward for policy optimization. Across four quadruped locomotion environments, MAPL trains policies using only LLM-generated preferences and achieves performance comparable to or better than expert-designed rewards, while eliminating task-specific reward engineering.

</details>


### [71] [HEART: Coordination of Heterogeneous Expert Agents for Physically Grounded Robotic Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25404)
*Junho Lee,Seabin Lee,Wonjong Lee,Nayoung Kim,Moonjeong Kang,Changjoo Nam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了HEART框架，通过将复杂指令分解并由不同的专家大模型 Agent 协同处理，在满足计算和通信约束的前提下，实现了更具物理可行性和逻辑鲁棒性的机器人任务规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的机器人规划器通常缺乏对物理空间环境（如可行性、可达性、逻辑顺序）的结构化推理能力，导致生成的规划方案在实际操作中不可行或不完整。

Method: 提出HEART（Heterogeneous multi-LLM framework）框架，核心包括：1. 将复杂指令分解为原子推理任务；2. 将任务分配给具有不同职能的专家Agent；3. 引入令牌预算（Token budget）机制以平衡计算与通信开销；4. 通过约束驱动的规划合成（Constraint-driven plan synthesis）进行可行性验证。

Result: 在多个家庭环境基准测试中，HEART框架在规划成功率和执行质量上均优于单一LLM及基于规则的规划器。

Conclusion: 通过异构多LLM协作（HEART框架）能够显著提升机器人任务规划在物理约束下的成功率、鲁棒性和执行效率，证明了分工协作优于单一LLM模型。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can reason over complex instructions but often fail to satisfy the physical and spatial constraints required for robotic task planning. Recent LLM-based planners directly translate text into action sequences, yet they lack structured reasoning about feasibility, reachability, and logical order, resulting in invalid or incomplete plans. We present a heterogeneous multi-LLM framework that decomposes instructions into atomic reasoning tasks and allocates them to role-specialized expert agents under a token budget for real-world computational and communicational constraints. By combining role-oriented reasoning from heterogeneous agents followed by constraint-driven plan synthesis, HEART validates capability, reachability, and constraint conditions before planning and helps produce physically executable plans while maintaining efficiency. Experiments across different household benchmarks show that HEART consistently improves plan success compared to single-LLM and rule-based planners, demonstrating that heterogeneous LLM collaboration enables robust and scalable robotic task planning under resource constraints.

</details>


### [72] [Delta-Position Estimation-Based IMU Odometry: A Comparison of MLP and Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25454)
*Osman Tokluoglu,Emin Keresteci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究证明，在惯性里程计任务中，使用可学习B样条激活函数的KAN模型比传统MLP更高效且精确，能在大幅压缩参数量的同时降低轨迹累积漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对位置回归方法容易导致较大的恒定误差。研究旨在探讨基于学习的惯性里程计问题，并验证具有可学习B样条激活函数的KAN（柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络）在减少轨迹累积误差方面的有效性。

Method: 该研究使用EuRoC MAV数据集的原始IMU数据进行学习。模型不直接回归绝对位置，而是估计每50ms的增量位移（Δp），并通过数值积分重构轨迹。研究对比了标准多层感知机（MLP）与配备可学习B样条激活函数的柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络（KAN）。

Result: KAN在参数量比MLP少6.9倍（8,444 vs 57,859）的情况下，最终累积漂移误差降低了44%（9.61米 vs 17.23米）。此外，KAN在长期误差累积表现更稳定，P50和P90的累积漂移值均低于MLP。

Conclusion: KAN在惯性里程计任务中展现出比传统MLP更优的性能。基于可学习B样条激活函数的KAN模型，在参数量显著减少的情况下，能更有效地降低轨迹漂移与长期误差累积，证明了其在处理该类动力学问题上的潜力。

Abstract: In this study, the learning-based inertial odometry problem is investigated using raw IMU measurements obtained from the EuRoC MAV benchmark dataset. Instead of absolute position regression-a formulation that may lead to large constant errors-the models are trained to estimate the incremental displacement (Δp) over a fixed 50 ms sliding window, and the full trajectory is reconstructed through numerical integration. A standard Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) is compared with a Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) equipped with learnable B-spline activations. Although KAN has 6.9 times fewer parameters than MLP (8,444 versus 57,859), it produces a 44% lower error in terms of final cumulative drift on the test trajectory (9.61 m versus 17.23 m). In addition, KAN exhibits more stable behavior in terms of long-term error accumulation, with lower P_50 and P_90 cumulative drift values. These findings indicate that learnable B-spline-based activations have the potential to reduce error accumulation in the inertial odometry problem.

</details>


### [73] [Generative AI for Safe and Photorealistic Drone Light Shows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25458)
*Pascal Reinhold,Alexander Gräfe,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SWAN是一个端到端框架，利用生成式AI自动生成从文本描述到无人机编队的动效，有效解决了传统编排的手工成本问题，并实现了大规模、安全的自动化集群飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机灯光秀依赖繁重的手工动画制作，自动化流程匮乏；现有的生成式AI方案难以实现高保真度与平滑动态效果的结合。

Method: 1. 利用生成式AI将文本转换为逼真的参考视频；2. 开发了一种新型自适应点跟踪算法，能在遮挡和拓扑剧变下保持空间一致性；3. 设计了轨迹分配规划器，并集成安全滤波器确保无碰撞飞行。

Result: 实现了从文本提示到大规模、无碰撞无人机编队的高效生成，在标准消费级硬件上完成了对49架真实无人机的密集编队控制。

Conclusion: SWAN成功构建了一个从文本到无人机编队编排的自动化流程，降低了无人机灯光秀的设计门槛，并在大规模模拟（2000架）和实物验证（49架）中展现了出色的扩展性与物理可行性。

Abstract: Drone light shows are redefining aerial entertainment, yet their widespread adoption is bottlenecked by labor-intensive, manual animation. While generative AI promises an automated alternative, current frameworks fail to provide photorealism with fluid, dynamic motion. To address this limitation, we introduce SWAN, an end-to-end pipeline that synthesizes photorealistic, large-scale, and collision-free drone choreographies directly from text prompts. SWAN converts text into realistic reference videos and translates these pixel-space dynamics into physical swarm kinematics using a novel, adaptive point-tracking algorithm. Unlike existing trackers, this method maintains spatial coherence through severe occlusions and rapid topological shifts. A dedicated planner then allocates these trajectories to individual drones, while a subsequent safety filter ensures collision-free execution. We demonstrate scalability by safely orchestrating simulated 2,000-drone formations and validate physical feasibility on a dense real-world swarm of 49 quadcopters, operating everything entirely on standard consumer hardware. Combined, this work demonstrates how generative AI can be leveraged to automate multi-robot choreography design, providing an accessible new framework for drone light shows.

</details>


### [74] [SAGE-Nav: Leveraging LLM Planning and Alignment Fusion for Hierarchical Scene Graph-Guided Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25497)
*Hao Su,Yuehao Huang,Yukai Ma,Yong Liu,Jiajun Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAGE-Nav 是一种结合大语言模型与动态场景图的分层导航框架，通过异步全局规划与实时局部感知的协同，有效解决了智能体在复杂环境中的长程导航与泛化难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单体式导航方法在长程推理能力以及对未知环境的零样本泛化能力上表现较差，难以满足实际机器人高效导航的需求。

Method: 提出了一种分层框架 SAGE-Nav：1. 使用 LLM 进行异步全局语义规划；2. 开发层次化场景图编码器 (HSGE) 以构建结构化空间语义嵌入；3. 设计目标感知融合网络 (GAFN)，通过自适应门控机制实现高频实时感知与结构先验的融合。

Result: 在 i-THOR 和 RoboTHOR 环境中达到了 SOTA 性能，显著提升了导航效率和零样本泛化能力，同时满足了物理机器人部署所需的低控制延迟。

Conclusion: SAGE-Nav 通过结合 LLM 的长期推理能力与动态场景图，有效解决了物体导航任务中的长程推理和零样本泛化难题，在保持低延迟的同时实现了行业领先的导航效率，适用于机器人部署。

Abstract: Object-Goal Navigation (ObjNav) requires embodied agents to autonomously locate specified targets using only egocentric visual observations. Existing monolithic methods struggle with long-horizon reasoning and generalize poorly to novel environments. To address these limitations, we propose SAGE-Nav, a novel hierarchical framework that integrates the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) with dynamic scene graphs. Crucially, it decouples asynchronous global semantic planning from the high-frequency reactive control loop. The LLM serves as a global planner, decomposing abstract instructions into a sequence of semantically grounded waypoints. To translate these plans into dense multi-modal guidance, we design a Hierarchical Scene Graph Encoder (HSGE) that leverages relational graph convolutions to produce structure-aware embeddings preserving both semantic and spatial topology. Furthermore, we develop the Goal-aware Alignment-Fusion Network (GAFN) to dynamically fuse real-time perception with these structural priors. Using an adaptive gating mechanism with an explicit inductive bias, GAFN ensures robust visual-topological alignment for the low-level policy. Extensive evaluations in the i-THOR and RoboTHOR environments demonstrate that SAGE-Nav achieves state-of-the-art performance, delivering substantial gains in navigation efficiency and zero-shot generalization while maintaining the low control latency required for physical robotic deployment.

</details>


### [75] [AISPO: Enhancing Depth Reliability for Robotic Manipulation of Non-Lambertian Objects via Affine-Invariant Shape Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25503)
*Zhiming Chen,Linfang Zheng,Kun Zhang,Hyung Jin Chang,Wei Zhang,Hongyu Yu,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AISPO 是一种专门针对透明和高反光物体设计的深度补全框架，通过引入仿射不变形状先验，在保证深度图结构完整性的前提下，显著提升了机器人在复杂环境下的抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度感知技术在处理透明或高反光等非朗伯体物体时容易产生深度缺失或测量偏差，导致机器人抓取规划失败；现有方法往往过度关注平均测量精度，忽略了深度图的物理合理性和结构完整性。

Method: 提出了一种名为 AISPO 的深度补全框架，核心机制包括：1. 多尺度 RGB-D 特征融合；2. 集成仿射不变形状先验（affine-invariant shape prior）以强化几何一致性；3. 优化目标侧重于预测深度图的物理合理性与结构完整性，而非单纯的均值准确度。

Result: 在基准测试中展现出与业界领先方法相当的性能，具备出色的未知物体与场景泛化能力；真实场景实验表明，该方法在透明物体抓取任务上显著提升了成功率。

Conclusion: AISPO 通过引入仿射不变形状先验和多尺度特征融合，有效解决了非朗伯体（如透明或高反光物体）的深度缺失与伪影问题，提升了深度图的物理合理性与结构完整性，从而显著增强了机器人对复杂物体的抓取鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reliable depth perception is critical for robotic manipulation, especially for non-Lambertian objects such as transparent or highly specular surfaces, where raw depth measurements are often corrupted or missing. These failures frequently propagate to motion planning, resulting in invalid grasp poses and execution errors. We propose AISPO, a depth completion framework that improves depth reliability for manipulation in challenging sensing conditions. AISPO combines multi-scale RGB-D feature fusion with an affine-invariant shape prior to enforce geometric consistency and mitigate catastrophic depth failures. Unlike methods that focus primarily on average depth accuracy, our approach emphasizes physical plausibility and structural integrity of the predicted depth maps. Extensive benchmark evaluations demonstrate competitive performance and strong generalization to unseen objects and novel scenes. Real-world grasping experiments further show that enhanced depth reliability significantly improves manipulation success rates, particularly for transparent objects where many existing methods fail to produce physically usable depth estimates.

</details>


### [76] [GROVE: Grounded Pedestrian Simulation via Natural Language for Interactive Social Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25504)
*Duc Tai Nguyen,Volodymyr Shcherbyna,Anh Do Duc,Zhengcheng Shen,Teham Buiyan,Linh Kästner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GROVE是一个文本驱动的行人仿真框架，通过模块化的Prompt工程和SotA集成，自动生成高保真、可定制的社交交互场景，以优化社交机器人的导航训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的行人仿真系统过于僵化，需要繁琐的人工数据生成，难以快速构建用于社交机器人导航训练的复杂、多样的实际场景。

Method: 提出了GROVE框架，采用模块化设计，针对长周期人类行为、中周期行人导航、短周期社交交互分别集成先进模型（SotA），并根据用户提示词（Prompt）动态选择策略，最终与 Isaac Sim、Gazebo 和 RViz 等仿真器无缝集成。

Result: 成功构建了一个能够根据文本提示生成复杂、多样且符合社交逻辑的行人仿真框架，并在住宅、医院和办公室等多种环境下验证了其高保真度和对导航任务的挑战性。

Conclusion: GROVE 有效解决了行人仿真场景构建的僵化问题，通过文本驱动实现了高保真、可定制的社交机器人导航训练环境，显著弥合了仿真与现实之间的差距（sim2real gap）。

Abstract: Pedestrian simulation is a critical component for training and deploying social robot navigation approaches, yet it remains a largely rigid system that repeatedly requires manual data generation to define even simple scenarios. We propose GROVE, a text-to-scenario pedestrian simulation framework that combines state-of-the-art approaches to produce realistic, socially challenging scenarios for social robot navigation. Our framework allows users to customize one of several common presets (emergency, queuing, normal) or even enter a fully independent prompt to generate a highly customizable pedestrian simulation. Multiple modules separately ensure the realism and soundness of long-horizon human behavior, medium-horizon pedestrian navigation, and short-horizon robot/social interactions. Each module is tuned by the prompt in a way that reflects the user intent across all aspects of pedestrian simulation. By dynamically selecting one of several state-of-the-art (SotA) approaches in our modules based on the scenario, we capture many situational nuances of pedestrian behavior in order to narrow the simulation-to-real (sim2real) gap. The human simulation is directly integrated into Isaac Sim, Gazebo, and RViz simulators for robot deployment in highly social environments. We validate our approach through qualitative comparison against existing pedestrian simulation baselines across scenarios of varying complexity in residential, hospital, and office environments. The result is a high-fidelity pedestrian simulation that challenges social robot navigation with complex, diverse, realistic human behaviors.

</details>


### [77] [ASSCG: Just-Right Gating over Chattering for Fast-Slow LLM Planning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25509)
*Sining Ang,Yuan Chen,Liu Haiyan,Xuanyao Mao,Jason Bao,Xuliang,Bingchuan Sun,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ASSCG 通过基于 RWKV 的轻量级门控机制，利用计算感知 RL 训练，实现了自动驾驶中 LLM 调用的高效化，显著降低延迟并提升了行车性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶快慢规划系统过度依赖人工设计的触发规则，导致大模型（LLM）查询成本高昂且调用不精准（调用时间点不当或调用频率过高）。

Method: 将慢系统调用建模为资源感知的序列决策问题；设计了 Adaptive Slow-System Control Gate (ASSCG) 模块；采用 RWKV 骨干网络进行长时序门控；训练过程包含监督微调 (SFT) 及 GRPO 风格的计算感知强化学习微调。

Result: 在 nuPlan Hard20 评估中，分数提升至 67.28 (+2.28)，端到端推理延迟降低 60%；在 NAVSIM 评估中，PDMS 达到 91.4 (+0.6)，平均速度提升 25%。

Conclusion: 通过计算感知策略，ASSCG 有效平衡了自动驾驶系统中大模型的高推理成本与驾驶表现，证明了无需手动规则即可实现高效的快慢系统调用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can improve autonomous driving planning but are costly to query online, and existing fast-slow planners often rely on hand-designed triggering rules that either over-call the slow system or call it at the wrong times. We formulate slow-system invocation as a resource-aware sequential decision problem and propose the Adaptive Slow-System Control Gate (ASSCG), which makes frame-level Query/Cache/Drop decisions to refresh, reuse, or suppress slow guidance. ASSCG uses an RWKV backbone for efficient long-horizon gating and is trained with supervised fine-tuning followed by GRPO-style compute-aware reinforcement fine-tuning. We apply ASSCG to two different fast-slow architectures: (i) AsyncDriver on nuPlan Hard20 closed-loop evaluation, where ASSCG improves score to 67.28 (+2.28) while reducing average end-to-end inference latency by 60%; and (ii) a RecogDrive-based dual system that we build by replacing its original VLM-2B module with a lightweight ViT-based fast planner and adding an LLM slow planner, evaluated on NAVSIM, where ASSCG achieves 91.4 PDMS (+0.6) and increases average speed by 25%. The project page, including video visualizations and additional results, is available at https://williamxuanyu.github.io/asscg/.

</details>


### [78] [One Body, Two Minds: Variable Autonomy Approach for a Co-embodied Robotic Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25575)
*Piotr Koczy,Yuchong Zhang,Danica Kragic,Michael C. Welle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种“共身（co-embodiment）”可穿戴机器人手，通过结合基于扩散策略的自主抓取与用户主导的执行控制，在单一机体中实现了灵活的人机变分自主协作，显著提升了任务效率与用户协作体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辅助机器人面临“完全自主”失去人机控制权与“完全受控”导致用户认知负担过重的博弈。现有共享自主方案多基于分离实体，缺乏紧密的人机物理耦合体验。因此，作者旨在探索在单一物理实体中实现人机协作的“一体双智”范式。

Method: 提出了一种基于“共身”的可穿戴机械手。采用基于学习演示（LfD）的视觉运动扩散策略（visuomotor diffusion policy）实现物体抓取的自主化；在操作阶段引入基于头部手势的免提控制；并设计了实时“否决权”机制，在不同任务阶段动态调整人机自主权权重。

Result: 通过44名参与者的双手动任务验证，完成时间缩短了23.3%，任务成功率达93.6%，且用户对系统的整体印象（5.70/7）和日常使用意愿（5.52/7）评分较高，证明了该范式的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功验证了“共身（co-embodiment）”与“变分自主性（variable autonomy）”相结合的范式，证明这种“一体双智”的设计能显著提升人机协作任务的效率与用户接受度，为辅助机器人领域提供了一种平衡用户控制权与自主执行能力的新路径。

Abstract: Assistive robotic systems face a fundamental trade-off: fully autonomous systems lack user agency, while fully user-controlled systems demand continuous cognitive effort. Existing shared autonomy approaches blend human and robot commands but are mostly deployed in separate physical bodies. We introduce co-embodiment with variable autonomy, where human and robot share a single physical body and operate at different autonomy levels across task phases, from mutual autonomy during object search and grasping to human-dominant control during actuation.
  We present a co-embodied, wearable robotic hand that has its own ``mind'' and operates with variable autonomy levels. A learning-from-demonstration visuomotor diffusion policy enables autonomous grasping when the user positions the hand near known objects. Once grasped, the system signals completion and the human can actuate the grasped tool (drill, spray bottle, infrared thermometer, lighter, and ice-cream scoop) via hands-free head gestures. The human retains veto authority at all times through a release gesture that returns the system to the initial phase. Unlike blended autonomy, where control is continuously negotiated, our co-embodied approach consists of variable autonomy from full human control to full independent actions while maintaining physical coupling, realizing a one body, two minds paradigm.
  In a user study with 44 participants performing five bimanual tasks, users rapidly adapted to this ``two minds'' paradigm: completion times improved by 23.3% across trials ($p < 0.001$, Cohen's $d = 0.94$), the best-performing policy variant reached a 93.6% task success rate, and acceptance ratings were high (5.70/7 overall impression, 5.52/7 daily use willingness). This work establishes co-embodiment with variable autonomy as a viable approach for assistive robotics, enabling human-robot collaboration through co-embodiment.

</details>


### [79] [WOLF-VLA: Whole-Body Humanoid Optimal Locomotion Framework for Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25591)
*Melya Boukheddimi,Omar Adjali,Daniel Sontag,Frank Kirchner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了WOLF-VLA框架，通过融合最优控制合成数据与多模态训练，实现了人形机器人基于自然语言指令的鲁棒全身运动控制，并开源了相关数据集、模型与仿真基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在人形机器人全身、复杂接触环境下的运动控制研究不足，主要受限于数据匮乏、缺乏动力学一致性演示以及难以在学习中兼顾最优性与安全性。

Method: 提出WOLF-VLA框架，集成全身最优控制（OC）合成大规模动态一致的运动轨迹数据；基于自然语言指令、自我中心视觉及关节轨迹数据训练VLA策略网络。

Result: 建立了包含6大类任务的全人形机器人 locomotion 数据集；模型表现出强大的推理能力和对初始条件变化的鲁棒性，并在多个环境设置中展现了较强的任务完成能力。

Conclusion: WOLF-VLA通过结合优化控制与大规模多模态数据训练，有效解决了人型机器人动态稳定运动的指令驱动控制问题，为全身机器人运动控制提供了可扩展、鲁棒的基准框架。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently demonstrated strong generalization in robotic manipulation, yet their applicability to whole-body, contact-rich humanoid locomotion remains severely underexplored due to data scarcity, the absence of dynamically consistent demonstrations, and the difficulty of encoding optimality and safety in learning-based pipelines. This work introduces a unified framework WOLF-VLA that integrates whole-body optimal-control (OC) motion synthesis with large-scale multi-modal dataset to train VLAs capable of generating humanoid locomotion policies directly from natural-language instructions. We construct a comprehensive dataset of dynamically feasible humanoid trajectories across six locomotion-related task families, each parameterized by environmental variations, object colors, placements, and visual distractors. We train a VLA model using the collected joint trajectories, ego-centric visual observations and natural language instruction, yielding a policy that exhibits strong reasoning and robustness to initial-condition variability, and competitive performance across several tasks and environment settings. A systematic ablation study demonstrates the impact of each modality on the model performance. The full dataset, model checkpoints, and benchmarking simulation suite will be openly released, establishing a reproducible dynamically consistent benchmark for whole-body humanoid locomotion rich VLA control and enabling future research in scalable transfer of instruction-driven locomotion policies.

</details>


### [80] [1000 Rallies: An Event-Camera Dataset and Real-Time Learned Ball-State Estimation for Robotic Table Tennis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25620)
*Raphaela Kreiser,Asude Aydin,Yin Bi,Claudio Fanconi,Peter Dürr,Naoya Takahashi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人乒乓球高速运动的感知挑战，本文发布了首个大规模事件相机数据集，并开发了基于CNN的事件驱动运动状态估计模型，有效降低了落点预测误差，成功实现了基于事件相机的实时机器人乒乓球对打系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于帧的相机在乒乓球这类高速运动场景中面临“帧率与延迟/计算成本”的权衡困境；事件相机具备微秒级时间分辨率和无运动模糊特性，但目前缺乏大规模相关数据集来支撑该领域的研究。

Method: 1. 构建数据集：涵盖1000多个不同水平（业余至专业）的乒乓球回合，同步记录事件流与14台200FPS高速相机数据，生成1kHz的球体位置、速度和旋转伪真值；2. 模型开发：训练能够抵抗背景干扰的卷积神经网络（CNN），从事件流中联合估计球体在图像平面上的位置和速度；3. 系统集成：将预测的速度作为卡尔曼滤波的额外观测值，并接入Stäubli机械臂实现感知-动作闭环。

Result: 1. 建立了首个大规模乒乓球事件相机数据集；2. 通过结合事件驱动的速度预测，将落点预测误差降低了36%；3. 成功实现了首个基于事件感知的实时人机乒乓球对打系统。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建了首个乒乓球领域的大规模事件相机数据集，并验证了利用事件相机进行感知能够显著提升机器人乒乓球的实时轨迹预测精度，最终实现了基于事件感知的实时人机乒乓球对打，为高速运动场景下的实时机器人感知提供了新范式。

Abstract: Robotic table tennis has emerged as a compelling benchmark for real-time robotic perception due to its fast ball dynamics and stringent timing requirements. Accurate, high-frequency, and low-latency ball state estimation is critical for reliable trajectory prediction and timely control. Traditional frame-based cameras face an inherent trade-off: low frame rates leave temporal blind spots that miss fast-moving objects and high frame rates raise data and computational cost. Event cameras instead offer microsecond temporal resolution and, under sufficient illumination, remain largely free of motion blur even at high ball speeds. However, the community lacks large-scale datasets to develop and benchmark event-based perception in realistic sports scenarios. We address this gap by introducing the first large-scale event-camera dataset for table tennis, comprising over 1000 rallies from a diverse group of players ranging from amateurs to elite-level athletes. Each recording captures the event stream alongside 14 synchronized high-speed frame-based cameras at 200 FPS, which we use to produce 1 kHz pseudo ground-truth labels for ball position, velocity, and spin. Building on this dataset, we train a convolutional neural network robust to background player motion that jointly estimates the ball's position and velocity in the image-plane from events. Treating the predicted velocity as an additional measurement in the Kalman filter reduces bounce-point prediction error by 36% relative to a position-only baseline. Finally, we close the perception-action loop by integrating the event-based system with a Stäubli robotic arm, enabling the first real-time human-robot table tennis rallies driven by event-based perception.

</details>


### [81] [Event-Adaptive Motion Planning with Distilled Vision-Language Model in Safety-Critical Situations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25629)
*Zhenwei Huang,Changsheng You,Shuai Wang,Chao Zhou,Wei Xu,Yi Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种事件驱动的机器人导航框架EAMP，通过PC-SET触发器仅在必要时调用VLM进行推理，结合SMPC完成动态路径修正，解决了大模型在实时控制中的高延迟与安全导航难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键场景中，机器人导航面临的主要挑战不是未见过的物体，而是动态主体不可预测的行为。现有大模型（VLM）计算延迟高，无法直接放入实时控制环中，导致性能不稳定。

Method: 提出事件自适应运动规划（EAMP）框架；包含三个核心模块：1. PC-SET（提示可配置语义事件触发器）：持续监测行为异常；2. 蒸馏SemNav-VLM：仅在触发时进行推理，生成策略级决策；3. SMPC（语义模型预测控制）：将高层决策转化为控制目标和几何约束。

Result: 实验证明，EAMP相较于现有基线算法，在安全关键的物流场景中，显著提高了动态安全裕度，同时保持了高效的实时响应能力。

Conclusion: EAMP框架通过将高层逻辑推理与低层控制通过事件驱动机制解耦，在保障实时性的前提下，显著提升了机器人应对安全关键场景下动态障碍物异常行为的能力。

Abstract: Robot navigation in safety-critical scenarios faces significant challenges from unforeseen semantic events, where collisions arise primarily from the unpredictable behaviors of dynamic agents rather than unseen objects. While large vision-language models (VLMs) offer remarkable capabilities in commonsense reasoning, frequently invoking them within the continuous control loop introduces severe computational latency, fundamentally destabilizing physical execution. To address these challenges, we propose event-adaptive motion planning (EAMP), an efficient framework for VLM-based robot navigation. Specifically, a prompt-configurable semantic event trigger (PC-SET) selectively activates semantic intervention by continuously monitoring short temporal clips for behavioral anomalies. Upon triggering, an event-triggered distilled SemNav-VLM, fine-tuned via physically verified semantic distillation, maps detected anomalies into discrete strategy-level decisions. Subsequently, a semantic model predictive control (SMPC) module translates these strategies into dynamic reconfigurations of optimization objectives and geometric references. Extensive experiments in safety-critical logistics scenarios demonstrate that EAMP effectively aligns high-level reasoning with low-level control, significantly improving dynamic safety margins over existing baselines while preserving real-time efficiency.

</details>


### [82] [Calousel: Extrinsic Calibration of Non-overlapping Multi-camera Systems from Pure Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25646)
*Gwanhyeong Song,Chaehyeon Song,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅需一个静态标定板配合纯旋转运动的非重叠视场相机外参标定方案，通过全局优化解决了传统方法的易用性与精度矛盾，适用于实地快速部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统目标物标定方法设置复杂且开销大，而基于运动的方法存在漂移、尺度模糊和退化问题，旨在解决高精度与高易用性难以兼顾的难题。

Method: 提出了一种基于潜在转盘坐标系（latent turntable frame）和 SE(3) 上 3D 误差的全局优化框架，通过时间序列对同一标定板的观测，将非重叠视场相机的坐标系关联起来。

Result: 在受控相机平台和异构相机车载平台上进行了验证，证明了该方法对非理想转盘运动的鲁棒性，且精度与现有方法具有竞争优势。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种利用单一静态标定板进行纯旋转运动的非重叠视场多相机系统外参标定方法，在无需高精度专用硬件的情况下实现了高精度标定，具有极强的实地部署实用性。

Abstract: Extrinsic calibration of multi-camera systems with non-overlapping FOVs has been a challenging problem in the robotics literature. Conventional target-based methods impose substantial target setup overhead, either deploying large calibration targets or requiring pre-measured multi-target poses. Motion-based approaches instead suffer from drift error, scale ambiguity, and motion degeneracy. Securing both accuracy and usability, we propose a novel calibration method that leverages pure rotational motion, requiring only a single static calibration board. The key idea is to make all cameras sequentially observe the same target under a shared geometric reference, even without overlapping views. To integrate these time-separated observations, we formulate the problem using a latent turntable frame and a 3D error on SE(3) within a global optimization framework. We validate the proposed method on both a controlled camera rig and a full-scale vehicle platform with heterogeneous cameras, and analyze robustness under non-ideal turntable motion. Extensive experiments show that our approach maintains competitive accuracy without specialized precision hardware, proving its strong suitability for realistic on-site deployments. Our code is publicly available here.

</details>


### [83] [Learning to Adapt: Reptile-D-Learning for Robust and Efficient Control Under Parametric Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25659)
*Haipeng Cao,Zhaolong Shen,Quan Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Reptile-D-learning 框架，利用元学习优化 Lyapunov 控制器，解决了模型参数变动导致的稳定性失效和泛化难题，实现了对未知系统的高效适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的 Lyapunov 控制（LLC）高度依赖精确模型，参数变动会导致稳定性失效及高额重训练成本；而现有的 D-learning 方法在单一任务动力学中受限，难以应对参数的大幅偏移。

Method: 结合 D-learning（无需显式动力学模型的 Lyapunov 导数估计方法）与 Reptile 元学习算法，通过元学习优化 Lyapunov 网络初始化，从而在多个不同参数的系统任务间共享动力学结构知识，实现对新任务的快速适应。

Result: 实验表明，Reptile-D-learning 在多个非线性控制系统中，对比单一模型具有更好的泛化性能，能够对未见的参数配置实现更快速的适应。

Conclusion: Reptile-D-learning 框架有效地解决了基于学习的 Lyapunov 控制中对模型依赖过强及泛化能力不足的问题，通过元学习实现了对不同参数系统动力学结构的捕捉，显著提升了控制器在未见参数配置下的适应性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Learning-based Lyapunov Control (LLC) provides formal stability guarantees for nonlinear systems, but its validity relies heavily on accurate system models. Parameter variations and uncertainties may invalidate stability constraints, leading to costly retraining. Although D-learning can estimate Lyapunov derivatives without relying on explicit dynamics models, it remains limited by single-task dynamics and degrades under large parameter shifts. We propose Reptile-D-learning, a framework that leverages the Reptile meta-learning algorithm to capture shared dynamical structures across systems with different parameters, thereby learning a generalizable Lyapunov network initialization and a high-performance controller. Experiments on multiple nonlinear control systems demonstrate that Reptile-D-learning significantly improves both generalization and rapid adaptation to unseen parameter configurations.

</details>


### [84] [Power-Budgeted Underwater Vehicle Control via Constrained Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25680)
*Yinuo Wang,Gavin Tao,Yuze Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）的强化学习控制方法，通过直接设定物理单位的能耗预算，有效解决了水下航行器在保证任务精度前提下难以高效节能的调参难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习在水下航行器控制中往往只关注任务精度，导致严重的能量浪费。现有的能量惩罚项机制由于权重因子缺乏物理意义，导致参数调整困难且灵活性差。

Method: 将节能控制建模为受限马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），并采用PPO-Lagrangian算法进行求解。该方法允许用户直接设定物理功耗目标（预算），通过在线更新单个对偶变量来满足约束。

Result: 在MarineGym模拟器的12种测试场景中，该方法均实现了最低功耗，相较于纯任务基准功耗降低了14%至64.9%，同时在绝大多数场景下实现了最平滑的操纵，且保持了任务精度。

Conclusion: 将能耗显式定义为约束条件的方法，提供了一种无需针对特定任务或设备进行调参的节能控制策略，不仅能有效降低功耗，还能保证任务执行的准确性。

Abstract: Underwater vehicles operate from a fixed onboard energy budget that propulsion rapidly depletes, so a controller that completes its task while drawing less thruster power directly extends mission range and endurance. Reinforcement learning yields capable model-free controllers for station-keeping and trajectory tracking, but optimizing task accuracy alone drives the policy toward oscillatory, energy-wasting actuation. The established remedy subtracts an energy penalty from the reward, yet this sets the task-power trade-off through a single weight with no physical units: a target power level cannot be specified, the weight must be re-tuned for every vehicle and task, and a mismatched weight can even raise power. This paper instead formulates energy-efficient underwater control as a constrained Markov decision process in which average thruster power is subject to an explicit budget, solved with a PPO-Lagrangian algorithm. The power level is set by declaring a budget in physical units, and a single dual variable is updated online to meet it for each vehicle and task, without manual weight search. Across three vehicles and four tasks in the MarineGym simulator, the energy-constrained policy draws the least power in all twelve settings, reducing it by 14--65\% (up to 64.9\%) over a task-only baseline and below an energy-reward baseline everywhere, while remaining the smoothest in ten settings and preserving task accuracy except in one deliberately power-limited regime. Imposing energy as an explicit constraint thus offers a tuning-free route to energy-efficient underwater control that needs no per-vehicle, per-task weight search.

</details>


### [85] [SA-LIVO: Efficient LiDAR-Inertial-Visual Odometry with Subspace-Aware Degeneracy Handling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25699)
*Yinong Cao,Xin He,Yuwei Chen,Shijie Liu,Chunlai Li,Jianyu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对现有 LIVO 系统在传感器退化时无法精细化分配信息约束的问题，提出 SA-LIVO，通过子空间感知融合技术在特征方向实现自适应的信息权重管理与计算高效的 InEKF 优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的紧耦合 LiDAR-视觉-惯性里程计（LIVO）多基于模态层级的故障处理，无法解决信息矩阵中方向相关的退化问题，导致在特定方向缺乏约束或视觉补偿弥散，无法实现最优的联合约束。

Method: 提出 SA-LIVO 系统：1. 子空间感知信息融合（SAIF）框架：通过对联合信息矩阵进行特征分解，在各特征方向应用线性钳位软门控，实现方向选择性融合；2. 优化流程：将 LiDAR 和视觉残差统一在单个不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（InEKF）循环中，利用光度雅可比矩阵复用技术降低计算开销。

Result: 在 HILTI'22 等三个基准测试集的 29 个序列及并发退化场景中表现优异；在笔记本 CPU 上平均每帧处理耗时 12.3 ms，嵌入式 ARM 开发板上为 26.8 ms，内存消耗降低至竞品的 3.6-6.3 倍，且在竞争系统失效时仍能保持系统鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SA-LIVO 通过子空间感知信息融合（SAIF），实现了在方向层面更精确的传感器融合，解决了 LiDAR 和视觉传感器在特定维度失效时的耦合问题，显著提升了位姿估计的鲁棒性和计算效率。

Abstract: Tightly coupled LiDAR-visual-inertial odometry (LIVO) fuses precise geometric depth with complementary visual measurements, yet its exteroceptive sensors face independent failure modes: LiDAR degenerates when scan geometry is under-constrained, while visual measurements degrade under adverse illumination or texture absence. Existing countermeasures, including binary degeneracy detection, covariance inflation, and scene-level quality gating, operate at the modality level and leave the direction-dependent structure of the joint information matrix unaddressed. Consequently, visual residuals enter pose directions where LiDAR is well-constrained, while in deficient directions visual compensation disperses across the full state space rather than concentrating where needed. We propose SA-LIVO, a LiDAR-inertial-visual odometry system addressing these limitations through direction-selective fusion and information-efficient processing. The Subspace-Aware Information Fusion (SAIF) framework eigendecomposes the joint LiDAR-visual information matrix and applies a linear-clamp soft gate per eigendirection, attenuating degenerate directions while preserving observable ones at full strength. LiDAR and visual residuals are then jointly optimized in one InEKF loop at a shared linearization point. Since visual information contributes only where LiDAR is deficient, photometric Jacobians are assembled once before the loop and reused across iterations, avoiding the per-iteration cost of conventional iterated filters. Experiments on 29 sequences from three benchmarks (HILTI'22, New College, Oxford Spires) and concurrent-degradation scenarios show accuracy competitive with the strongest baselines and bounded drift where competing systems diverge. SA-LIVO averages 12.3 ms per frame on a laptop CPU and 26.8 ms on an embedded ARM board without GPU, with 3.6-6.3x lower peak memory. The code will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [86] [Learning Asynchronous Upper-body Task-space Trajectory Tracking Policy for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25706)
*Yumeng Liu,Dongqi Wang,Jiyu Yu,Yijun Fan,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于人形机器人的异步上肢任务空间跟踪框架，通过蒸馏、滑动窗口奖励训练及MPC引导，成功解决了规划与控制之间的频率异步及轨迹稀疏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高层人形机器人规划器通常输出稀疏、低频的任务空间轨迹，而全机身控制器运行频率较高，导致规划与执行之间存在时序不同步，且全机身控制缺乏结构完整性。

Method: 1. 采用教师-学生蒸馏技术初始化学生策略；2. 策略以完整的缓存未来轨迹和执行时间索引为条件；3. 使用滑动窗口全局奖励进行训练以减轻帧漂移；4. 结合MPC模块对稀疏参考轨迹进行补全，生成浮动基座与上肢引导；5. 引入动作级与正运动学（FK）级自引导机制以约束策略漂移。

Result: 在仿真和Unitree G1硬件实验中，该框架在低更新率环境下表现出更优的跟踪效果，优于同步和解耦基准方法，且对分布外（OOD）运动的适应性更强、更安全。

Conclusion: 所提出的异步上肢任务空间跟踪框架有效解决了高层规划与底层控制之间的时序不同步和结构不完整问题，在低更新率下显著提升了人形机器人的跟踪性能、鲁棒性和安全性。

Abstract: High-level humanoid planners often output sparse task-space, low-rate trajectories, whereas whole-body controllers run at high frequency. This creates temporal asynchrony between the planning and execution, and structural incompleteness for full-body control. We propose an asynchronous upper body task-space tracking framework for humanoids. A student policy is initialized by teacher-student distillation, conditioned on the full cached future trajectory and an execution-time index, and trained with a sliding-window global reward to reduce frame drift without explicit frame estimation. For task-specific post-training, an MPC module completes sparse references into floating-base and upper-body guidance, while action- and FK level self-guidance constrain policy drift. Simulation and Unitree G1 hardware experiments show improved tracking under low update rates, stronger performance than synchronous and decoupled baselines, and safer adaptation to out-of-distribution motions.

</details>


### [87] [Stage-Aware and Roughness-Constrained Diffusion Policy for Multi-Stage Robotic Polishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25754)
*Shuai Ke,Jiexin Zhang,Huan Zhao,Zhiao Wei,Yikun Guo,Tiange Wu,Guoqiang Guo,Haoyuan Zhou,Jie Pan,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人抛光任务，提出 SRDP 模型，通过阶段感知与粗糙度约束技术，解决了多阶段工艺下的参数耦合与长程控制难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人模仿学习方法难以应对工业抛光中复杂的多阶段转换、长程依赖关系以及工艺参数（如转速、进给、压力）的高度耦合与精密调控需求。

Method: 提出一种阶段感知与粗糙度约束的扩散策略（SRDP）：1. 通过多模态观测推断过程阶段后验，无需外部标签即可实现阶段一致的动作生成；2. 加入面向粗糙度的约束扩散采样机制，在预设转速下实时调节进给速度与接触力，确保物理可行性与加工一致性。

Result: 通过航天舱壁涂层和内腔结构表面抛光实验验证，SRDP 相比现有基准方法，在阶段转换稳定性、参数一致性及最终表面粗糙度指标上均表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: SRDP 框架有效解决了机器人抛光中的长程依赖、阶段转换不确定性及工艺参数耦合调节难题，显著提升了抛光过程的稳定性及最终表面质量，在航天及精密零件加工领域展现出良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Polishing is a critical finishing process in high-end manufacturing fields such as aerospace, where surface quality directly affects the service performance and reliability of components. Robotic imitation learning provides a flexible solution for such tasks, but current methods remain limited in industrial polishing because of long-horizon dependencies, uncertain stage transitions, and the difficulty of modeling and regulating coupled process parameters. To address these issues, this paper proposes a Stage-Aware and Roughness-Constrained Diffusion Policy (SRDP) for robotic polishing. SRDP infers the process-stage posterior from multimodal observation histories and uses it to condition the shared reverse denoising process, enabling stage-consistent action generation without external stage labels during execution. Furthermore, a roughness-oriented process-constrained diffusion sampling method is incorporated to generate constrained feed speed and normal contact force under stage-wise preset spindle speeds, thereby improving process consistency and physical feasibility. Systematic experiments are conducted on two representative scenarios, namely spacecraft cabin coating-surface polishing and inner-cavity structural surface finishing. Comparisons with advanced baselines, ablation studies, and real-robot validations comprehensively evaluate the proposed method. The results show that SRD improves stage-transition stability, process-parameter consistency, and final surface quality across different polishing scenarios.

</details>


### [88] [StairMaster: Learning to Conquer Risky Hollow Stairs for Agile Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25765)
*Xincheng Tang,Youhan Xie,Zhengjie Shu,Wanyu Li,Lai Jiang,Wenkang Hu,Yitong Li,Ruigang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StairMaster 是一个针对四足机器人镂空楼梯攀爬的深度强化学习框架，通过融合注意力机制、时空记忆与高保真感知建模，成功在零样本迁移下实现了 55° 超陡楼梯的稳定攀爬。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在镂空楼梯环境下常面临腿部受困、深度数据缺失严重及高频噪声干扰，导致运动规划困难。现有方法难以处理此类极端的不连续地形。

Method: 提出了一个三阶段强化学习框架：1. 引入交叉注意力机制（Cross-Attention）捕捉结构化特征；2. 设计空间感知循环单元（SRU）维持 spatio-temporal 记忆；3. 构建高保真 sim-to-real 深度传感器建模管线以解决噪声与感知盲区；4. 采用 3D 航点引导的主动感知奖励与基于运动学的惩罚机制。

Result: 在 Unitree Go2 机器人上成功验证，能够实现零样本迁移（zero-shot transfer），突破了最高 55° 的镂空楼梯攀爬限制。

Conclusion: StairMaster是首个通过强化学习在真实世界环境中实现高坡度（达55°）镂空楼梯攀爬的方法，证明了通过结构化注意力、时空记忆和高保真感知建模，能够有效解决四足机器人感知稀疏与噪声问题。

Abstract: Climbing hollow stairs remains a challenging problem for quadruped robots due to the high risk of leg trapping, severe depth sparsity, and high-frequency depth-sensing noise. In this paper, we propose StairMaster, a novel three-stage reinforcement learning framework for stable locomotion on such extreme discontinuous terrains. Our architecture integrates a Cross-Attention mechanism to extract structural features from noisy depth data, alongside a Spatial-aware Recurrent Unit (SRU) that maintains robust spatio-temporal memory to mitigate perception blind spots. To bridge the sim-to-real gap in depth perception, we propose a high-fidelity sim-to-real depth sensor modeling pipeline that faithfully replicates real-world sensor artifacts. Additionally, we employ a 3D waypoint-guided active perception reward for proactive sensing, alongside hollow gap kinematic and stair edge penalties to ensure precise foothold placement. We successfully deployed StairMaster on a Unitree Go2 robot, demonstrating its ability to conquer hollow stairs with an unprecedented incline of up to 55$^\circ$ through zero-shot transfer. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first RL-based policy to achieve such steep hollow stair climbing in real-world environments.
  Project Website: https://sivan666666.github.io/StairMaster/.

</details>


### [89] [MIL-LC: A Robust Magnetometer-Inertial-LiDAR Fusion Multimodal Localization Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25796)
*Qiyang Lyu,Zhenyu Wu,Wei Wang,Hongming Shen,Danwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对 AMR 在挑战性复杂环境下的定位难题，本文提出了 MIL-LC 框架，通过融合磁力计、IMU 和 LiDAR 数据，克服了单一传感器受环境退化影响的限制，无需额外基础设施即可实现高鲁棒性定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单模态定位受限于环境特征；现有的多模态融合多依赖几何/纹理特征或特定基础设施（信标），缺乏灵活性；尽管基于环境磁场（AMF）的定位具有潜力，但目前多集中于行人场景，缺乏针对自主移动机器人（AMR）的可靠工程方案。

Method: 提出了一种名为 MIL-LC 的多模态融合定位框架，该框架集成了磁力计（Magnetometer）、惯性测量单元（IMU）和激光雷达（LiDAR），并设计了专门定制的传感器套件来应对环境退化及长期环境变化带来的挑战。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架在仿真环境和真实场景中均展现出了鲁棒且精确的定位能力，特别是在 LiDAR 发生几何退化或磁场地图发生长期变化时，仍能保持稳定的定位性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 MIL-LC 框架有效解决了在几何重复、纹理缺失或 GNSS-denied 环境下 AMR 的鲁棒定位问题，证明了磁场与 LiDAR/IMU 融合在移动机器人应用中的可行性与卓越性能。

Abstract: Localization in challenging environments, such as GNSS-denied, geometrically repetitive, or textureless scenes commonly found in offices, hotels, and underground parking facilities, remains an open problem for reliable autonomous mobile robot (AMR) deployment. Single-modality localization methods are inherently limited by the constraints of individual sensors. Although multimodal fusion frameworks have shown improved robustness, most existing approaches still rely heavily on geometric or texture features, or on infrastructure-based beacons, which increase installation and maintenance costs while reducing deployment flexibility. Recently, ambient magnetic field (AMF)-based localization has attracted growing attention because it does not depend on geometric or texture features, nor does it require additional infrastructure, making it a promising complementary modality for AMR localization. However, existing studies have only explored such fusion in pedestrian scenarios using smartphone-mounted sensor suites, and practical solutions for AMR systems remain largely unexplored. To address this gap, this article proposes a magnetometer-inertial-LiDAR fused multimodal localization framework with a custom-designed sensor suite, termed MIL-LC, which provides reliable localization even when LiDAR suffers from geometric degeneration or when the magnetic map changes during long-term deployment. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that the proposed MIL-LC framework achieves robust and accurate localization performance.

</details>


### [90] [Beyond a Shadow of a Doubt: Close Proximity Geometry Reconstruction Using FMCW Radar Shadow Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25829)
*Felix de Trogoff du Boisguezennec,Benjamin Ramtoula,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种利用车载雷达底盘形成的几何阴影作为线索的新技术，成功从二维雷达数据中推断出细长物体的3D倾斜角度，为雷达在3D场景重建中的应用提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相机和LiDAR在恶劣天气和光照条件下性能下降，而毫米波雷达不受这些环境影响。然而，传统雷达由于缺乏高度信息导致“仰角坍塌”（elevation collapse），限制了其进行3D几何推理的能力，因此需要开发利用雷达特有几何特性的新方法来获取3D信息。

Method: 利用毫米波FMCW雷达扫描时由于车身底盘遮挡形成的几何阴影，通过建立雷达回波边界与物体张角之间的解析闭式映射（closed-form mapping），实现对细长垂直物体倾斜角度的推断。

Result: 通过仿真和基于Navtech CTS350-X雷达的实验验证，证明该方法能够有效估计物体的倾斜度。实验结果显示，该方法在实际条件下的主要性能瓶颈在于雷达扫描中物体的分割精度。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一种利用车载雷达底盘阴影作为几何线索的方法，证明了在无需对场景进行复杂假设的情况下，可以通过分析雷达回波边界恢复细长垂直物体的3D倾斜角度。该研究扩展了二维旋转雷达在3D场景重建中的应用能力。

Abstract: Reliable perception in adverse conditions remains challenging for autonomous systems, as cameras and LiDAR degrade in poor lighting or weather. Millimetre-wave FMCW radar is robust to such conditions, but its elevation collapse limits geometric reasoning. We observe that vehicle chassis occlude radar rays and form a distinctive geometric shadow, and its consistency can enable us to infer useful information about objects whose returns intersect this shadow. Motivated by this observation, we propose a method to recover the 3D, in-plane inclination of nearby slender vertical objects from this cue. The object inclination is retrieved without assumptions about the wider scene, but through an analytical, closed-form mapping between its radar return boundaries and the opening angle. Validation in simulation and experimentation on a Navtech CTS350-X radar shows that inclinations can be estimated under practical conditions, with segmentation of the object in the radar scan emerging as the main bottleneck. This work highlights chassis shadows as a novel geometric cue, extending the role of 2D rotating radar beyond localisation and toward 3D scene reconstruction.

</details>


### [91] [TacVerse: A Multi-Sensor Dataset and Benchmark for Cross-Sensor Vision-Based Tactile Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25877)
*Lan Wei,Gurmeher Khurana,Sirine Bhouri,Wenhao Hong,Zeyuan Xin,Qingzheng Cong,Wen Fan,Yanzheng Xiang,Dandan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacVerse 是首个针对视觉触觉传感器的跨传感器数据集与基准，旨在解决多传感器硬件环境下的泛化难题，实验证实了现有迁移学习方法的局限性及自监督学习（MAE）的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉触觉传感器（VBTS）缺乏对跨不同传感器设计泛化能力的深入理解，且缺乏相应的基准测试工具，限制了触觉感知技术在多硬件环境下的广泛应用。

Method: 构建了包含7种视觉触觉传感器、106,800张触觉图像的 TacVerse 数据集；针对形状分类、纹理分类和力回归三个任务，评估了传感器内训练、零样本跨传感器迁移、少样本适应以及 MAE 自监督预训练的性能表现。

Result: 实验发现，直接的零样本跨传感器迁移会导致性能大幅下降，其中纹理分类和力回归对传感器差异更为敏感；少样本适应可提升力回归性能，但无法完全达到传感器内训练的水平；MAE 预训练被证实对于跨任务和跨传感器的特征提取表现出最强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TacVerse 作为一个受控的测试平台，揭示了传感器差异对视觉触觉感知的显著影响。研究表明，虽然零样本跨传感器迁移效果有限，但通过少样本适应和自监督预训练（如 MAE）可以有效缓解感知偏差，为提升触觉感知系统的泛化能力提供了基准。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensors (VBTSs) enable robots to infer contact geometry and force-related cues by imaging deformation through an internal camera, yet generalisation across sensor designs remains poorly understood. We present TacVerse, a multi-sensor dataset and benchmark for cross-sensor vision-based tactile perception.
