This single conditioning mechanism supports both de novo generation and fragment-conditioned lead optimization, letting a medicinal chemist prune a hit to a scaffold and have Sesame grow it in productive ways. In addition to this module, we also introduce a diffusion framework for joint denoising of atom types, bond types, and positions, along with a trajectory finetuning scheme that trains on the model's own sampling rollouts to improve generation quality. Sesame is trained on a large corpus of ligand-only and protein-ligand datasets.

</details>


### [128] [Are Safety Guarantees in Neural Networks Safe? How to Compute Trustworthy Robustness Certifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23858)
*Merkouris Papamichail,Konstantinos Varsos,Giorgos Flouris,João Marques-Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 AI 对抗攻击的鲁棒性认证，论文提出了一种基于内切圆半径（apothem）而非传统体积的新度量标准，有效解决了现有方法计算成本过高的问题，并通过 ParallelepipedoNN 系统实现了性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经网络鲁棒性认证方法多致力于最大化认证区域的“体积”，由于该计算过程具有不可计算性（intractability），导致无法在合理时间内完成认证。因此，需要寻找更有效的度量替代方案以平衡计算效率与认证精度。

Method: 1. 引入“内切圆半径”（apothem）度量标准，替代传统的体积度量；2. 开发了一种在神经网络验证器（oracle）调用次数上呈线性增长的优化算法；3. 提出双重认证（dual certifications）用于提供鲁棒性认证的下界（apothem-minimum upper bounds）；4. 实现并测试了 ParallelepipedoNN 系统。

Result: 1. 从理论上证明了在不考虑预言机成本的情况下，无法实现最优体积的认证算法；2. 证明所提的 apothem 优化算法在输入域直径的线性时间内即可完成；3. 在 MNIST 和 Fashion MNIST 数据集上的评估显示，该方法的最小边长比现有工作提升了至少两倍。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的基于内切圆半径（apothem）的鲁棒性认证方法相比传统的体积最大化方法更为高效且可行。实验证明其在基准数据集上显著优于现有方法，为神经网络的鲁棒性评估提供了新的理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: A primary challenge in AI safety is the existence of adversarial examples -- slightly distorted inputs that cause a neural network (NN) to misclassify. To mitigate this problem, recent research focuses on the computation of robustness certifications, which, for a given input, determine the largest distortion the input may receive without breaking the network's prediction. Robustness certifications can be interpreted as an axis-aligned hyper-rectangle (multi-dimensional intervals). Most existing approaches focus on maximizing the certification's volume, but recent intractability results prohibit the computation of volume-optimal certifications in reasonable time. We introduce the apothem measure and show how to compute apothem-optimal certifications in a linear number of calls to a NN verifier (oracle) w.r.t. the input domain's diameter. Moreover, we prove that we cannot have a volume-optimal, oracle-based algorithm, even if we discard the oracle costs. Also, we introduce dual certifications -- an interval including all instances of a class -- thus providing apothem-minimum upper bounds to a robustness certification. Further, we present the ParallelepipedoNN system, which we evaluate on the standard MNIST and Fashion MNIST benchmarks. A preliminary comparison with existing work on the same datasets reveals at least two-fold improvement w.r.t. the minimum edge length.

</details>


### [129] [Exact Schur-Sylvester Dimensionality Reductions for Non-Smooth Stochastic Complexity and Manifold Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23867)
*Trenton Lau,Gary P. T. Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种优化算法，通过数学变换显著降低了非光滑模型（如Lasso、SVM）精确NML计算的复杂度，实现了超过1.4万倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NML编码长度的精确计算在处理正则化非光滑估计器（如Lasso）时，受限于流形约束投影和体积积分过程中的高昂计算成本，存在 $\mathcal{O}(N^3)$ 的计算瓶颈。

Method: 利用分块舒尔补（Block Schur complement）和西尔维斯特行列式恒等式（Sylvester's determinant identity），将几何PPMH采样器中每步的计算复杂度从 $\mathcal{O}(N^3)$ 降低至 $\mathcal{O}(k^3 + N^2 k)$，并将其推广至稀疏支持向量机、Elastic Net和Group Lasso。

Result: 在保持双精度数值等价的前提下，实证基准测试显示计算速度提升超过 14,100 倍，并验证了该方法的数值稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过代数简化和矩阵分解技术，成功突破了非光滑估计器NML计算的立方计算瓶颈，使得大规模统计推理中精确NML估计成为现实。

Abstract: The exact computation of the Normalized Maximum Likelihood (NML) codelength for regular non-smooth estimators (e.g., Lasso) has been historically limited by the cubic scaling walls of manifold-constrained projection and volume integration. At each step of the geometric Propose-and-Project Metropolis--Hastings (PPMH) sampler, evaluating the projection operator requires inverting an $(N+k) \times (N+k)$ generalized KKT matrix, while calculating the volume factor requires the determinant of an $(N-k) \times (N-k)$ Gram matrix. This paper presents an exact, mathematically equivalent formulation that bypasses both bottlenecks by utilizing the block Schur complement and Sylvester's determinant identity. We prove that the computational complexity of both operations collapses from $\mathcal{O}(N^3)$ to $\mathcal{O}(k^3 + N^2 k)$ per step. We generalize this reduction to Sparse Support Vector Machines (SVMs), Elastic Net, and Group Lasso. Finally, we provide a rigorous numerical stability analysis and evaluate the sampler's efficiency using the Effective Sample Size (ESS) per second. Our empirical benchmarks on high-dimensional datasets confirm a constant speedup exceeding $14{,}100\times$ while maintaining double-precision numerical equivalence, rendering exact non-smooth NML estimation highly tractable for large-scale statistical inference.

</details>


### [130] [Federated Survival Analysis in Healthcare: A Multi-Model Evaluation on Cross-Institutional Heterogeneous Breast Cancer Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23871)
*Natalia Moreno-Blasco,Anusha Ihalapathirana,Pekka Siirtola,Miguel Fernandez-de-Retana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习在生存分析中的应用，通过跨机构乳腺癌数据集评估了多种模型与优化算法，证明了联邦学习能有效克服数据隐私限制，并提供了针对不同医疗场景的模型选择建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存分析对临床决策至关重要，但受限于医疗数据隐私与孤岛效应，传统的集中式训练难以实现；联邦学习虽是潜在解决方案，但在异构医疗场景下的有效性尚待验证。

Method: 对乳腺癌数据集进行多中心分布模拟，综合对比了三种生存模型（Cox、DeepSurv、RSF）以及三种联邦优化策略（FedAvg、FedProx、FedAdam），并将其与本地训练和集中式训练进行性能评估。

Result: 联邦学习的性能显著优于本地训练，并能接近甚至超越集中式训练的效果。RSF在区分度、校准度和稳健性方面表现最优。联邦学习的性能受限于客户端数据的异构程度。

Conclusion: 联邦学习在生存分析中是有效的，RSF显示出最佳的综合性能。FedAvg和FedProx在稳定性上优于FedAdam。研究提供了针对不同临床约束条件的实操指导方案。

Abstract: Survival analysis is central to clinical decision-making, yet reliable time-to-event models require large, diverse cohorts that are rarely available at a single institution, while privacy regulations restrict the centralization of patient data. Federated learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative by training shared models without exchanging raw data, but its effectiveness for survival modeling under realistic, heterogeneous conditions remains insufficiently understood. This paper presents a systematic, multi-model evaluation of federated survival analysis on a cross-institutional breast cancer cohort with naturally heterogeneous distributed clients. Three representative survival models, the Cox Proportional Hazards model, DeepSurv, and Random Survival Forest (RSF), are compared across centralized, local, and federated training, and three federated optimization strategies (FedAvg, FedProx, and FedAdam) are assessed for the gradient-based models. Results show that FL consistently outperforms local training and approaches, and occasionally exceeds, centralized performance, while RSF offers the best overall balance of discrimination, calibration, and robustness across heterogeneous clients. We further find that performance depends on the diversity of client distributions, and that FedAvg and FedProx are stronger and more stable than FedAdam. Based on these findings, we derive practical, decision-oriented guidelines mapping data, privacy, interpretability, and resource constraints to recommended model and training-paradigm choices for federated survival modeling in healthcare.

</details>


### [131] [MGI: Member vs Generated Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23872)
*Bihe Zhao,Michel Meintz,Juangui Xu,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对生成模型难以区分训练数据与生成样本的问题，本文提出了Data Circuit Breaker (DCB)方法，通过组合模型内部的多维信号，实现了对训练成员与生成输出的高精度判别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型性能的提升使得人类难以区分样本是来自原始训练集还是模型生成，特别是在模型发生过拟合（记忆训练数据）的情况下。现有的成员推理（Membership Inference）和溯源（Attribution）方法往往因过度依赖似然比信号而导致分类错误。

Method: 提出了Data Circuit Breaker (DCB)方法，该方法包含三个阶段，核心思想是利用生成模型的自动编码器（autoencoder）和潜在生成器（latent generator）提供的互补信号，从而精准区分训练成员与生成数据。

Result: 在多种生成模型（如自回归模型、扩散模型）上进行了测试，DCB能够有效克服现有方法的错误分类缺陷，并在模型重复生成训练样本副本或在模型衍生物场景下表现出良好的稳健性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: DCB通过结合重构误差和潜在表征分析，有效解决了生成模型中“训练集成员”与“模型生成样本”混淆的问题，为数据溯源和版权保护提供了稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: As generative models increasingly produce samples that are indistinguishable from human-created content, it becomes difficult to determine whether a given data point was part of a model's natural training set or was generated by the model itself, especially when models memorize and reproduce training data. We formalize this challenge as Member vs Generated Inference (MGI): given a sample and a target generative model, infer whether the sample is a true training member or a generated output of that model. Focusing on image generation, we show that existing membership inference methods systematically misclassify generated samples as training members, while attribution-based methods often misclassify true members as generated. This failure arises because both approaches rely on likelihood-related signals that are similarly elevated for training examples and for the model's own outputs. To address MGI, we propose Data Circuit Breaker (DCB), a three-stage method that combines complementary signals from a generative model's autoencoder and latent generator to distinguish training members from generated samples. Across multiple generative models, including image autoregressive and diffusion models, DCB consistently addresses the shortcomings of membership inference and attribution methods, remains effective even when models reproduce near-duplicates of training samples, and generalizes to challenging model derivative settings in which new models are trained on generated data.

</details>


### [132] [GRACE: Gated Refinement for Accurate Causal Edge Discovery in High-Dimensional Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23880)
*Mohammad Fesanghary,Abhinav Havaldar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大规模因果发现难题，GRACE结合了快速线性CI骨架与带有Hard Concrete门控机制的L0正则化模型，实现了比非线性测试快75倍且更精确的因果结构发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代复杂系统数据维数极高，导致现有的因果发现方法面临困境：约束基方法（Constraint-based）中的非线性CI测试在大规模数据下计算不可行，而基于分数的方法（Score-based）则依赖启发式阈值导致结果不稳定。因此需要一种既能保持统计严谨性又能保证扩展性的高效方法。

Method: 提出了一种名为GRACE的框架。该方法首先利用快速线性条件独立性（CI）测试构建高召回率的候选骨架，随后引入带有硬具体（Hard Concrete）门控和L0正则化的模型，通过动态学习边对预测的贡献，自动剔除伪阳性边，从而在保持计算效率的同时实现稳健的二元边判别。

Result: 1. 在合成数据集上：GRACE在多种图拓扑结构下显著提升了F1分数，且性能优于现有的基于注意力机制和分数的方法，计算速度快约75倍。2. 在真实世界数据集（易北河河流流量监测）上：GRACE成功去除了骨架中99%的伪阳性边，实现了0.86的F1分数和0.99的AUROC，证明了其在处理违反传统假设的复杂现实场景中的优越性。

Conclusion: GRACE 成功解决了传统因果发现方法在准确性（受制于线性假设）与计算效率（受制于非线性CI测试）之间的权衡问题。其实验结果证明了引入硬具体（Hard Concrete）门控机制和L0正则化在处理高维复杂数据时的有效性，使其成为一种高效且鲁棒的因果结构发现工具。

Abstract: From climate teleconnections to gene regulation, modern time-series datasets encompass tens or hundreds of interacting variables, making causal discovery increasingly challenging. Constraint-based methods offer statistical rigor but their nonlinear CI tests are infeasible at scale, while score-based alternatives avoid CI testing but require arbitrary thresholds to binarize continuous edge scores. We propose GRACE ($\textbf{G}$ated $\textbf{R}$efinement for $\textbf{A}$ccurate $\textbf{C}$ausal $\textbf{E}$dge discovery), which refines constraint-based discovery using Hard Concrete gates with $L_0$ regularization: each candidate edge has an independent gate whose values concentrate near 0 or 1, yielding a clean bimodal separation that makes the binary decision robust, unlike the narrow, overlapping score distributions produced by $L_1$ and attention-based methods. A fast linear CI skeleton provides high-recall candidates; a single gated model then prunes false positives by learning which edges genuinely improve prediction, with automatic regularization adapted to problem dimensions and skeleton density. Systematic experiments on synthetic benchmarks, spanning diverse graph topologies (scale-free, Erdős-R'enyi, small-world) and dimensionalities up to $d=100$, show that GRACE substantially improves F1 over its base CI method while maintaining high precision, and outperforms attention-based and score-based alternatives. GRACE matches or exceeds expensive nonlinear CI tests at a fraction of the cost ($75\times$ faster). On a real-world river flow dataset, where rainfall confounders, variable propagation lags, and distributional shifts violate standard assumptions, a temporal bootstrap variant of GRACE recovers 9 of 11 causal edges along the Elbe River with only 1 false positive ($F_1 = 0.86$, AUROC${} = 0.99$), reducing the skeleton's 106 false positives by 99%.

</details>


### [133] [ARIA: Adaptive Region-Based Importance Allocation for Conditional Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23898)
*Loay Mualem,Vinh Tong,Samir Darouich,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARIA是一种用于条件扩散模型蒸馏的框架，通过动态监测教师-学生模型的偏差区域，自适应分配训练资源，显著提升了模型在广泛条件空间下的泛化能力和蒸馏效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件扩散模型蒸馏方法在面对大规模条件空间时（如海量文本提示），存在训练资源分配效率低下、难以覆盖训练分布外条件以及计算成本高昂的问题。需要一种机制来智能地分配训练预算，以提升模型在未见或表征不足区域的性能。

Method: 提出了ARIA（Adaptive Region-based Importance Allocation）框架。该方法将条件空间划分为多个粗粒度区域，通过在线维护教师模型与学生模型在各区域间的差异估计，动态调整训练任务的权重，优先优化模型在差异较大或不足区域的表现，同时保持原有的蒸馏目标不变。

Result: 实验结果显示，ARIA在多种架构和设置下均优于基准方法（RC），特别是在未见（unseen）和表征不足（underrepresented）的条件下表现出显著优势。此外，理论分析证明了该跟踪机制在有界方差和漂移假设下能有效刻画训练过程中的差异演变。

Conclusion: ARIA框架通过在线评估和自适应分配训练资源，有效解决了条件扩散模型蒸馏中训练效率和分布外性能泛化问题，提供了一种高效的知识蒸馏策略。

Abstract: Distilling conditional diffusion models aims to transfer the behavior of a large teacher to a smaller student while preserving alignment across conditioning inputs. Unlike recognition tasks, knowledge distillation in conditional diffusion often struggles to transfer knowledge beyond the training distribution, since the predicted noise strongly depends on the conditioning signal. As a result, effective distillation requires exploring a large conditioning space. In practical settings, this creates a major bottleneck. Paired image-condition data may be limited, and generating synthetic images for every available condition is often computationally infeasible, while the pool of conditions, such as text prompts, can be extremely large. Recent work addresses this issue by switching conditions during training, exposing the student to a broader conditioning space without changing the distillation objective. Yet this raises a complementary question: once a large conditioning corpus is available, how should the training effort be allocated? In this work, we introduce ARIA, a framework that adaptively allocates training effort across coarse regions of the conditioning space. By maintaining online estimates of teacher-student discrepancy at the region level, ARIA focuses updates where misalignment persists while preserving the original distillation objective. Empirically, ARIA improves over RC across most architectures and settings, with the clearest gains observed in unseen and underrepresented regimes. We also provide a theoretical analysis showing that the proposed tracking mechanism follows the evolving discrepancy during training under bounded variance and drift assumptions.

</details>


### [134] [Closing the Loop: Formally Verified Law as a Reward Signal for Self-Improving Legal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23913)
*Armin Heydari,Torben Leowald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过“形式化法律演算+验证内核”来约束大型语言模型法律推理的架构，旨在为法律AI提供可验证的逻辑保障，并解决法律领域强化学习缺乏高质量奖励信号的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的法律AI在处理复杂法律推理时缺乏确定性标准，难以直接通过强化学习进行有效训练，且法律逻辑的正确性难以保证。因此，需要引入形式化验证方法来确保法律AI推理的严谨性和正确性。

Method: 提出了一种基于形式化验证的法律AI架构，包含三个核心组件：(1) 将法律条文通过LLM自动形式化为扩展的Catala语言；(2) 引入形式化验证内核；(3) 基于形式化证明路径生成可解释性回答。该架构通过确定性的外部验证器，为强化学习提供了可靠的奖励信号。

Result: 在德国程序性期限计算、美国宪法商业条款分析及跨司法管辖区制裁比例原则测试中证明了该架构的有效性。实验表明，该架构能确保法律论证的完整性、步骤的正确性及逻辑链条的有效性，并为法律AI的强化学习训练提供了可行的反馈闭环。

Conclusion: 该架构通过将大模型（LLM）的生成能力与形式化验证机制有机结合，为法律人工智能提供了一种兼具“计算层面的可证明性”与“开放性法律分析结构保障”的可行方案，并有效解决了法律领域强化学习中缺乏确定性奖励信号的难题。

Abstract: This article develops an architecture that creates a formally verifiable reward signal to train legal AI, adapting the LLM proposes, verifier disposes paradigm from mathematical AI to the distinctive demands of law. We present an architecture comprising LLM-driven autoformalization into a formal legal calculus extending Catala, a verification kernel, and explanation generation grounded in formal proof traces. For the computational components of law, the architecture provides provable correctness. For open-textured legal analysis, it provides structural guarantees: every required stage of the legal argument is addressed, argumentation is exercised at the correct stages and not omitted, and the deductive links between steps are valid. We demonstrate the architecture on procedural deadline calculations in German law, Commerce Clause analysis in U.S. constitutional law, and cross-jurisdictional sanction proportionality. We further show that the same architecture has a structural advantage for legal AI training: a deterministic external verifier supplies verifiable outcomes for legal problems and thereby closes the traditional reinforcement-learning loop gap in law.

</details>


### [135] [Catastrophic Compositional Generation: Why Vanilla Diffusion Models Fail to Extrapolate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23920)
*Duncan Soiffer,Chandler Squires,Yuan Guan,Jason Hartford,Pradeep Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 组合式扩散模型在处理分布外组合任务时，简单的推理阶段校正方法因无法克服得分估计误差而失效，指出了当前该类研究在理论与实践上的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在使用仅训练于部分条件子集的生成模型，进行组合式目标分布采样时，传统方法在处理分布外组合任务时的局限性与可行性。

Method: 通过理论推导的泛化论证以及在合成数据和真实数据上的对比实验，对普通条件扩散模型在组合式生成任务中的能力边界进行评估。

Result: 研究表明，当目标分布相对于源分布属于分布外（OOD）时，得分估计误差（score estimation error）会对生成质量产生灾难性影响，现有的推理时改进方法难以有效弥补这一缺陷。

Conclusion: 提出组合式生成任务中，仅依靠推理阶段的技巧（如Feynman-Kac校正）无法解决分布外（OOD）组合导致的灾难性得分估计误差，因此需要探索全新的方法论。

Abstract: The task of compositional generation involves using a conditional generative model, trained only on a subset of the possible conditions, to produce samples from compositionally-defined target distributions such as a geometric combination of the source distributions. In this work, we argue that this task is often infeasible for vanilla conditional diffusion models: we conjecture that no inference-time technique can efficiently produce samples from the target distribution in certain well-motivated settings. This idea is supported by theory-guided generalization arguments and carefully-designed experiments on both synthetic and realistic data. In particular, while recent methods such as Feynman-Kac correction reduce inference-time approximation error, our results show that score estimation error has a more catastrophic effect on performance when the target distribution is out-of-distribution with respect to the sources, highlighting the need for a different approach to this task.

</details>


### [136] [KLip-PPO: A per-sample KL perspective on PPO-Clip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23932)
*Riccardo Colletti,Robin Holzinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了PPO的两种主流形式（Clipped Surrogate与KL Penalty）在数学上是等价的：PPO-Clip本质上是一种在信任域边界具有阶跃特性的逐样本动态KL惩罚方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术界长期将PPO的两种变体（裁剪代理函数和KL散度惩罚）视为各具不同梯度和超参数的独立算法，旨在澄清两者之间的数学联系及其结构特征。

Method: 通过数学推导证明并对比了两种目标函数的梯度形式，并在五个MuJoCo连续控制基准任务上进行了实证对比分析。

Result: 数学上证明了裁剪目标的梯度可以由一个具有逐样本动态系数的KL代理函数精确复现，且在五个基准任务上的训练曲线几乎完全不可区分。

Conclusion: PPO的“裁剪目标函数”与“KL惩罚目标函数”在数学上是等价的。裁剪目标的本质是一种自适应的、逐样本变化的KL惩罚，其在信任域边界呈现阶跃函数特性。这一发现为改进与泛化PPO算法提供了新的理论设计视角。

Abstract: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) is the standard policy-gradient algorithm for on-policy reinforcement learning. The literature presents it in two forms, a clipped surrogate that bounds the importance ratio between successive policies and a Kullback-Leibler penalty between them. These forms are treated as separate algorithms with their own gradients, their own hyperparameters, and their own reference implementations, and a sizeable body of empirical work compares them. We show that the gradient of the clipped surrogate is reproduced exactly by a Kullback-Leibler surrogate whose coefficient varies per sample, with closed-form dependence on the importance ratio and the advantage. The identity holds at every minibatch step and across the entire inner loop, and on five MuJoCo continuous-control benchmarks the two losses produce indistinguishable training curves. The reformulation exposes a structural feature of the clipped surrogate that the min notation hides. PPO-Clip's implicit per-sample penalty is a step function at the boundary of the trust region, and the shape of this coefficient is the natural design axis for generalising the algorithm. We sketch the resulting follow-up directions in the discussion.

</details>


### [137] [DREG: A Layer-Wise Jacobian Regularization as a General-Purpose Penalty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23942)
*Rowan Martnishn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大规模实证研究验证了 DREG 在各类数据集和现代架构（如 Transformer）中的优异性能，证明其作为一种几何归纳偏置，在数据稀缺和非平滑激活函数场景下具有显著的正则化效果与易用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了探究 DREG（导数正则化）在不同条件下（何时、何地、为何）的有效性及贡献，以明确其在通用深度学习架构中的实用价值。

Method: 开展了一项大规模实证研究，涵盖了 4 种激活函数、6 种正则化项、8 个数据集和 5 个随机种子，共计 960 次全因子实验，对比分析了 DREG 与其他正则化方法的性能。

Result: DREG 在总准确率和纯净数据集准确率上均表现最优；在噪声鲁棒性上仅次于频谱归一化（SN）；在 GELU 激活函数及数据稀缺场景下表现极其突出；保持了无需针对特定数据集进行超参数调优的鲁棒性（即插即用）。

Conclusion: DREG 是一种高效的、即插即用的正则化方法，特别适用于数据稀缺场景以及包含复杂 Jacobian 结构的现代模型（如 Transformer/GELU 网络），其优越性源于其能够自适应地对梯度最大的层施加约束。

Abstract: We present a large-scale empirical study isolating the contributions of the Derivative Regularization penalty (DREG). Across a fully-crossed factorial sweep of 960 experiments spanning 4 activations, 6 regularizers, 8 datasets, and 5 random seeds, we ask: when, where, and why does DREG work? Our results establish three principal findings. First, DREG achieves the highest overall and clean-regime accuracy among all regularizers evaluated (significantly so against the unregularized baseline, Weight Decay, and IGPen; Wilcoxon $p \leq 0.031$). It ranks second in noise robustness behind Spectral Normalization (SN) - the only two layer-wise regularizers in the study. Second, DREG is globally the best-performing regularizer under GELU, the default activation in modern transformer architectures, particularly on both messy vision and messy NLP benchmarks, suggesting direct applicability to frontier deep learning settings. Third, DREG's advantage over competing regularizers is most pronounced under data scarcity, consistent with its role as a geometric inductive bias that substitutes for the regularizing effect of data volume. Throughout, DREG is applied with a single fixed hyperparameter $λ= 10^{-2.5}$ and no per-dataset tuning, supporting its characterization as a plug-and-play regularizer for neural networks with nontrivial Jacobian structure. These findings are consistent with DREG's design: concentrating regularization pressure on layers where the activation derivative is largest, rather than constraining the network uniformly.

</details>


### [138] [Learning the Koopman Operator using Attention Free Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23957)
*Mohammed Nagdi,Evangelos-Marios Nikolados,Alexey Yermakov,Mars Gao,Nathan Kutz,Filippo Menolascina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 Koopman 算子在长程预测中易漂移的问题，提出一种结合线性注意力记忆模块与在线漂移检测重编码的紧凑型算法，实现了高精度、低延迟的长时轨迹预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 Koopman 算子自动编码器在处理具有切换、连续谱或强瞬态的系统时，长时预测容易导致潜变量偏离流形，引发相位和幅度误差。

Method: 1. AFT（Attention-Free, Temporal）记忆模块：利用短窗口历史潜变量进行修正，通过线性时间复杂度和极少参数实现上下文捕捉；2. 动态重编码（Dynamic Re-encoding）：集成 EWMA、CUSUM 和顺序双样本检验等机制，实时检测潜变量漂移并将其投影回自动编码器流形。

Result: 在 Duffing 振子、Repressilator 和 IRMA 三个基准系统上，该模型在长达 1000 步的预测中显著降低了误差累积，且推理延迟更低，优于 Koopman 自动编码器及多头注意力机制架构。

Conclusion: 通过引入 AFT（注意自由记忆）模块和动态重编码机制，该方法在保持低推理延迟的同时，有效解决了 Koopman 算子在长时预测中易偏离流形导致误差累积的问题，在多个基准系统上表现优于现有的 Transformer 和 GRU 架构。

Abstract: Learning Koopman operators with autoencoders enables linear prediction in a latent space, but long-horizon rollouts often drift off the learned manifold, leading to phase and amplitude errors on systems with switching, continuous spectra, or strong transients. We introduce two complementary components that make Koopman predictors more robust. First, we add an attention-free latent memory (AFT) block that aggregates a short window of past latents to produce a corrected latent before each Koopman update. Unlike multi-head attention, AFT operates in linear time and adds only $\approx$30k parameters ($3d^2 + T^2$, fewer than matched multi-head attention), yet captures the local temporal context needed to suppress error divergence. Second, we propose dynamic re-encoding: lightweight, online change-point triggers (EWMA, CUSUM, and sequential two-sample tests) that detect latent drift and project predictions back onto the autoencoder manifold. Across three benchmark systems -- Duffing oscillator, Repressilator, IRMA -- our model consistently reduces error accumulation compared to a Koopman autoencoder and matched-capacity multi-head attention. We also compare against GRU and Transformer autoencoders, evaluated both from initial conditions and with a 50-step context, and find that Koopman+AFT (with optional re-encoding) attains markedly lower long-horizon error while maintaining lower inference latency. We report improvements over horizons up to 1000 steps, together with ablations over trigger policies. The result is a fast, compact predictor that stays on the learned manifold over long horizons.

</details>


### [139] [Forget Without Compromise: Nexus Sampling for Streaming KV-Cache Eviction Under Fixed Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23961)
*Duc Duong,Hoang Anh Duy Le,Jianwen Xie,Anshumali Shrivastava,Zhaozhuo Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对长上下文大模型 KV cache 容量限制问题，提出 Nexus Sampling 方法，通过迭代评分和概率采样取代传统的 Top-K 硬剔除，显著提升了重要上下文信息的留存率和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 KV cache 淘汰方法均依赖于基于每步注意力分数进行 Top-K 截断，这种确定性方法会导致一些虽不显著但在长期上下文中有重要作用的令牌被永久性误删。

Method: 1. Nexus 评分：通过对直接注意力的迭代遍历来识别“桥接令牌（bridge tokens）”；2. 加权水库采样：使用随机概率替代确定性的 Top-K 筛选，以提高重要但非显著令牌的存活率。

Result: 在 80% KV cache 淘汰率下，Nexus Sampling 在 LongBench 上表现与密集注意力模型差距在 1% 以内，在检索任务中显著优于 Top-K 基线，并将缓存内存需求降低至原来的 1/10。

Conclusion: Nexus Sampling 是一种无需训练的 KV cache 淘汰策略，通过引入 Nexus 评分机制和加权水库采样，有效克服了传统 Top-K 确定性剔除带来的信息丢失问题，在显著降低内存占用的同时保证了模型性能。

Abstract: Long-context and agentic LLM workloads push the KV cache past any fixed memory budget, forcing the inference stack to permanently evict tokens at every step of a continuous-inference stream. Existing methods all share the same template, a per-step direct-attention score followed by deterministic top-$K$ selection, which converts a single below-cutoff step into an irreversible verdict and permanently erases any subtly important token that direct attention cannot single out from noise. To address this challenge, we propose Nexus Sampling, a training-free eviction method that pairs Nexus scoring, an iterative walk over direct attention that surfaces bridge tokens, with weighted reservoir sampling, which retains tokens with inclusion probability in place of deterministic top-$K$. Theoretically, we show that Nexus Sampling dominates deterministic top-$K$ in long-run survival of subtly important tokens. Empirically, at 80% KV cache eviction, Nexus Sampling matches dense attention within 1% on LongBench while outperforming top-$K$ baselines on retrieval-heavy tasks, with up to 10x smaller per-sequence cache memory.

</details>


### [140] [3D Masked Autoencoders are Robust Learners of Volumetric and Multimodal Cellular Representations for Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23964)
*Amirhossein Kardoost,Lion Gleiter,Tingying Peng,Carsten Marr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出MAE-3D架构，证明了在显微图像分析中原生3D建模配合多模态（蛋白质语言模型）对齐，能显著提升单细胞任务的特征表示性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自监督学习方法大多依赖于2D投影，忽略了细胞本质上的3D属性，限制了从显微图像中提取空间特征的能力。

Method: 构建并对比了2D和3D掩码自编码器（MAE），引入了通道交叉注意力机制（Channel cross-attention）和频域正则化（Frequency-domain regularization），并利用预训练蛋白质语言模型（ESM2）实现跨模态监督对齐。

Result: MAE-3D在单细胞下游任务中表现优于2D方法。在蛋白质相互作用预测中，ROC-AUC达到0.865；在蛋白质定位任务中，AUC_micro达到0.952，F1_micro达到0.742，均刷新了SOTA。

Conclusion: 原生3D建模与多模态对齐对于单细胞显微镜的表征学习具有显著优势，能有效捕捉3D空间上下文，从而在蛋白质相互作用预测和定位任务中实现SOTA性能。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning in fluorescence microscopy often relies on 2D projections, despite the inherently three-dimensional nature of cells. We present a systematic comparison of 2D and 3D masked autoencoders (MAE-2D vs. MAE-3D) on volumetric microscopy data. Under matched architectures and training protocols, MAE-3D consistently outperforms 2D max-projection and slice-based variants on downstream single-cell tasks. We further align visual representations with a pretrained protein language model (ESM2) and show that cross-modal supervision yields larger gains for volumetric models. Channel cross-attention and frequency-domain regularization are critical for leveraging 3D spatial context. On a protein--protein interaction task, MAE-3D achieves a ROC--AUC of 0.865, outperforming prior methods by up to +0.025. For protein localization, our best 3D model attains state-of-the-art AUC$_{\text{micro}}$ (0.952) and F1$_{\text{micro}}$ (0.742), improving over previous approaches by +0.003 and +0.010 absolute, respectively. Overall, these results demonstrate the advantages of native 3D modeling and multimodal alignment for representation learning in single-cell microscopy.

</details>


### [141] [A Comparative Study of Bayesian Contextual Bandits for Real-Time Warehouse Sorter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23977)
*Tina Dongxu Li,Mouhacine Benosman,Ken Meszaros,Trevor Dardik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对自动化仓库分拣系统的静态控制问题，提出并验证了基于贝叶斯语境老虎机（BCB）的实时自适应控制框架，在提升系统效率及响应实时复杂环境方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型电商仓库现有的自动化物料搬运系统（MHS）中的分拣分流控制多依赖静态权重成本函数，因无法适应动态业务环境（如流量模式、拥塞程度、设备状态等变化）而导致效率低下，需寻求更智能、更具自适应性的实时优化方案。

Method: 通过高保真物理感知模拟器进行离线训练及在线演进，比较了三种框架：线性回归+梯度下降（LR+GDO）、XGBoost+贝叶斯优化（XGB+BO）以及贝叶斯语境老虎机（BCB）。评价指标涵盖预测准确性、语境敏感度、动作分布及奖励提升。

Result: BCB框架相比基准启发式方法实现了2.03%的奖励提升，并表现出更低的推理延迟、Bang-Bang控制逻辑支持以及卓越的持续在线学习及探索平衡能力。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯语境老虎机（BCB）框架在处理实时分拣分流控制问题上表现优于现有的静态权重启发式方法及其他机器学习模型，具备高实时性、持续在线学习能力以及优秀的探索与利用平衡，是大规模仓储自动化系统控制优化的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient sorter diversion control of automated material handling systems (MHS) is critical for optimizing operational efficiency in large-scale warehouse environments. In this study, we use an inbound receiving sorter at a high-volume e-commerce warehouse as our primary use case, where the sorter diversion system relies on cost functions with static weight configurations that fail to adapt to highly dynamic system contexts, such as volume mode, congestion level, equipment physical status, and upstream/downstream dependencies. To address this real-time sorter diversion optimization challenge, we conducted a comparative study of three candidate hybrid machine learning frameworks: Linear Regression with Gradient Descent Optimization (LR+GDO), XGBoost with Bayesian Optimization (XGB+BO), and Bayesian Contextual Bandits (BCB). Model training and evaluation were enabled by leveraging a high-fidelity physics-aware emulator to overcome the cold-start problem and allow a safe transition from offline to online learning. We performed comprehensive evaluations including reward model predictive accuracy, contextual sensitivity, action distribution, and projected reward uplift. Our results demonstrate that while tree-based reward models offer slightly better predictive power, the BCB framework achieved overall higher performance with 2.03% reward uplift over the heuristic baseline. Furthermore, BCB exhibits several superior characteristics, such as its decisive time-optimal policy backed by Bang-Bang control theory, continuous online learning capability, strategic balance between exploration and exploitation, and significantly shorter inference latency. These results demonstrate the potential of the BCB framework for real-time control optimization in large-scale warehouse environments, motivating further investigation toward operational deployment.

</details>


### [142] [Offline Reinforcement Learning for Warehouse SLAM Throughput Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23978)
*Tina Dongxu Li,Mouhacine Benosman,Rajat Kumar,Kevin Tan,Ken Meszaros,Trevor Dardik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离线强化学习的仓库 SLAM 吞吐量优化框架，通过历史数据训练策略，成功在保障下游系统稳定的前提下，显著提升了整体吞吐效率与系统健康度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库 fulfillment 环境中，SLAM（扫描/贴标/应用/清单）吞吐量直接决定了系统拥堵状况和运营效率，亟需一种能够平衡吞吐量最大化与下游系统稳定性的智能化控制方案。

Method: 提出了一种通用的离线强化学习框架：包括基于历史信息的环境状态表征、针对延迟影响控制的动作空间抽象，以及综合考虑上下游指标的奖励函数。作者通过多种离线 RL 算法（如 CQL 等）进行部署，并采用了多维度评估策略，包括回归模型、FQE 以及基于 Deep Koopman 动态模型的评估方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架（特别是 CQL 策略）性能最优，使系统健康度提升了 22.97%，并将平均限流（throttling）时间缩短了 3.18%。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了离线强化学习在仓库 SLAM 吞吐量控制中具有显著的优化潜力，能够有效提升系统稳定性并实现任务流程的智能化调度。

Abstract: We present an offline reinforcement learning (RL) framework for optimizing SLAM throughput control in a warehouse fulfillment environment. SLAM (Scan/Label/Apply/Manifest) throughput directly influences system congestion and operational efficiency. Our RL-based control approach dynamically recommends SLAM throughput settings that adaptively balance throughput maximization with downstream stability through intelligent adjustment of throttling behavior. We include a history-informed state representation, action space abstraction for delayed-impact control, and a reward function that captures both upstream and downstream operational metrics. Our approach is algorithm-agnostic, enabling integration of multiple offline RL methods under a unified architecture. We instantiate our framework with three state-of-the-art offline RL algorithms, and trained the models offline using de-identified historical operational logs from a large-scale warehouse. Policy performance is evaluated using a comprehensive multi-method strategy. These include model-free approaches including immediate reward estimation via regression models and long-horizon Fitted Q Evaluation (FQE), as well as model-based Deep Koopman dynamics evaluation. Empirical results reveal that the CQL policy consistently outperforms alternatives, improving system health by 22.97% and reducing average throttling duration by 3.18%. These findings demonstrate the potential of offline RL for safe and scalable warehouse throughput control optimization.

</details>


### [143] [Learning to Trigger: Reinforcement Learning at the Large Hadron Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23993)
*Zixin Ding,Shaghayegh Emami,Giovanna Salvi,Cecilia Tosciri,Abhijith Gandrakota,Jennifer Ngadiuba,Nhan Tran,Christian Herwig,David W. Miller,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出利用强化学习（GFPO算法）动态优化大型强子对撞机的实时触发阈值，在模拟及真实物理实验数据中均证明了其在平衡信号效率与背景约束方面的优越性，显著优于传统人工调节策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC等科研设施的在线触发器（trigger）大多依赖静态、人工调节的策略。随着探测器条件、堆积效应（pileup）和背景组成随时间漂移，手动调节难以维持最优的信号效率与背景抑制平衡，因此需要一种能够自动、动态适应实时数据的智能控制方法。

Method: 将在线触发阈值调整建模为序列决策问题，并利用强化学习（RL）进行优化。研究引入了群过滤策略优化（GFPO）的两种变体（GFPO-F和GFPO-FR），专门用于在训练过程中强制执行背景率的可行性约束，以确保在实时流式传输环境中满足带宽和物理需求。

Result: 在模拟环境下，与基准方法相比，该方法在$H_T$触发器和异常检测（AD）触发器上的容差时间内区间占比分别提升了48%和28%，且信号效率提高了2%；在真实CMS碰撞数据（Run 283408）上，未经过细调的模型依然实现了56%（$H_T$）和28%（AD）的容差性能提升。

Conclusion: 该研究成功证明了强化学习（特别是所提出的GFPO变体）在大型强子对撞机（LHC）在线触发系统中的有效性。它不仅在仿真环境中表现出色，且直接迁移至真实碰撞数据时表现稳健，证明了RL在解决动态环境触发阈值调整问题的巨大潜力，能够显著提高实验数据的信号效率与背景抑制稳定性。

Abstract: High-throughput scientific facilities such as the Large Hadron Collider depend on real-time event filtering (\textit{triggering}) under tight constraints on bandwidth, latency, and storage. In practice, trigger menus are largely static and hand-tuned and can become suboptimal as detector conditions, pileup, and background composition drift over time. We cast online threshold tuning as a sequential decision-making problem: a reinforcement learning agent ingests streaming summaries of recent rates and signal-sensitive features and updates trigger thresholds to maximize signal efficiency while tracking a target background rate within a tolerance band. We adapt Group-Filtered Policy Optimization (GFPO) to streaming control and introduce two variants (GFPO-F, GFPO-FR) that enforce background rate feasibility during training. On a benchmark that emulates realistic collider operation, we study two representative triggers: a total transverse energy ($H_{T}$) trigger sensitive to pileup variation, and an anomaly-detection (AD) trigger based on reconstruction loss for rare or non-standard signatures. On Monte Carlo streams, our agent increases the fraction of in-tolerance time intervals by 48\% ($H_T$) and 28\% (AD), with a cumulative gain of up to 2\% in signal efficiency on those in-tolerance intervals. Transferring from simulation to \emph{real} collision data (CMS Run 283408), the same agent, without fine-tuning, achieves a 56\% ($H_T$) and 28\% (AD) in-tolerance improvement over baselines, with further signal-efficiency gain on both triggers. To our knowledge, this is the \emph{first} demonstration of RL-based trigger control on real Large Hadron Collider collision data. Code is available at https://github.com/Zixind/GFPO\_LHC.

</details>


### [144] [EMAgnet: Parameter-Space EMA Regularization for Policy Gradient Self-Play in Large Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23995)
*Tristan Maidment,JB Lanier,Chase McDonald,Nathan Tsang,Eugene Vinitsky,Roy Fox,Albert Wang,Wesley N. Kerr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对零和博弈中的自对弈训练，提出 EMAgnet 通过自适应的 EMA 策略参数正则化，取代了传统的均匀分布正则化，有效提升了智能体在复杂环境下的博弈性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然基于正则化的策略梯度方法（如 PPO）在零和博弈中表现出色，但通用的均匀分布正则化方法无法区分不同动作的可行性，导致正则化过于盲目。

Method: 引入 EMAgnet 算法，将正则化目标从固定的均匀分布改为对最近一次迭代策略参数的指数移动平均（EMA），实现正则化目标的动态演进。

Result: 在标准零和博弈基准和包含探索挑战及大量劣势策略的特殊基准上，EMAgnet 相比使用均匀分布正则化的 PPO，显著降低了 exploitability，表现更强且更稳健。

Conclusion: EMAgnet 提出了一种自适应正则化方法，通过移动平均参数动态调整正则化目标，能有效应对复杂博弈环境中的探索挑战和劣势策略，在降低博弈可利用性（exploitability）方面优于传统的均匀分布正则化方法。

Abstract: Recent work has established that regularized policy gradient methods such as PPO, when used in self-play, can match or exceed specialized game-theoretic algorithms for solving two-player zero-sum imperfect-information games. The uniform distribution has emerged as a strong policy regularization target for this purpose, but it regularizes equally toward all actions regardless of their viability. We introduce EMAgnet, which instead regularizes toward an exponential moving average (EMA) of the last-iterate policy's parameters, providing an adaptive regularization target that evolves with the agent's improving strategy. We evaluate EMAgnet on both standard two-player zero-sum benchmarks and modified benchmarks with exploration challenges and large numbers of strictly dominated strategies. Relative to PPO self-play with uniform-magnet regularization under both linear and power-law annealing schedules, EMAgnet achieves lower exploitability in the majority of tested environments, with consistent performance gains across games containing strictly dominated strategies.

</details>


### [145] [Cyclic Denoising Reveals Ultrastable Memories in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24000)
*Rishabh Sharma,Stefano Martiniani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为“循环去噪”的物理启发式攻击方法，通过反复加/去噪，无需任何额外信息即可诱导扩散模型提取出其训练集中存储的图像（即记忆），揭示了模型的隐私泄露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示图像扩散模型中学到的分布中那些常规采样难以触及的区域，并研究模型内部的“记忆”行为，以评估扩散模型的隐私和数据安全风险。

Method: 提出“循环去噪”（cyclic denoising）攻击，即在受控噪声振幅下重复进行前向加噪与反向去噪过程。该方法无需梯度、权重访问、提示词或训练数据先验，属于非受控（unconditioned）攻击。

Result: 研究发现扩散模型中存在被称为“超稳定吸引子”的结构，它们对应于训练数据中的记忆图像（如照片、水印等）。该过程表现出类似非晶态固体中“屈服”的物理相变现象，不同幅度的循环会导致不同的稳定结果。在Stable Diffusion v1.4和DDPM上证实了该方法的普适性。

Conclusion: 循环去噪是一种强大的、受物理学启发的探测方法，揭示了扩散模型中普遍存在的记忆现象。它不仅是研究生成景观的有力工具，也是用于审计模型隐私和版权合规性的实用手段。

Abstract: We introduce cyclic denoising -- repeated forward and reverse diffusion at controlled noise amplitudes -- as an extraction attack for image diffusion models. Inspired by random organization in disordered solids, cyclic denoising exposes regions of the learned distribution that are largely inaccessible to standard sampling. The dynamics drive samples toward attractors with a broad stability spectrum. The deepest attractors are ultrastable: they regenerate after near-total corruption and persist through thousands of noising-denoising cycles. Many of these attractors correspond to memorized training images, including stock photographs, brand watermarks, and web-crawl artifacts. The attack requires only sampler-level control, with no gradients, weight inspection, prompts, captions, or prior knowledge of the training data. Unlike generate-and-filter attacks, which rely on large-scale prompted generation and post-hoc similarity or membership-inference filtering, our main protocol is fully unconditioned. We demonstrate the phenomenon in Stable Diffusion v1.4 and in a pixel-space DDPM, showing consistent behavior across latent- and pixel-space diffusion models. Across noise amplitudes, we observe a yielding-like transition: low-amplitude cycling produces trivial absorbing fixed points or limit cycles, while larger amplitudes induce rearrangements, basin hopping, and long-lived trapping in structured memorized attractor basins. We also observe hierarchical partial absorption, prompt-stabilized basins, and cross-initial-condition universality of the recovered attractor set. Our results therefore show that cyclic denoising is both a physics-inspired probe of generative landscapes and a practical tool for memorization auditing, with implications for privacy, copyright compliance, and model fingerprinting.

</details>


### [146] [Fast and Slow Variational Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24007)
*Subarnaduti Paul,Yohan Jung,Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan,Siddharth Swaroop,Thomas Möllenhoff,Martin Mundt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CoVON优化器，通过将“后验合并”策略引入VCL框架，实现了快慢适应机制，有效解决了持续学习中稳定性与可塑性的权衡问题，并在多种任务中表现卓越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习优化器缺乏内置的“快慢适应”机制，难以有效平衡持续学习中的稳定性（stability）与可塑性（plasticity）之间的矛盾。

Method: 在变分持续学习（VCL）框架的基础上，提出了“后验合并”（merging of past posteriors）机制作为缓慢适应策略，将其集成到IVON优化器中，形成了CoVON优化器。该方法利用合并后的后验分布作为先验，从而在持续学习过程中控制知识的漂移。

Result: 实验结果表明，CoVON在领域增量学习、持续预训练以及大语言模型微调等任务中，性能均优于现有的VCL优化器及其他权重正则化策略。

Conclusion: 提出了一种名为CoVON的持续优化器，通过平滑后验更新机制显著提升了深度网络在持续学习场景下的性能，证明了在VCL框架内引入快慢适应机制的有效性。

Abstract: Continual learning remains a major challenge for modern deep networks, partly because commonly used optimizers lack inherent mechanisms for continual adaptation. One such natural mechanism is fast and slow adaptation to balance stability and plasticity. This mechanism has deep roots in neuroscience and biology, but there is no consensus on how to best incorporate it in commonly used optimizers. Here, we show that this can be easily done via the VCL framework, where past posteriors are used as priors in the future. Our key idea is to incorporate slow adaptation via merging of past posteriors to slow down the drift in the knowledge as learning progresses. The merged posterior is then used as the prior in the VCL update to implement the fast-weight updates. These steps can be seamlessly implemented in the IVON optimizer, whose form and costs are nearly identical to that of Adam. We call this new optimizer the Continual IVON (CoVON) optimizer and show that it not only consistently improves over existing VCL optimizers, but also performs better than other weight-regularization strategies across domain-incremental learning, continual pre-training, and fine-tuning of large language models.

</details>


### [147] [You Don't Need to Run Every Eval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24020)
*Yuchen Zeng,Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明大语言模型的评测基准存在极高冗余，仅需5个代表性基准测试便能准确预测模型在百余项测试中的表现，大幅降低了评估开销。作者为此提出了BenchPress补全工具与最优基准子集方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代模型发布时通常会进行繁琐的大规模基准评估，用于衡量训练进度、比较方案和决策。作者旨在回答一个核心问题：是否真的需要运行每一个基准测试，或者是否存在一种更高效的评估策略来减少冗余工作。

Method: 1. 构建了一个包含84个前沿模型和133个基准测试的评分矩阵。
2. 通过矩阵分析发现该矩阵呈秩-2（rank-2）特性。
3. 提出BenchPress：一种基于Logit空间的秩-2矩阵补全方法，并添加了可信度评估层。
4. 基于BenchPress算法识别出两组核心基准测试子集，用于替代全量评估。

Result: 1. 证明了前沿模型的评分矩阵约为秩-2，即模型在所有基准测试上的表现主要由两个潜在因子决定。
2. 开发了BenchPress，能以平均4.6分的误差恢复缺失分数。
3. 识别出两套精简的5个基准测试子集，能以极高精度（3.93-4.55分误差）预测模型的完整得分矩阵。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在数百个基准测试中的表现存在高度线性相关性，仅需极少数核心基准测试（5个）即可准确推断模型在其余大规模基准测试上的表现，从而极大地降低了模型评估的计算成本和复杂性。

Abstract: A modern model release reports scores on 40+ benchmarks and the same evaluations were run many more times before it: to track training progress, compare design choices, and select the checkpoint for the release. But do we need to run every eval? We compile a public score matrix of 84 frontier models on 133 benchmarks (2,604 cells, 23.3% filled) and find it is approximately rank-2: a model's scores across all 133 benchmarks are largely determined by just two numbers. We confirm this in two ways: scores hidden from the matrix are best recovered using two factors, and two factors already explain over 90% of the variation among models on the benchmarks they share. Building on this, we design BenchPress: a logit-space rank-2 matrix completion method that recovers held-out scores to within 4.6 points, and a confidence layer that says when each prediction can be trusted. Using BenchPress, we find a subset of five benchmarks {GPQA-D, HLE, Codeforces, MMLU-Pro, ARC-AGI-1} that can recover the rest of a model's public scorecard to within 3.93 points. For a tighter inference budget, a cheaper set {GPQA-D, MMLU-Pro, Aider Polyglot, MATH-500, AIME 2026} can predict a model's evals to within 4.55. We release the score matrix, the BenchPress code, and an interactive tool that predicts any model's score on any benchmark.

</details>


### [148] [Information-Theoretic Classifier-Free Guidance with Adaptive Schedule Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24025)
*Haobo Chen,Xiangxiang Xu,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对扩散模型中 CFG 权衡一致性与多样性的问题，提出了一种信息论驱动的动态调度优化方法，实现了更佳的生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型中广泛使用的无分类器引导（CFG）虽然能提升条件一致性，但增加引导权重会导致多样性和分布覆盖率下降；目前缺乏针对反向轨迹中如何优化此权衡的系统研究。

Method: 提出了一种基于信息论的调度优化框架，引入清晰的端点参考（clean endpoint reference）来设定一致性与覆盖率的平衡目标；通过推导轨迹层面的公式，利用样本和分数评估对损失函数进行估计，从而避开了复杂的显式密度估计过程。

Result: 在 ImageNet-512（使用 EDM-XXL 模型）和 COCO（使用 SD-XL 模型）数据集上的实验证明，该方法学习到的动态调度策略在权衡一致性与覆盖率方面表现优于传统的固定权重策略。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种基于信息论的 CFG 调度优化框架，通过在反向扩散过程中动态分配引导权重，实现了生成质量（一致性）与多样性（覆盖率）之间更优的平衡。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved strong performance in image, text-to-image, and video generation, where conditional generation is often controlled by classifier-free guidance (CFG). CFG improves condition consistency by increasing a guidance weight, but stronger guidance typically reduces diversity and distributional coverage. It remains unclear how this consistency-coverage trade-off should be controlled across the reverse trajectory, since the distribution induced by CFG is not simply the fixed-time tilted distribution given by the guided score field. To address this issue, we propose an information-theoretic framework for CFG schedule optimization. Our approach uses a clean endpoint reference to specify the desired consistency-coverage trade-off, while optimizing the actual distribution induced by the guided sampler toward this reference. We derive trajectory-level formulas to estimate the objective from samples and score evaluations, avoiding explicit density estimation. On ImageNet-512 with EDM-XXL and COCO with SD-XL, the learned schedules achieve competitive or improved trade-offs over constant guidance and allocate guidance selectively across noise levels.

</details>


### [149] [RoPE-Aware Bit Allocation for KV-Cache Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24033)
*Fengfeng Liang,Yuechen Zhang,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Block-GTQ 是一种 RoPE 感知的 KV Cache 量化技术。它通过将 key 向量分解为频率块并进行自适应位宽分配，解决了传统量化方法忽略 RoPE 结构敏感性的问题，显著提升了长上下文任务的表现并实现了高效的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 KV Cache 量化技术通常将 key 向量视为平坦结构，忽略了 RoPE 编码中 key 对注意力 logit 的贡献是基于二维频率块分布的，导致量化过程中对高能感知的 RoPE 块分配不足，进而影响模型在长上下文任务中的性能。

Method: 提出 Block-GTQ 方法。该方法利用 RoPE 的二维频率块特性，基于 TurboQuant-MSE (TQ-MSE) 计算无标签能量得分，并采用贪婪算法根据边际收益动态分配各块的比特宽度（而非传统的平坦处理），最后实现 packed-cache 服务加速。

Result: 1. 精度提升：在 K/V 位宽匹配下，将每层 MAE 降低 32-80%。在 Llama-3.1-8B 和 DeepSeek-R1 等模型上，长上下文检索（NIAH）和推理任务表现显著优于均匀量化。2. 系统效率：在 Qwen2.5-3B 上实现 3.24x 压缩，推理速度比 fp16 FlashAttention2 快 1.34 倍，显存占用显著减少，并支持 512K 超长上下文。

Conclusion: Block-GTQ 通过感知 RoPE 的结构化特性，实现了高效的 KV Cache 量化。该方法不仅在精度上显著优于均匀量化，而且通过位宽调整实现了极高的缓存压缩比，使得超长上下文（如 256K-512K）推理在单 GPU 上变得可行且高效。

Abstract: Existing low-bit KV-cache quantizers often treat each cached key as a flat vector. Under RoPE, however, a key's contribution to a future attention logit decomposes into a position-dependent sum over two-dimensional frequency blocks. This makes key-cache quantization a block-wise bit-allocation problem: high-energy RoPE blocks are more sensitive to quantization error and should receive more bits. We introduce Block-GTQ, a RoPE-aware bit allocator for key-cache quantization built on TurboQuant-MSE(TQ-MSE). For each layer and KV head, Block-GTQ computes a label-free energy score for each RoPE block and greedily allocates integer bit widths by marginal gain. Under matched K/V bit budgets, Block-GTQ better preserves RoPE query-key logits on a ten-model diagnostic panel, cutting per-layer MAE by 32-80% at 2 and 3 b/dim K-only quantization and winning all 367/367 layer comparisons against uniform TQ-MSE. These fidelity gains translate to stronger downstream long-context retrieval, understanding, and reasoning. At K2V2 on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, Block-GTQ raises the six-task NIAH average from 70.6 to 97.4, and the LongBench-EN average from 36.87 to 53.31. On AIME 2024/2025 with DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B, without an fp16 recent-key buffer, Block-GTQ at K3V2 scores 51.7/37.5, close to fp16's 54.2/37.9, whereas uniform TQ-MSE collapses to 0.0/0.0. We further implement a packed-cache serving path. On a single H800 GPU with Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, packed K3V3 achieves 3.24x KV-cache compression with fp16-comparable quality, runs 1.34x faster than fp16 FlashAttention2 at 128K context, reduces peak memory from 56.31 GB to 19.85 GB, and remains feasible at 256K and 512K where fp16 OOMs. Code is available at https://github.com/JIA-Lab-research/blockgtq.

</details>


### [150] [Rapid FinFET Modelling Using an Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24046)
*Amit Sarkar Suman Sau,Swagata Mandal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于自编码器（AE）的FinFET机器学习建模框架，通过将VDS作为输入特征压缩I-V曲线至潜在空间，实现了高效的器件特性建模与关键参数（如VTH、SS、gm）的直接提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FinFET器件建模计算复杂度高且耗时，需要一种能够利用数据驱动方法实现高效、准确建模及关键参数提取的替代方案，以加速器件表征与电路仿真流程。

Method: 1. 利用BSIM-CMG模型生成FinFET的ID-VG特征数据集；2. 构建自编码器（Autoencoder）架构，将完整的I-V曲线压缩至低维潜在空间（Latent Space）；3. 将漏源电压（VDS）作为显式输入特征，以捕捉偏置相关的非线性变化；4. 通过解码器重建I-V曲线并直接提取关键器件参数（VTH, SS, gm）。

Result: 所提框架能够以少量训练数据实现对FinFET I-V曲线的高精度重建，并准确提取包括阈值电压（VTH）、亚阈值摆幅（SS）和峰值跨导（gm）在内的关键器件物理指标。

Conclusion: 基于自编码器的数据驱动紧凑模型是FinFET器件建模的高效方案，能够实现快速的器件表征和电路级仿真，且具有高精度和低数据需求特性。

Abstract: This work presents a machine learning framework that leverages an autoencoder (AE) for the efficient modeling of FinFET. We first calibrated a BSIM-CMG model to generate a dataset of current-voltage (ID-VG) characteristics. This data was used to train an autoencoder that compresses full I-V curves into a low-dimensional latent space, which intrinsically encodes key device physics. A key innovation is the explicit incorporation of parameter such as drain to source voltage (VDS) as an input feature, enhancing the model ability to capture bias dependent variation. The trained model successfully reconstructs full I-V curves and directly extracts critical device metrics including threshold voltage (VTH), subthreshold slope (SS), and peak transconductance (gm). This approach demonstrates that data driven compact models, built from actual characterization data, can achieve high accuracy with minimal training data, providing a powerful tool for rapid device characterization, modelling and circuit level simulation.

</details>


### [151] [RAVEN: A Regime-Aware Variable-context Expert Network for Financial Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24062)
*Cheng He,Zhenyu Guan,Xijie Liang,Defu Lian,Jiajia Li,Enhong Chen,Patrick P. C. Lee,Geng Hu,Zehao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对金融时间序列“低信噪比”和“非平稳性”挑战，本文提出了RAVEN模型，通过自适应选择不同长度的时间窗口进行专家混合建模，显著超越了现有固定窗口预测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SOTA时间序列模型在金融预测中表现不佳，主要原因是固定上下文窗口无法适应价格过程的非平稳性和随时间变化的最佳回看长度。

Method: 提出RAVEN架构：1. 采用嵌套窗口层级结构，通过累积重要性阈值（CIT）动态路由；2. 使用全局压缩表示（GCR）维持全局一致性；3. 引入相关性感知加权（CAW）处理变长专家输出的重叠与相似性问题。

Result: 在HS300和S&P500指数预测中，Pearson相关性分别提升了9.2%和20.2%；在基金销售预测中MSE降低了18.2%；在PEMS交通数据集的16项指标中有14项达到最优。

Conclusion: RAVEN通过自适应确定时间窗口长度，有效解决了金融时间序列中非平稳性和动态依赖性的问题，相较于传统固定窗口模型在预测精度上取得了显著提升，且在金融和交通领域展现出通用性。

Abstract: Financial time series forecasting presents structural challenges absent from standard benchmarks. Log-returns are non-stationary, exhibit exceptionally low signal-to-noise (SNR) ratios, and are governed by regime-dependent temporal dependencies. We identify a key limitation of state-of-the-art (SOTA) time series models in financial settings. A fixed context window is mismatched to the time-varying optimal look-back of non-stationary price processes. We propose the Regime-Aware Variable-context Expert Network (RAVEN), a Mixture-of-Experts framework designed to adaptively determine the temporal context for each input sample. Instead of relying on a fixed look-back horizon, RAVEN constructs a hierarchy of nested contiguous windows whose lengths are determined by the data itself. Specifically, RAVEN scores patches by learned importance in reverse chronological order and applies the Cumulative Importance Thresholding (CIT) mechanism to derive nested prefix windows, each routed to a scale-specialized expert. A Global Compressed Representation (GCR) branch runs in parallel over the full context, preserving global temporal coherence that local experts cannot guarantee. Because the nested routing induces structured overlap among expert inputs, we introduce a Correlation-Aware Weighting (CAW) to align variable-length expert outputs and penalize pairwise cosine similarity prior to aggregation. Experiments on cumulative log-return prediction (HS300, S&P500) and fund sales forecasting demonstrate that RAVEN achieves SOTA performances, improves Pearson correlation by 9.2% on HS300 and 20.2% on S&P500, and reduces MSE by 18.2% on fund sales forecasting, while achieving the best results in 14 of 16 metrics on four PEMS traffic benchmarks.

</details>


### [152] [Blockwise Policy-Drift Gating for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24084)
*Liwen Zheng,Haiyun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对策略蒸馏（OPD）在长程推理中的性能不稳定问题，提出了一种轻量级的“分块策略漂移门控”机制，通过动态重加权损失函数有效提升了模型重用轨迹训练的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长程推理任务中，基于采样标记（sampled-token）的策略蒸馏（OPD）常面临脆弱性问题；通过利用重用轨迹（rollout reuse）进行稳定训练成为了关键挑战。

Method: 提出了一种“分块策略漂移门控”（Blockwise Policy-Drift Gating）方法。该方法通过计算行为策略（behavior student）与当前更新策略（current student）在采样路径上的对数概率偏移，并将其聚合为分块的、均值归一化的门控权重，进而对策略蒸馏（OPD）位置损失进行重加权，而无需修改教师目标或采样策略。

Result: 在 Qwen3 数学推理基准测试中，采用 64-token 分块门控机制实现了 0.4978 到 0.5160 的 pass@8 平均分提升，并在 AIME24、AIME25、MATH500 和 AMC23 等多个基准测试中优于现有的 Teacher-TopK/LSM 方法。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了局部“旧-当前”策略漂移（old-current policy drift）是利用重用轨迹进行策略蒸馏的有效控制信号，且基于块（blockwise）的门控机制是提升求解鲁棒性的简单有效默认方案。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student policy using teacher signals computed on trajectories sampled by the student itself. Recent work shows that sampled-token OPD can be fragile on long-horizon reasoning tasks and that local teacher-support matching is a simple and effective repair. This paper introduces blockwise policy-drift gating, a lightweight student-only old-current drift controller for OPD under rollout reuse. The method computes log-probability shifts between the behavior student and the current student on the sampled token path, aggregates these shifts over fixed blocks or spans, and uses the resulting detached, mean-normalized gates to reweight OPD position losses. It does not change teacher targets, teacher top-K supports, or the rollout policy. In a six-variant Qwen3 math reasoning benchmark with a uniform 200-step training budget for all trained variants, we use pass@8 as the primary problem-level solve-rate metric. Fixed 64-token block gating improves sampled-token OPD mean pass@8 from 0.4978 to 0.5160 across AIME24, AIME25, MATH500, and AMC23. On Teacher-TopK/LSM, Block64 gives the best four-benchmark mean pass@8 among trained students. The results identify local old-current policy drift as a practical control signal for reused OPD rollouts and motivate block-level gating as a simple default for improving solve-rate robustness.

</details>


### [153] [NeuroSonic: Conditional Flow Matching for EEG-to-Speech Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24087)
*Wenhao Gao,Yifan Wang,Yijia Ma,Carl Yang,Wen Li,Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroSonic 是一种基于条件流匹配的新型 EEG 语音重构模型，通过学习确定性概率流场取代随机采样，显著提升了语音重构的真实性、频谱保真度和抗干扰能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电信号（EEG）空间弥散、弱信号且变异性大，与语音信号的高相干性之间存在巨大鸿沟。直接进行波形回归或使用传统的随机去噪方法容易导致模型不稳定，易受伪影干扰及个体差异影响。

Method: 提出 NeuroSonic 模型，采用条件流匹配（Conditional Flow-Matching）框架。核心包括将脑电与音频映射至共享 token 空间，并使用时间条件门控 Transformer 对预测确定性概率流速度场（Velocity Field）的常微分方程进行参数化，从而实现从噪声状态到干净语音的转换。

Result: 在 CineBrain 和 EAV 基准测试中，NeuroSonic 在跨受试者评估中表现优于现有的 GAN、扩散模型及均流基线，整体感知质量提升高达 26.3%，在伪影较多的片段中表现尤为稳健。

Conclusion: 确定性条件传输（Deterministic Conditional Transport）为脑电驱动的语音重构提供了一种稳定且高效的架构，通过建模轨迹演变显著缓解了传统方法的性能波动和伪影敏感性。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous speech from scalp electroencephalography (EEG) remains fundamentally challenging. EEG provides a weak, spatially diffuse, and highly variable measurement of distributed cortical activity, whereas speech is organized as a coherent acoustic trajectory with strong harmonic and temporal structure. The resulting mismatch makes waveform regression unstable and causes stochastic multi-step generation to be sensitive to artifact-dependent conditioning and subject variability. We introduce NeuroSonic, a conditional flow-matching framework for EEG-to-speech reconstruction. Instead of predicting waveforms directly or refining them through stochastic denoising, NeuroSonic learns a deterministic probability-flow velocity field that transports a noise-corrupted acoustic state toward clean speech under EEG conditioning. EEG and audio are embedded into a shared token space and processed by a time-conditioned gated Transformer that parameterizes the transport ordinary differential equation. This formulation models trajectory evolution explicitly while avoiding iterative stochastic sampling. We evaluate NeuroSonic on the CineBrain and EAV benchmarks under cross-subject evaluation. Across both datasets, the proposed method improves distributional realism, spectral fidelity, and perceptual quality over representative GAN-, diffusion-, and mean-flow baselines, with up to a 26.3\% gain in overall perceptual quality. The performance gap is most evident in artifact-heavy segments, where conditioning variability is strongest. These findings indicate that deterministic conditional transport provides a stable and effective formulation for EEG-driven speech reconstruction. Code is available at https://github.com/Y-Research-SBU/NeuroSonic/ .

</details>


### [154] [FedUP: One-Shot Federated Unlearning via Centroid-Guided Plug-in Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24113)
*Feihong Nan,Zhengyi Zhong,Pan Wang,Weidong Bao,Xiongtao Zhang,Quan Wen,Ji Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedUP 是一种新型联邦遗忘框架，通过服务端训练轻量级过滤器实现“一键”去中心化遗忘，在极短时间内完成遗忘任务的同时，兼顾了高性能、低延迟与可逆性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有联邦遗忘（FU）方法中存在的非目标知识损失严重、请求延迟高以及通信开销大等问题，以满足“被遗忘权”的法律需求。

Method: 提出 FedUP 框架，冻结原始模型参数，在服务端使用经过差分隐私（DP）保护的类中心样本训练轻量级“知识漏斗”过滤器，从而过滤目标数据并保持原模型性能。

Result: 将遗忘延迟从分钟级降至秒级，实现了极高的遗忘精度和效率，并支持通过移除过滤器无损恢复知识，在多项图像和文本任务中表现优异。

Conclusion: FedUP 通过引入轻量级可插拔过滤器，有效地解决了联邦学习中遗忘机制的性能损失与延迟困境，为去中心化系统的高效合规提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Federated unlearning (FU) is critical for complying with legal mandates like the right to be forgotten in decentralized systems, yet current methods face a persistent dilemma between non-target knowledge loss and high request latency. To resolve these issues, we propose FedUP, a one-shot federated unlearning framework utilizing lightweight pluggable filters that act as a "knowledge funnel" to screen out target data while preserving original model performance. By freezing original model parameters and training filters at the server side using differentially private (DP)-protected class centroid samples, FedUP bypasses the need for multi-round client-server communication and complex retraining, reducing unlearning latency from minutes to mere seconds. Additionally, the framework's pluggable architecture ensures inherent reversibility, enabling the seamless restoration of forgotten knowledge by simply removing the filters. Extensive experiments on diverse image and text tasks demonstrate that FedUP effectively reduces non-target knowledge loss and achieves superior unlearning precision and efficiency across various scenarios. Code is available at: https://github.com/suows/FedUP-code.

</details>


### [155] [When Top-1 Fails: Calibrating LoRA Monitors for Masked Diffusion LMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24119)
*Lucky Verma,Pratik Yadav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现传统的 top-1 argmax 浓度指标对 DLM 的 PEFT 微调崩溃预警完全失效，并提出将 max LoRA 梯度范数作为捕捉训练早期不稳定性的有效诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散扩散语言模型 (DLM) 诊断方法（如 top-1 argmax 浓度）在 PEFT 微调场景下的有效性尚未经过实证检验，且存在误报率极高的问题。

Method: 设计了包含 816 种 LoRA/PEFT 配置的实验，对比了 top-1 argmax 浓度与 max LoRA 梯度范数在识别训练崩溃中的效能；使用了回归控制以验证指标的有效范围。

Result: Top-1 argmax 浓度在 816 次实验中误报率为 100%（Precision=0），是无效的预警指标；而 max LoRA 梯度范数在 LLaDA 模型族中表现良好，能够以 0.68 的精度和 0.79 的 F1 分数识别出高损失配置。

Conclusion: top-1 argmax 浓度在离散扩散语言模型 (DLM) 的 PEFT 微调中无法作为训练崩溃的预警指标；相较之下，LoRA 梯度范数最大值是一个更有效的早期监控信号，但其阈值需要针对特定模型系列进行校准。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language model (DLM) fine-tuning inherits inexpensive diagnostics from denoising-time confidence monitors, but their PEFT-training meaning is untested. We test top-1 argmax concentration as a collapse warning. Across 816 LoRA/PEFT configurations from three DLM families, the warning fires for every configuration while logs record 0/816 actual collapses at the 200 step horizon, giving zero precision. The cause is pre-equilibrium saturation: top-1 concentration is already high before optimization and quickly becomes insensitive to final training stability. We then evaluate max LoRA gradient norm, a parameter-side signal that samples gradient routing rather than token concentration. On a pooled held-out LLaDA-family split, a train-optimized threshold identifies top-decile final-loss configurations with precision 0.68 and F1=0.79, above the all-positive top-1 baseline even at the lower split-bootstrap confidence bound. Autoregressive controls and cross-family threshold failures bound the result to short-horizon DLM-LoRA inspection rather than a universal collapse detector. Workflow: drop top-1 as a PEFT alarm, log max-gradient early in training, and calibrate thresholds per DLM family before routing runs for inspection.

</details>


### [156] [Holistic Data Scheduler for LLM Pre-training via Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24133)
*Chenhao Dang,Jing Ma,Mingjie Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 HDS 的在线数据混合框架，利用强化学习（SAC）和多维度奖励函数，有效优化了 LLM 预训练的数据组成，大幅提升了训练效率与模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线数据混合（ODM）方法多基于单一优化视角，忽视了大模型预训练中对动态数据组合的多维度需求。

Method: 提出 Holistic Data Scheduler (HDS) 框架，将数据调度建模为连续控制空间的强化学习问题，采用 Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) 算法，并设计了集数据质量、跨域影响（Loss）和权重范数（模型驱动）于一体的多目标奖励函数。

Result: 在 The Pile 基准上，相比最优竞品，HDS 减少了 44% 的训练迭代次数；在 MMLU 0-shot 任务中准确率提升了 7.2%，并在其他基准测试中表现出一致的正向收益。

Conclusion: HDS 框架通过多维度、多目标的在线数据调度策略，在显著提升训练效率的同时，增强了 LLM 的最终模型能力，证明了在预训练中考虑多维数据动态组合的必要性与有效性。

Abstract: The composition of training data, governed by the diversity of sources and their mixing strategy, is a cornerstone of Large Language Model (LLM) pre-training. Online Data Mixing (ODM), the technique of adaptively adjusting data mixtures during training, has emerged as a promising direction to improve efficiency. However, existing methods are constrained by their reliance on a singular optimization perspective, which fundamentally overlooks the need for complex LLM pre-training to consider the dynamic data composition from multiple dimensions. To overcome this limitation, we introduce the Holistic Data Scheduler (HDS), a novel online data mixing framework. HDS formulates the data scheduling challenge as a reinforcement learning problem in a continuous control space and leverages the Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) algorithm for its stability and sample efficiency in exploring the high-dimensional policy space. At the core of HDS lies a novel multi-objective, holistic reward function that integrates three critical perspectives: a data-driven reward for quality, a loss-driven reward capturing inter-domain influence, and a model-driven reward based on weight norms. To validate our design and determine its optimal configuration, we conducted systematic experiments on LLMs of various sizes. On The Pile benchmark, HDS reaches the final validation perplexity of the next best method with 44% fewer training iterations. Furthermore, it achieves a 7.2% improvement on the MMLU 0-shot task along with consistent gains on other benchmarks, showcasing its ability to enhance both training efficiency and final model capability.

</details>


### [157] [A Time-Reparameterized Cumulative Intensity Extrapolation Sampler for Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24140)
*Feiyang Fu,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 TR-CIE 的离散流匹配采样器，通过时间重参数化和累积强度外推策略，在保持原始 NFE 开销的同时，显著提升了低推理步数下的生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散流匹配（DFM）在采样时常用 $\tau$-leaping 等离散化方法，但在有限函数评估（NFE）次数下的采样效率和质量仍有待提升。

Method: 提出了时间重参数化累积强度外推（TR-CIE）采样器，包含两个核心组件：1. 基于噪声调度的时间重参数化（解决采样末期的刚性问题）；2. 累积强度外推更新规则（利用历史缓存优化步长估计）。

Result: 理论上证明了该方法具有局部近似误差界和收敛性；实验表明，在合成任务、文本生成及文生图基准测试中，相比标准 $\tau$-leaping，TR-CIE 在有限 NFE 下表现出更好的采样质量。

Conclusion: TR-CIE 采样器在不增加额外模型评估（NFE）开销的前提下，通过针对性优化时间网格和累积强度估计，显著提升了离散流匹配模型在采样阶段的效率与质量。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching (DFM) provides a principled framework for generative modeling on discrete state spaces via continuous-time Markov chain dynamics. In practice, sampling for DFM commonly employs discretizations such as $τ$-leaping, yet efficient sampling methods under a limited number of function evaluations (NFE) remain less studied. To address this gap, we propose the Time-Reparameterized Cumulative Intensity Extrapolation (TR-CIE) sampler, which aims to improve sampling quality when function evaluations are restricted. TR-CIE consists of two components. First, a schedule-based time reparameterization rescales the time grid according to the noise schedule. Under standard factorized DFM rate parameterizations, this transformation of variables absorbs the schedule-dependent growth term and mitigates stiffness near the terminal sampling stage. Second, we introduce a cumulative-intensity extrapolation updating rule. By reusing cached model outputs from the previous step as a history term, this improves the approximation of stepwise cumulative intensities on the resulting non-uniform time grid. We provide a theoretical analysis that bounds the local approximation error of cumulative intensities and establishes convergence results. The resulting sampler requires one NFE per step and introduces no additional model evaluations compared to the standard $τ$-leaping sampler. Extensive experiments on synthetic tasks, text generation, and text-to-image benchmarks demonstrate that our method improves sampling quality under limited NFE.

</details>


### [158] [AsyncOPD: How Stale Can On-Policy Distillation Be?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24143)
*Wonjun Kang,Kevin Galim,Seunghyuk Oh,Minjun Kang,Sanghyun Park,Donghoon Kim,Minjae Lee,Minseo Kim,Rishabh Tiwari,Yuchen Zeng,Hyung Il Koo,Kangwook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对异步策略蒸馏（AsyncOPD）中过时数据影响性能的问题，本文系统研究了 KL 散度选择、重新计算代价及估计器偏差，并构建了 AsyncOPD 系统，实现了吞吐量的大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 后训练中的在策略蒸馏（OPD）存在严重的计算瓶颈，而引入异步训练流水线虽能改善吞吐量，但会引入“过时策略数据（stale-policy data）”的问题，目前学界对异步 OPD 中该问题的理解尚不深入。

Method: 1. 对比分析前向 KL 与反向 KL 在处理过时数据（stale data）时的表现差异；2. 针对反向 KL 的脆弱性，提出在学习器端重新计算（recompute）梯度信号的方案；3. 针对受限的教师分数缓存，提出多样本蒙特卡洛（MC）估计器以处理偏差-方差权衡；4. 构建了完整的异步训练流水线 AsyncOPD。

Result: 实验证明 AsyncOPD 在训练吞吐量上实现了 1.6 倍至 3.8 倍的提升，且最终模型精度与同步训练水平相当。

Conclusion: 本文提出了 AsyncOPD 框架，通过采用对 stale policy 更鲁棒的 KL 散度选择策略、再计算（recomputing）机制以及多样本蒙特卡洛估计，成功解决了异步策略蒸馏（OPD）中的过时数据失效问题，在保持精度的前提下显著提升了训练吞吐量。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own rollouts guided by teacher feedback and is becoming increasingly important for large language model (LLM) post-training. Like reinforcement learning (RL), however, OPD faces an on-policy systems bottleneck, as rollouts can dominate training time for reasoning workloads. Asynchronous training pipelines can alleviate this bottleneck by decoupling rollout generation from learner updates, but doing so introduces stale-policy data. While prior work has studied stale data in asynchronous RL, its effects in OPD remain underexplored. We present the first systematic study of staleness in asynchronous OPD, focusing on a practical setting where teacher feedback is implemented through local KL losses and full-vocabulary teacher logits are too expensive to store or transfer, necessitating finite teacher-score caches. We first show that KL direction changes the stale-data problem: teacher-weighted forward KL is more robust to stale rollouts, whereas student-weighted reverse KL is vulnerable. Second, for this vulnerable reverse-KL case, we study whether methods designed to stabilize asynchronous RL can mitigate OPD staleness. In our experiments, they do not improve over a simpler OPD-specific surrogate: recomputing the reverse-KL signal under the current student at learner time. Third, we analyze how finite teacher-score caches create a bias-variance tradeoff for sparse and sampled reverse-KL OPD estimators. This motivates multi-sample Monte Carlo (MC), which preserves MC correctability while reducing one-sample variance. Finally, we present and open-source AsyncOPD, a fully asynchronous OPD training pipeline built from these estimator choices. Experiments show that AsyncOPD improves training throughput by $1.6\times$ to $3.8\times$ over strict synchronous training while reaching comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [159] [Lightweight Transformer Models for On-Device Fault Detection: A Benchmark Study on Resource-Constrained Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24173)
*Disha Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究对比了传统ML与轻量级Transformer在边缘故障检测中的表现，发现尽管有量化和自适应推理优化，轻量级Transformer在模型容量和延迟上仍远逊于传统算法，且两者在处理极度不平衡数据时均表现受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的边缘设备上部署故障检测模型时，需要在准确性、延迟和模型大小之间取得平衡，但目前对于轻量级Transformer与传统机器学习方法在该场景下的对比研究尚不充分。

Method: 在NASA C-MAPSS、SECOM和UCI AI4I 2020三个公开数据集上，对比了传统机器学习方法（RF、XGBoost等）与轻量级Transformer架构（DistilBERT、TinyBERT等），评估指标涵盖F1-score、AUC、模型大小及推理延迟，并测试了INT8动态量化与两阶段自适应推理流水线技术。

Result: 在C-MAPSS数据集上，轻量级Transformer的性能与传统ML相当，但模型大小和延迟分别高出100倍和9000倍；TinyBERT-4L表现最佳（55 MB，18 ms）；INT8量化可显著减小模型体积且性能损耗低；自适应推理管道有效地平衡了精度与速度。所有模型在不平衡数据集上表现均较差。

Conclusion: 轻量级Transformer在资源受限设备上仍面临巨大的开销挑战。虽然INT8量化和两阶段自适应推理管道能改善性能，但针对极度不平衡的数据集，传统ML与Transformer模型均表现不足，仍有待开发更具针对性的解决方案。

Abstract: On-device fault detection enables real-time diagnostics without cloud dependency, but deploying machine learning models on resource-constrained hardware demands careful tradeoffs between accuracy, latency, and model size. We present a benchmark comparing traditional ML methods (Random Forest, XGBoost, SVM, Logistic Regression) against lightweight transformer architectures (DistilBERT, TinyBERT-6L, TinyBERT-4L, MobileBERT) for binary fault detection across three public datasets: NASA C-MAPSS turbofan degradation, SECOM semiconductor manufacturing, and UCI AI4I 2020 predictive maintenance. We evaluate classification performance (F1-score, AUC), model size, and CPU inference latency, and further assess INT8 dynamic quantization and a two-stage adaptive inference pipeline. Our results reveal that on well-separated sensor data (C-MAPSS), lightweight transformers match traditional ML at 87.8% F1 but at 100x the model size and 9000x the latency. TinyBERT-4L emerges as the most deployment-friendly transformer at 55 MB and 18 ms CPU latency. INT8 quantization reduces size by 25% while preserving 86.9% F1. Our adaptive pipeline, routing 97.9% of predictions through a quantized triage model and only 2.1% to a larger expert, achieves 87.6% F1 at 19.5 ms average latency. On severely imbalanced datasets (SECOM, UCI-PM), both traditional and transformer methods struggle significantly, highlighting fundamental limitations of current approaches for extreme class imbalance in fault detection. All code is publicly available.

</details>


### [160] [Project Ariadne: Prompt-Conditioned Route Generation for Synthesis Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24184)
*Anton Morgunov,Victor S. Batista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Ariadne 是一种新型多任务逆合成路线生成模型，通过单一的提示词驱动架构，实现了对深度和原料约束的灵活控制，并显著提升了生成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逆合成规划器计算成本高或需要专用算法，而现有的序列生成方法通常需要针对不同约束训练多个独立模型，缺乏通用性和灵活性。

Method: 提出了一种基于解码器（decoder-only）的架构 Ariadne，将目标分子、规划约束条件（如路线深度、特定的起始原料）以及合成路线整合为单一的提示词-补全（prompt-completion）序列进行生成。

Result: 在 RetroCast/PaRoutes 基准测试中，Ariadne 通过增加约束提示词显著提升了指标（Solv-0 深度约束提升 13.7 分，原料约束提升 31.2 分）；推理效率优于 DESP 和 DMS Explorer XL，并在路线留出（route-holdout）测试中表现出色。

Conclusion: Ariadne 展示了将逆合成规划从专用模型转向提示词条件（prompt-conditioned）结构化路线生成的可行性，证明了单个模型可以通过提示词执行多种约束规划任务。尽管在某些指标上超越了现有规划器，但距离实际化学实验应用仍需结合更成熟的路线验证工具。

Abstract: Retrosynthetic planning seeks to connect a target molecule to commercially available starting materials through a multistep route. Classical planners construct such routes by iteratively applying single-step reaction models within a search procedure; constrained variants often require specialized algorithms or architectural changes. Direct route generation reframes retrosynthesis as sequence generation, but existing direct-generation methods still train separate models for different planning specifications. We introduce Ariadne, a decoder-only route generator that represents the target, optional constraints, and route in one prompt-completion sequence. On the RetroCast/PaRoutes mkt-cnv-160 benchmark family, one 24-layer checkpoint follows route-depth and required-starting-material prompts: adding the corresponding prompt fields raises Solv-0 by 13.7 points for depth constraints and 31.2 points for required-leaf constraints. Ariadne also improves over DESP, a bidirectional search planner, on required-leaf Top-10 and Solv-0 in 24 GPU-minutes versus 6.8 GPU-hours. On standard reconstruction, Ariadne is comparable to DMS Explorer XL at about half the reported inference time. Across additional target-only benchmarks, Ariadne's clearest gains are on route-holdout reconstruction, whereas AiZynthFinder MCTS remains stronger on several Solv-0 comparisons. These results extend sequence generation from specialist retrosynthesis models to prompt-conditioned structural route generation. We release the codebase and training scripts to support further work, but do not introduce Tier-1--3 route checkers; those remain the main bottleneck before models of this kind can become useful to experimental chemists.

</details>


### [161] [Managing Task Execution for Unknown Workloads in Batteryless IoT: A Hardware-Agnostic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24340)
*Samer Nasser,Henrique Duarte Moura,Ritesh Kumar Singh,Maarten Weyn,Jeroen Famaey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对无电池物联网设备，本文提出两种无需预设能量信息的动态调度策略（RL与AP），并证明了其在资源受限环境下的鲁棒性，同时指出大缓存设备仍适用轻量级静态策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网正向无电池能量采集架构转型，但传统调度器依赖静态阈值或预设配置文件，难以应对复杂边缘应用中波动的能源供应与不可预测的工作负载。

Method: 提出了两种与硬件无关的“黑盒”动态调度策略：1) 基于无模型强化学习（RL）的智能体；2) 实时近似预测（AP）方法。利用真实世界太阳能数据和LoRa传输剖面进行仿真对比评估。

Result: 三种策略各具优劣：AP方法轻量且吞吐量接近最优；RL方案在生存能力与任务执行之间提供了可调平衡；AsTAR方案擅长长周期能源间隙中的执行速度调节。

Conclusion: 无需追求普适性的最优方案，应根据系统的能量缓存大小选择策略：资源严苛系统需采用RL或AP等动态策略以保障鲁棒性，而拥有较大能量缓冲的设备则可继续采用低计算开销的静态策略。

Abstract: In recent years, the Internet of Things (IoT) paradigm has been shifting toward batteryless, energy-harvesting architectures. Sustaining reliable operation in these systems requires intelligent management of highly volatile stored energy. As edge applications grow in complexity, traditional energy-aware schedulers struggle with unpredictable workloads due to their reliance on static execution thresholds or pre-measured, hardware-specific task profiles. To overcome this, we propose two novel, hardware-agnostic dynamic scheduling strategies treating applications as a "black box," requiring no prior energy information: a model-free Reinforcement Learning (RL) agent and an on-the-fly Approximated Prediction (AP) method. We evaluate these methods against an adaptive task rate approach (AsTAR) and optimized static thresholds using a custom-built, physically accurate simulation framework driven by real-world solar data and dynamic LoRa transmission profiles. Rather than claiming universal superiority, our analysis exposes the distinct operational trade-offs of each method: the AP approach delivers lightweight, near-oracle task throughput; the RL agent provides tunable survival-execution balancing; and AsTAR excels at execution pacing across long energy gaps. Finally, we demonstrate that while these advanced strategies provide critical resilience for severely constrained systems with small capacitors, devices with larger energy buffers can efficiently rely on simpler, less computationally expensive static policies.

</details>


### [162] [Parallel Manifold Steering: Efficient Adaptation of Large Associative Memories via Residual Energy Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24396)
*Kanishk Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 H-Res 机制，通过引导 Transformer 的激活轨迹而非修改权重，有效解决了大模型的适配难题，在提升性能的同时保持了模型的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大型 Transformer 在适配新任务时面临的“塑性-稳定性”困境，克服直接修改权重导致的灾难性遗忘以及通过提示词（prompt）引入带来的容量衰减问题。

Method: 提出 H-Res（分层残差转向）机制，将模型适配建模为激活流形上的控制问题，通过学习状态依赖的向量场，将 token 轨迹引导至特定任务的吸引子盆地。

Result: 在关联检索任务中，H-Res 的性能比全局权重修改方法提升了 26%，消除了基于提示方法的计算开销，并展现出良好的扩展性。

Conclusion: H-Res 是一种有效的模型适配方法，能够在不修改底层权重或增加序列长度的情况下，通过调节激活流形来提升模型性能，且具备良好的可扩展性和理论支持。

Abstract: Large Transformer models function as Dense Associative Memories (DAMs), retrieving knowledge via high-dimensional attractor dynamics driven by the self-attention mechanism \citep{ramsauer2020hopfield, wu2024attention}. However, adapting these frozen memory systems to new tasks presents a fundamental ``Plasticity-Stability'' dilemma. Current methods either risk catastrophic interference by modifying synaptic weights directly (e.g., LoRA) \citep{hu2021lora} or degrade associative capacity by clogging the retrieval buffer with static prompt tokens (e.g., VPT) \citep{jia2022vpt}. In this work, we propose \textbf{H-Res} (Hierarchical Residual Steering), a mechanism that modulates the effective energy landscape of the Transformer without altering its global equilibrium or expanding its sequence length. By formulating adaptation as a control problem on the activation manifold \citep{chen2018neuralode}, H-Res learns a state-dependent vector field that steers token trajectories into task-specific basins of attraction.
