We formally prove that H-Res preserves the attention entropy of the foundation model and facilitates Neural Collapse \citep{papyan2020prevalence}. Empirically, Manifold Steering outperforms global weight modification by 26\% on associative retrieval tasks and eliminates the computational overhead of prompt-based methods, scaling effectively to structured domains \citep{zha2023vtab}.

</details>


### [163] [Natural Identifiers for Privacy and Data Audits in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24408)
*Lorenzo Rossi,Bartłomiej Marek,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对LLM隐私审计中缺乏可溯源数据的问题，本研究提出了自然标识符（NIDs）方法，通过利用训练语料中原有的结构化随机字符串，实现了无需重训的后期差分隐私审计及无第三方参考集的数据集推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的差分隐私审计方法大多依赖于训练阶段植入金丝雀数据（无法用于已训练模型），或依赖于难以获取的同分布私有非成员数据集（数据集推理的局限性），导致无法开展可扩展的后期（Post-hoc）审计。

Method: 利用训练数据中自然存在的结构化随机字符串（如哈希值、短URL等）作为Natural Identifiers (NIDs)。通过这些NIDs生成同分布的数据，从而构造虚拟的金丝雀（Canaries）用于差分隐私审计，并作为数据集推理所需的非成员测试集。

Result: 实验证明，利用NIDs可在无需重新训练的情况下实现差分隐私审计，并能在缺乏私有非成员数据集的情况下，对包含NIDs的怀疑数据集进行有效的数据集推理。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的NIDs为大规模语言模型的后期隐私审计提供了一种实用且可扩展的解决方案，能够绕过训练数据隐私审计中对重新训练及私有非成员数据集的依赖。

Abstract: Assessing the privacy of large language models (LLMs) presents significant challenges. In particular, most existing methods for auditing differential privacy require the insertion of specially crafted canary data during training, making them impractical for auditing already-trained models without costly retraining. Additionally, dataset inference, which audits whether a suspect dataset was used to train a model, is infeasible without access to a private non-member held-out dataset. Yet, such held-out datasets are often unavailable or difficult to construct for real-world cases since they have to be from the same distribution (IID) as the suspect data. These limitations severely hinder the ability to conduct scalable, post-hoc audits. To enable such audits, this work introduces natural identifiers (NIDs) as a novel solution to the above-mentioned challenges. NIDs are structured random strings, such as cryptographic hashes and shortened URLs, naturally occurring in common LLM training datasets. Their format enables the generation of unlimited additional random strings from the same distribution, which can act as alternative canaries for audits and as same-distribution held-out data for dataset inference. Our evaluation highlights that indeed, using NIDs, we can facilitate post-hoc differential privacy auditing without any retraining and enable dataset inference for any suspect dataset containing NIDs without the need for a private non-member held-out dataset.

</details>


### [164] [Data Augmentation: A Fourier Analysis Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24418)
*Behrooz Tahmasebi,Melanie Weber,Stefanie Jegelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，随机采样部分数据增强在统计性能上趋近于全数据增强，平衡了计算成本与学习效果，同时明确了实现精确不变性必须遍历群的理论边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全数据增强虽有效但计算成本过高，在大群作用下不可行。论文旨在探究部分数据增强是否能在保持全增强统计性能（泛化与样本复杂度）的同时实现计算可扩展性。

Method: 利用傅里叶分析（Fourier analysis）和有限群的表示论（representation theory of finite groups）构建分析框架，研究部分数据增强下的泛化和样本复杂度。

Result: 证明了在广泛的经典学习问题中，基于随机采样子集的部分数据增强可以达到与全增强相同的统计极限，误差随子集增大快速减小；同时证明了若假设空间足够复杂，无法通过部分增强实现完全不变性。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了全数据增强与部分数据增强、精确对称性与近似对称性之间的统一理论。部分增强在统计上可以达到与全增强相同的最小最大（minimax）收敛速率；但在假设空间足够丰富时，若要实现精确的不变性，则必须对整个群进行平均，仅使用子集无法实现。

Abstract: Data augmentation is a simple and model-agnostic approach for exploiting known invariances in learning problems. Given a group acting on the input space, one augments the training set with transformed copies of each sample. Because it exploits symmetries without modifying the underlying learning algorithm, data augmentation can be applied broadly across learning methods. However, this universality comes at a computational cost: when the group is large, full group-sized augmentation quickly becomes computationally infeasible. This raises a fundamental question: Can partial data augmentation achieve the same statistical benefits as full augmentation in terms of generalization and sample complexity? We develop a general framework for investigating this question using Fourier analysis and the representation theory of finite groups. We show that, for a broad class of classical learning problems, partial data augmentation based on a randomly sampled subset of group elements achieves the same minimax rates as full augmentation, up to an approximation error that vanishes as the subset size increases. Our results provide a theoretical explanation for why partial augmentation can retain the statistical benefits of full augmentation despite enforcing symmetry only approximately, and shed light on a recently raised question in learning with symmetries: whether statistically optimal learning under general group invariances can be achieved using computationally scalable methods. Moreover, we prove a complementary impossibility result: enforcing exact invariance via data augmentation requires averaging over the entire group, and cannot be achieved by any strict subset when the hypothesis space is sufficiently expressive. Together, these results provide a unified perspective on full and partial data augmentation, as well as exact and approximate symmetry enforcement.

</details>


### [165] [An LLM-based Two-Stage Transformer Framework for Cross-Domain Bearing Fault Diagnosis with Limited Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24459)
*Jinghan Wang,Feng Cheng,Wentao Wu,Hang Li,Gaoliang Peng,Tianchen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于轻量级Transformer的知识引导迁移学习框架，通过显式原型学习提升了工业轴承在少样本、高异构场景下的故障诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业环境下轴承故障诊断面临多源异构数据、工况多变及标签数据稀缺的叠加挑战，现有孤立且隐式的特征对齐方法难以满足实际需求。

Method: 提出了一种知识引导的两阶段迁移学习框架，核心组件包括：1) 基于轻量级GPT-2架构的因果自注意力Transformer用于分层特征提取；2) 利用预训练权重与故障原型嵌入作为显式知识载体；3) 结合多源学习、原型知识调制及分类学自适应分类，以应对数据分布偏移与类别差异。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上的实验表明，在仅有10%目标域标签数据的情况下，平均准确率达到92.61%，较当前最先进（SOTA）方法提升了17.24个百分点。

Conclusion: 该框架通过显式知识引导有效解决了多源异构数据与有限标签下的故障诊断难题，为工业4.0环境下的低成本预测性维护提供了一种高效解决方案。

Abstract: Bearing fault diagnosis faces critical challenges when dataset heterogeneity, operating condition variations, and limited labeled data occur simultaneously in industrial environments. Existing approaches address these issues in isolation and rely on implicit feature alignment, limiting effectiveness under concurrent challenges. This paper proposes a knowledge-guided two-stage transfer learning framework that employs a lightweight GPT-2-style Transformer with causal self-attention for hierarchical feature extraction from vibration signals, establishing explicit pathways where pre-trained encoder weights and fault prototype embeddings serve as knowledge carriers from multi-source pre-training to target adaptation. The framework addresses the dual-shift challenge through multi-source learning for generalizable representations, prototype-based knowledge modulation for target adaptation, and taxonomy-adaptive classification for seamless transfer across heterogeneous fault categories. Experimental validation on four real-world datasets demonstrates 92.61% average accuracy with only 10% labeled target data, outperforming state-of-the-art methods by 17.24 percentage points, establishing a practical pathway toward cost-effective predictive maintenance in Industry 4.0 applications.

</details>


### [166] [A Fair Evaluation of Graph Foundation Models for Node Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24509)
*Oleg Platonov,Gleb Bazhenov,Dmitry Eremeev,Liudmila Prokhorenkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对9款图基础模型（GFM）进行了公平评估，发现大多GFM并不比调优后的传统GNN表现更优，只有基于Prior-data Fitted Networks的少数模型表现更佳但成本更高，指出当前GFM领域亟需统一的评价体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图基础模型（GFM）研究近期大热，但该领域缺乏统一的评估范式，不同研究间的实验方式偏差导致模型性能对比不可靠，难以验证GFM相比传统GNN的实际优势。

Method: 构建了统一的评估框架，对9种近期提出的用于节点属性预测的GFM进行了公平且严谨的重新评估，并将评估结果与性能调优后的经典图神经网络（GNN）基准进行了横向对比。

Result: 研究发现，大部分GFM模型在性能上并不具备明显优势；仅最新的基于Prior-data Fitted Networks范式的模型能够超越经过充分调优的传统GNN，但这些模型同时带来了更高的推理计算开销。

Conclusion: 目前的图基础模型（GFM）领域缺乏统一的评估标准，通过严谨的对比实验发现，仅有基于Prior-data Fitted Networks范式的最新模型在预测性能上优于性能调优后的传统GNN，且伴随着更高的推理成本，表明当前GFM的实用性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Due to the wide use of graph-structured data in different fields of industry and science, the development of Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) has recently attracted a lot of attention. While many different types of models are called GFMs, particular interest has been paid to GFMs designed for node property prediction tasks, which is one of the most popular settings in Graph ML with lots of real-world applications from fraud detection in financial and social networks to recommendation systems for e-commerce and user-generated content platforms. While a number of GFMs for this task have been recently proposed, the field has not converged to a unified evaluation setting, and different works evaluate their models in widely different ways, preventing reliable comparison of GFMs with each other and with other types of models. In this work, we conduct a fair and rigorous reevaluation of 9 recent GFMs for node property prediction, comparing them to strong Graph Neural Network (GNN) baselines. We find that, among these GFMs, only the most recent ones based on the Prior-data Fitted Networks paradigm outperform well-tuned GNNs in predictive performance, although at a higher inference cost.

</details>


### [167] [Reasoning as Attractor Dynamics: Latent Memory Retrieval via Gibbs-Weighted Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24543)
*Kanishk Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究将LLM推理重新建模为物理能量景观中的能量最小化过程，通过对推理路径进行热力学加权（Gibbs采样），有效抑制了幻觉并提升了数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将LLM视为自回归生成器，但作者认为其本质是高维稠密关联记忆，数学推理链对应能量景观中的深而宽的吸引子盆地（“平坦极小值”），而幻觉则对应尖锐且不稳定的局部极小值。

Method: 提出了一种基于Gibbs度量的搜寻机制。通过对多种推理路径进行采样，利用轨迹谱熵计算能量，并依据能量倒数权重（P ∝ e^-βE）进行加权，从而逼近关联记忆的平衡分布。

Result: 在Microsoft Phi-3.5模型上的实验显示，该方法使GSM8K数据集的性能提升了5.38%（从84.7%提升至90.1%）。

Conclusion: 推理过程应被视为系统动态能量稳态（沉降到吸引子盆地）的过程，而非单纯的贪婪式下一步预测。将LLM视为关联记忆存储系统可有效提升数学推理的准确性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are traditionally viewed as autoregressive generators. However, from the perspective of collective computation, they function as high-dimensional Dense Associative Memories that store complex reasoning patterns as latent attractors. In this work, we investigate the energy landscape of mathematical reasoning. We posit that correct reasoning chains correspond to deep, wide attractor basins ("flat minima") in the model's output distribution, whereas hallucinations manifest as sharp, unstable local minima. To exploit this geometry, we introduce a retrieval mechanism based on a Gibbs measure of the trajectory's spectral entropy. By sampling multiple reasoning paths and weighting them by their inverse energy ($P \propto e^{-βE}$), we approximate the equilibrium distribution of the associative memory, effectively ``relaxing'' the system into a robust solution. Empirically, this physics-inspired mechanism improves Microsoft Phi-3.5 performance on GSM8K by 5.38\% (84.7\% $\to$ 90.1\%), demonstrating that inference is better modeled as a dynamic settling process into an attractor basin rather than greedy next-token prediction.

</details>


### [168] [QC-SMOTE: Quality-Controlled SMOTE for Imbalanced Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24625)
*Parth Upman,Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QC-SMOTE 是一种新型质量受控的过采样框架，通过评估样本可靠性并采用自适应生成策略，有效解决了类不平衡学习中噪声和类重叠区域生成样本质量差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的过采样方法（如 SMOTE）在处理类不平衡问题时，往往会在噪声或类重叠区域生成不合理的低质量合成样本，从而损害分类性能。

Method: 提出 QC-SMOTE 框架，核心机制包括：1) 使用基于局部密度、安全水平和隔离度的综合信任得分来评估样本可靠性；2) 引入 IPQ（中点纯度与多数类清除）引导的 Best-of-K 策略来生成合成样本；3) 具备自适应生成行为，根据局部数据几何形态调整插值范围；4) 引入容错机制，当邻域纯度过低时，用原始样本重复代替低质量合成样本。

Result: 在 30 个不平衡数据集上的重复分层交叉验证实验表明，QC-SMOTE 在 AUC-ROC 和宏观 F1 值指标上均优于对比方法，特别是在中度和重度不平衡场景下表现更为显著。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了基于质量感知和几何自适应的合成采样策略对于提升不平衡分类任务的稳健性至关重要。QC-SMOTE 通过在噪声区域主动退化为复制采样，有效解决了传统方法在噪声或类重叠区域生成低质量样本的问题。

Abstract: Class imbalance poses a significant challenge in classification, where existing methods such as SMOTE often generate low-quality synthetic samples in regions with noise or class overlap. We propose QC-SMOTE, a quality-controlled oversampling framework that estimates minority sample reliability using a composite neighbourhood trustworthiness score combining local density, safe-level, and isolation from the majority class. Synthetic candidates are generated using an IPQ-guided best-of-K strategy that evaluates midpoint purity and, when required, majority clearance, with allocation guided by sample reliability and boundary informativeness. Generation behaviour adapts across overlap--imbalance regimes, adjusting interpolation range and selection criteria to match local data geometry. Low-quality synthetic samples are replaced with original minority duplicates when neighbourhood purity falls below an adaptive threshold, providing graceful degradation by reverting to duplication in severely noisy regions. Experiments on 30 imbalanced datasets using repeated stratified cross-validation show that QC-SMOTE achieves the strongest average AUC-ROC and Macro F1 among the compared oversampling methods, with particularly clear gains under moderate and severe imbalance. These results demonstrate the importance of quality-aware, geometry-adaptive synthetic sampling for robust imbalanced classification.

</details>


### [169] [FlowPipe: LLM-Enhanced Conditional Generative Flow Networks for Data Preparation Pipeline Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24679)
*Kunyu Ni,Lei Cao,Jie He,Xiaotong Zhang,Jianfeng Jin,Junyu Dong,Yanwei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowPipe 是一种基于条件生成流网络的新型自动数据准备管道合成框架，通过深度语义调制和失败感知探索策略，显著提升了搜索效率、上下文理解及最终模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多重深度 Q 网络（Multi-DQN）方法在自动构建数据准备管道时面临长程信用分配弱、数据集上下文利用率低以及在高维度稀疏搜索空间中探索效率低下等问题。

Method: 提出 FlowPipe 框架，利用条件生成流网络（C-GFlowNets）和轨道平衡（Trajectory Balance）目标进行管道合成；引入基于 FiLM 的深度语义调制（Deep Semantic Modulation）以融合 LLM 语义先验；并在流目标中加入“失败感知”（failure awareness）机制以规避无效状态搜索。

Result: 在包含 74 个真实数据集的两个基准测试中，FlowPipe 平均准确率提升 11.96%，训练收敛速度提升 12.5 倍。

Conclusion: FlowPipe 通过将管道合成建模为条件概率流生成，成功解决了自动化数据准备中的长程信用分配、上下文融入及搜索效率问题，实验证明在准确率和收敛速度上均显著优于现有 SOTA 方法。

Abstract: Data preparation pipelines improve data quality in machine learning by transforming raw tables into learning-ready data through sequential cleaning and feature transformation operators. However, automatically constructing such pipelines is computationally difficult because operator sequences are combinatorial and end-to-end evaluation is expensive. Existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) Multi-DQN methods still face three key limitations: decoupled value estimators weaken long-horizon credit assignment, dataset context is only weakly injected into the policy, and exploration is inefficient in a sparse search space with many invalid states. To address these issues, we propose FlowPipe, a unified framework that formulates pipeline synthesis as conditional probabilistic flow generation over a directed acyclic graph. FlowPipe uses Conditional Generative Flow Networks (C-GFlowNets) with a Trajectory Balance objective to connect terminal validation rewards with early pipeline decisions. It further introduces Deep Semantic Modulation through Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), allowing LLM-derived logical priors to condition the policy's internal activations according to dataset semantics. In addition, FlowPipe incorporates failure awareness into the flow objective to avoid invalid states and concentrate search on high-potential regions. Experiments on two benchmark suites with 74 real-world datasets show that FlowPipe outperforms SOTA baselines, improving accuracy by 11.96% on average and achieving 12.5x faster training convergence. Source code is available at https://github.com/KunyuNi/FlowPipe.

</details>


### [170] [Grad Detect: Gradient-Based Hallucination Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24790)
*Anand Kamat,Daniel Blake,Brent M. Werness*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Grad Detect 是一种利用大模型推理时的层级梯度模式来检测幻觉和预测拒答的新方法。它通过分析最后几层的梯度信息，在保持高性能的同时，提供了对模型故障起源的解释性支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）普遍存在幻觉问题，现有的基于置信度或采样的方法无法有效检测高风险应用中的错误，且缺乏对模型内部状态的深度利用。

Method: 提出 Grad Detect 方法，在推理过程进行单次前向-反向传播，通过提取和分析模型层级梯度（Layer-wise gradient patterns）来捕捉预测的确定性与正确性信号。

Result: 在多个问答基准测试中，Grad Detect 的表现优于现有的置信度及采样基准方法；消融实验表明，模型最后五层的梯度集中了超过 97% 的判别信息，显著提升了部署效率。

Conclusion: Grad Detect 通过分析模型层级梯度模式，提供了一种高效、高准确率且具可解释性的幻觉检测与拒答预测框架。研究证明模型内部梯度结构包含输出信号所不具备的鲁棒判别信息，且仅利用最后五层梯度即可实现高性能检测。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across diverse tasks, yet they remain prone to generating hallucinations. Detecting these hallucinations is critical for deploying LLMs reliably in high-stakes applications. We present Grad Detect, a gradient-based approach for predicting hallucinations by analyzing layer-wise gradient patterns from a single forward-backward pass during inference. Our method shows that the internal gradient structure of a model carries rich information about the correctness of its output. This information is not accessible through output-level signals alone. We evaluate Grad Detect on several Q&A benchmarks across both hallucination detection and model abstention prediction, where it consistently outperforms confidence-based and sampling-based baselines. Through comprehensive layer ablation studies across all eleven models from four architectural families, we find that the final five layers concentrate over 97% of the discriminative gradient signal, enabling efficient deployment with minimal performance loss. Grad Detect provides a unified framework for predicting multiple dimensions of LLM reliability, offering strong predictive performance alongside interpretable insights into where and how model failures originate.

</details>


### [171] [Real vs. Complex Spectral Bases for Neural Operators: The Role of Green's Function Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24851)
*Jason Sulskis,Sathya Ravi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出哈特利神经算子（HNO），研究发现算子表现与谱基的选择有关：对于静态椭圆方程，实数谱基（HNO）更优；而包含波动或传输性质的时变方程，复数谱基（FNO）更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 傅里叶神经算子（FNO）在处理实值PDE时存在由于共轭对称性带来的冗余。作者探索实数域的替代变换（Hartley变换）是否能更好地匹配特定偏微分方程（PDE）解算子的几何属性。

Method: 引入基于离散哈特利变换（DHT）的哈特利神经算子（HNO），通过仅使用实数乘法替换FNO中的复数傅里叶变换，在保持参数量一致的情况下，以实数谱基代替复数谱基进行对比实验。

Result: 实验表明HNO在椭圆算子（如泊松方程）上表现更佳，而FNO在具有相位传递效应的时变算子（如波动方程、纳维-斯托克斯方程）中更占优势；性能差异与算子的相位内容呈单调相关。

Conclusion: 并不存在一种在所有场景下均优的通用算子，研究结论表明最佳的谱基选择取决于算子的性质：对于对称性强的椭圆型方程，哈特利基（HNO）更优；对于包含相位传递的时变方程，傅里叶基（FNO）更优。

Abstract: Fourier Neural Operators (FNO) learn solution operators of partial differential equations by parameterizing global convolutions in the complex Fourier domain. For real-valued PDE solutions, the complex FFT carries representational redundancy through conjugate symmetry. We introduce the Hartley Neural Operator (HNO), the exact real-valued mirror of FNO: it replaces the FFT with the purely real Discrete Hartley Transform and learns a single real multiplier per retained spectral mode, with no complex arithmetic. Because the real Hartley spectrum is not halved by conjugate symmetry, HNO retains twice as many frequency corners as FNO but one real weight where FNO carries a complex pair, so the two operators are iso-parametric at equal width and differ only in spectral basis. Our central thesis is that the best basis is a property of the operator. Self-adjoint elliptic operators (Poisson, biharmonic) have real, symmetric Green's functions that the real Hartley multiplier diagonalizes exactly, and HNO is favored there. Time-dependent operators carry phase, from oscillation in the wave equation to transport in advection, Burgers, and Navier-Stokes, which a real diagonal multiplier cannot represent, so FNO is favored there, and increasingly so with the operator's phase content, leaving the phaseless heat equation as the borderline case. Training both operators identically and benchmarking across PDE classes, initial-condition families, and boundary conditions, we find an elliptic-versus-time-dependent split that is monotone in operator phase content and matches the Green's-function theory we develop. Rather than a universal winner, our findings give a predictive rule: match the spectral basis to the symmetry of the solution operator.

</details>


### [172] [When Do Conservation Laws Survive Learned Representations? Certified Horizons for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24945)
*Hongbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过提出一种基于“解码物理不变量”的认证框架，解决了物理世界模型在表征学习中守恒律难以验证的问题，并证明了相比于硬几何约束，软学习的见证函数在复杂表征环境下具有更好的守恒鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理世界模型虽然能够进行长期模拟，但缺乏对物理守恒律（如能量守恒）在表征学习过程中的系统验证方法，且隐空间中的哈密顿量或见证函数往往在学习过程中发生漂移，导致守恒性无法保证。

Method: 提出了一种基于“解码物理不变量”的认证框架。通过将潜在状态解码回物理观测空间并评估已知的不变量，推导出包含表征误差、读出误差和潜在动力学误差的“壳层视界（shell-horizon）”证书，并利用单调对齐桥接机制（monotone alignment bridge）将软学习到的见证函数转化为认证边界。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在状态观测、学习提升（learned-lift）及像素级观测上均有效。研究发现硬仿辛结构虽在已知坐标下表现优异，但在复杂表征下性能受限；而控制Lipschitz对齐的软不变量在学习表征中展现了极佳的鲁棒性，并在特定条件下实现了像素级的守恒认证。

Conclusion: 论文提出“解码物理不变量”应作为物理世界模型中守恒律认证的核心对象，而非隐空间哈密顿量。通过定义的“壳层视界证书（shell-horizon certificates）”，研究证明了此类证书能够有效量化并跨越表征学习过程，且软约束（soft-constrained）的不变量在复杂表征下比硬几何约束表现更好。

Abstract: We ask a representation-learning question about physical world models: when does a conservation law remain certifiable after a model learns a latent representation? A certified horizon bounds -- in advance, from measurable model defects -- how many steps a rollout provably stays on a physical invariant's level set. The key design choice is what is certified: not a learned latent Hamiltonian or a learned scalar witness (a model can conserve either while drifting in true energy), but the decoded physical invariant obtained by decoding the latent state and evaluating the known invariant. Around this object we derive shell-horizon certificates whose budget decomposes into representation, readout, and latent-dynamics defects, with a monotone alignment bridge through which a soft learned witness yields a certified horizon for the decoded invariant, and test them across state, learned-lift, and pixel observations on conservative systems. Conservation certificates can survive learned representation, but not all geometric priors survive equally: hard canonical symplectic structure yields the longest horizons in known phase coordinates yet does not cross a learned chart, whereas a controlled-Lipschitz-aligned soft invariant survives in the learned-representation settings we test; pixel certification is recovered on a readout-stable sub-tube; and the Kepler problem exposes a geometric boundary. The central object is therefore not a latent Hamiltonian, but a decoded physical invariant whose robustness to representation learning can be measured, certified, and falsified.

</details>


### [173] [Conformal Orbit-Valid Trust Horizons for Equivariant World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24946)
*Hongbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于群等变性的隐式世界模型置信视界认证框架。通过利用环境的对称结构，研究者成功证明并实现了将校准好的可信视界迁移至整个群轨道，在保证鲁棒性的前提下显著降低了模型验证的开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型世界模型通常存在累积误差，仅在有效的“置信视界”内可靠。现有方法难以在大状态空间中高效地为模型 rollout 提供有效的安全性保证，因此需要一种能利用环境对称性来降低校准成本并推广置信度的自动化验证框架。

Method: 1. 提出一种基于群对称性的隐式世界模型置信视界证书生成方法；2. 使用分裂共形预测（split-conformal）对原始视界曲线进行校准；3. 利用群等变性（equivariance）推导并证明了置信视界可以在群轨道（group orbit）内进行迁移；4. 在2D和3D环境下对比了等变模型与非等变基准模型，验证了其效率与安全性。

Result: 1. 对审计集合的测试显示，该证书保守且可靠（0次反保守违规）；2. 证明了等变性下置信视界在群轨道上的恒定性，实验误差极小（中位数残差1.1%）；3. 该证书非空（non-vacuous），中位比率为0.67；4. 在3D环境审计中，等变模型显著优于非等变基准，避免了严重的违规或过度保守。

Conclusion: 本文提出的通过群等变性实现置信视界的迁移是有效的。等变模型在复杂环境（如3D yaw任务）中表现出显著优势，能够在保持安全性的同时，以较低的校准成本提供非平凡的视界证书。该方法将局部的置信度量扩展到了整个群轨道，为复杂系统中的模型预测控制提供了鲁棒性保障。

Abstract: Learned world models are useful only over horizons on which their rollout error remains controlled. We study trust-horizon certification for latent world models with known group symmetries. Given a one-step latent residual and a finite-time expansion estimate, we form a raw horizon curve and calibrate it with a split-conformal multiplicative factor. On the reproducible audit set, the conformal factor is $γ_α=1.0$: the raw certificate is already conservative under the audit protocol. Across 50 stable audits, we observe zero anti-conservative violations, corresponding to an exact-binomial 95% upper bound of 5.8% on the violation rate. Our main structural result is that exact equivariance transports a calibrated trust-horizon curve over the group orbit: when the environment dynamics, encoder, predictor, action transform, and latent metric satisfy the stated equivariance/invariance conditions, rollout errors and trust horizons are orbit-constant. Empirically, the implemented models exhibit small orbit-transport residuals, with median 1.1% and maximum 4.1% over 14 orbit audits. The certificate is also non-vacuous (median certified-to-measured horizon ratio 0.67). A certificate-level calibration-cost study shows two complementary regimes. On a symmetric 2D substrate, equivariant, plain, and augmented models are all orbit-valid from a single calibration sector -- no separation, because the substrate already makes non-equivariant baselines approximately orbit-robust. A 3D yaw audit shows the other regime: the equivariant model obtains a one-sector safe and non-vacuous orbit-valid certificate, while healthy non-equivariant baselines pay violation, slack, sharpness, or additional-sector cost. The certificate is a conservative, distributional audit rather than a global reachability guarantee, and certificate-guided subgoal spacing is not confirmed in the current 3D CEM-MPC behavior layer.

</details>


### [174] [Swarm-Inspired Generation of Collective Behaviors in Graph Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24958)
*Ji Chen,Song Chen,Chengzhang Gong,Li Fan,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究团队提出了 SIES 框架，通过模拟生物群体行为，利用一种通用的局部交互规则实现复杂的全局同步与协调，具备极强的跨任务、跨图结构泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以设计出能够跨越不同图结构、动力学系统和任务进行泛化，并产生所需全局组织结构的局部交互规则。

Method: 提出了一种名为 SIES (Swarm-Inspired Emergent Synchronizer) 的图动力学框架。该框架将每个节点建模为具有状态和任务提示的代理，并使用“带符号的源-目标条件注意力机制”作为显式动力学模型中的自适应耦合项，模拟生物集群行为。

Result: 在同步控制任务中，SIES 实现了对未见过的网络规模、相位关系和内在动力学的同步控制；在机器人应用中，实现了对异构多足机器人的高效步态控制，并在物理机器人腿部失效后保持鲁棒性；在异质图分类任务中，达到了最优性能。

Conclusion: SIES 框架作为一个通用且可学习的图动力学交互框架，在同步控制、机器人自适应协调以及异质图表示学习方面展现出巨大潜力，特别是其在无需重训练情况下的强泛化能力。

Abstract: Collective behavior arises when locally interacting units produce coordinated global organization, from synchronization in dynamical systems to task-relevant information flow on graphs. The central challenge is not only to explain how collective behavior emerges, but to design local interaction rules that can produce desired global organization and generalize across graphs, dynamics and tasks.To address this challenge, we introduce the Swarm-Inspired Emergent Synchronizer (SIES), a graph-dynamical framework that learns generalizable local-interaction laws for controllable collective organization. Each node is an agent-like dynamical unit with a state and task cue, and signed source-target-conditioned attention acts as an adaptive coupling term inside an explicit evolution model. Therefore, SIES combines an explicit dynamical engine with local agent intelligence, similar to biological swarms. For synchronization control, SIES learns a generalizable coupling operator that produces prescribed synchronization patterns for CDSs across untrained network scales, target phase relations, and intrinsic node dynamics without retraining. The learned operator also reaches gait-related modes faster than three oscillator baselines and generalizes synchronization-driven locomotion to simulated multi-legged robots of different scales and a physical hexapod after leg disablement. For graph representation learning, SIES applies the same signed interaction principle to message passing and achieves the highest performance among the compared methods on heterophilous node-classification benchmarks. Together, these results position SIES as a generalizable and learnable graph-dynamical interaction framework with promise for synchronization control, adaptive robot coordination, and heterophilous graph representation learning.

</details>


### [175] [Memory-Efficient Policy Libraries with Low-Rank Adaptation in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25700)
*Samuel Valland Lyngset,Tor Viljen Raanaas,Gard Sveipe,Eirik Møller Nilsen,Jim Torresen,Kai Olav Ellefsen,Tobias Lømo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究将LoRA引入机器人强化学习，通过极低参数量微调实现了与全参数微调相当的性能，大幅降低了多任务策略库的内存足迹，缓解了机器人部署中的显存压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证大语言模型中高效的参数微调方法（如LoRA）是否能迁移至机器人强化学习领域，以解决多任务策略库部署中面临的内存资源受限和计算成本高昂的问题。

Method: 将低秩适应（LoRA）应用于基于近端策略优化（PPO）的强化学习算法中，对基准模型进行多任务机器人策略微调，并对比其与全参数微调的性能。

Result: LoRA相比全参数微调，在保持任务成功率无显著差异的前提下，内存使用量减少了20-160倍，且在存储10-50个策略库时节省了90-95%的存储空间。

Conclusion: LoRA在机器人多任务强化学习中不仅能显著降低内存占用（90-95%），还能保持与全参数微调相当的策略成功率，对于部署大规模专用策略库的机器人系统具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: When fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs), there has been success in minimizing both memory usage and computation with Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT), like Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA). In this article, we have explored whether this approach is transferable to the world of robotics and Reinforcement Learning (RL), allowing learning with reduced memory usage and improved computational performance. Specifically, we focused on a version of multi-task robotics, where a library of specialist policies are created. In such a library memory efficiency is especially important. We used a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm and fine-tuned a baseline model to different tasks using LoRA. Our results demonstrate that, depending on the hyperparameters, LoRA can minimize memory usage by a factor of 20-160 compared to full fine-tuning of all layers. This implies a 90-95% storage saving when deploying a library of many (10-50) specialized policies, which can be the differentiating factor between being able to store the entire library in memory or having to use swap-memory in an applied robotics setting. At the same time, our results indicate that there is no significant difference in the success-rate between full fine-tuning and LoRA fine-tuning for the selected tasks.

</details>


### [176] [ROAD-VLA: Robust Online Adaptation via Self-Distillation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25800)
*Kejing Wang,Toan Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Simon Khan,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 ROAD-VLA，一种通过优势引导的自蒸馏框架，通过在动作空间中构建近端教师，解决了 VLA 模型在线适配中稀疏奖励和模态差异导致的问题，实现了比 PPO 更优的适配效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在在线适配中面临稀疏奖励导致的弱监督问题，且现有基于文本的特权教师因模态鸿沟（符号导航与底层动作之间的差异）而导致适配效果不佳。

Method: ROAD-VLA（Advantage-guided self-distillation framework）：通过扰动动作 token 的 logits，使用经过校准的优势估计（calibrated advantage estimates）构建动作空间的近端教师，将稀疏奖励转化为密集的 token 级监督信号，并推导了策略改进的下界。

Result: 在七个机器人操作环境中，无论是分布内还是分布外设置，ROAD-VLA 的性能均优于 PPO，证明了其在鲁棒在线 VLA 适配方面的有效性。

Conclusion: ROAD-VLA 通过在动作空间中构建基于优势估计的近端教师，有效解决了 VLA 模型在线适配中稀疏奖励和模态鸿沟的问题，在多项机器人操作任务中表现优于 PPO。

Abstract: Effective online adaptation of vision-language-action (VLA) models remains challenging, as sparse rewards provide weak supervision for high-dimensional autoregressive action policies. Although self-distillation can in principle provide denser training signals, we find that text-based privileged teachers conditioned on demonstrations, retrieved experiences, or high-level plans are ineffective for VLA adaptation, exposing a modality gap between symbolic guidance and low-level robot actions. We propose ROAD-VLA, an advantage-guided self-distillation framework that constructs a proximal teacher directly in action space by perturbing action-token logits with calibrated advantage estimates. This converts sparse rewards into dense token-level supervision while keeping the teacher close to the current policy. We further derive a policy-improvement lower bound under calibrated advantages and accurate teacher matching. Across seven robotic manipulation environments with in-distribution and out-of-distribution shifts, ROADVLA outperforms PPO in nearly all settings, demonstrating robust online VLA adaptation.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [TabClean: Reusable LLM-Synthesized Programs for Tabular Data Cleaning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25388)
*Yibo Wang,Riteng Zhang,Yinghao He,Yongye Su,Bharat Bhargava,Chunwei Liu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: TabClean 是一种无需训练的数据清洗系统，它将 LLM 的复杂推理编译为高度可复用的 Python 清洗程序，在实现高精度修复的同时，通过避免重复调用 LLM 显著降低了大规模数据处理的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据清洗方案存在显著局限：基于约束的系统依赖专家手工制定规则，基于学习的系统需要大量标注或重新训练，而既有的 LLM 清洁系统因对每行、每单元或每步骤重复调用 LLM，导致处理大规模数据时计算消耗大、成本高且难以规模化。

Method: 提出了一种无需模型训练的系统 TabClean。其核心理念是将 LLM 的推理逻辑编译为“基于证据的守护修复子句”（evidence-backed guarded repair clause）。系统通过分析脏数据表、识别修复机制、合成可执行的 Python 转换脚本，并利用单元格级反馈进行验证，最终生成可应用于后续兼容数据批次的自动化清洗程序。

Result: 在六个基准测试中，TabClean 展现了高精度的清洗效果，在其中五个数据集上的 F1 分数优于现有的基于规则、基于学习和基于 LLM 的方案。更重要的是，通过将重复的推理过程转化为确定性程序执行，大幅降低了重复运行时的计算开销和 API 调用成本。

Conclusion: TabClean 通过将 LLM 的推理过程编译为确定性的、可重用的 Python 守护清洗程序，有效解决了传统系统在数据清洗中的准确性、易用性与成本之间的权衡问题。该方法在保证高精度的同时，显著降低了处理大规模和批处理数据的计算成本与 API 开销。

Abstract: Reliable analytics and machine-learning pipelines depend on clean tabular data, yet production tables often contain missing values, typographical errors, inconsistent formats, violated dependencies, unit mismatches, and ambiguous categorical values. Existing cleaning systems make different trade-offs. Constraint-based systems need experts to specify rules. Learning-based systems need labels or retraining. Recent LLM-based cleaners reduce setup effort, but many call an LLM on rows, cells, or repeated workflow steps, so their cost grows with table size and with every recurring batch.
  We present TabClean, a model-training-free system that compiles LLM reasoning into reusable guarded cleaning programs. Given a dirty table and a small annotated development set, TabClean profiles table evidence, diagnoses repair mechanisms, synthesizes executable Python transformations, validates candidates with cell-level feedback, and commits the best program for reuse on schema-compatible batches. The key abstraction is an evidence-backed guarded repair clause. A deterministic transformation may fire only when its dirty pattern, target-negative condition, evidence support, and scope constraints are satisfied. Across six benchmarks, TabClean achieves high precision, improves F1 over representative rule-based, learning-based, and LLM-based baselines on five datasets, and substantially reduces recurring runtime and API cost by replacing repeated LLM inference with deterministic program execution.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [SPORT: Spherical-PSNR-Optimized tRuncaTion for Power-Efficient 360-Degree Video Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24916)
*Md. Sajjad Hossain,Hasibur Rahman Hemel,Kyle Mooney,Yiwen Xu,William Oswald,Mario Renteria-Pinon,Hritom Das,Zhenlin Pei,Jinhui Wang,Na Gong*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SPORT是一种针对VR优化的位截断存储框架，通过感知用户视线（FoV），在保证视觉质量的前提下大幅降低了内存能耗和带宽使用，并通过ASIC硬件实验证实了其高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内存带宽占VR头显总功耗的30-40%，而现有系统对全景帧采用统一分辨率存储，导致了性能冗余和高功耗，需要一种能感知用户凝视的高效存储方案。

Method: 1. 提出SPORT框架，针对VR应用，对视野（FoV）外像素进行位截断存储；2. 引入基于WS-PSNR（加权球面均匀峰值信噪比）的优化算法，以消除度量不一致性；3. 开发凝视预测瓦片分类器以应对pipeline延迟；4. 在TrunMEM360 ASIC芯片上进行了硬件验证。

Result: SPORT-B变体在保持FoV无损的情况下实现47.9%的能耗和带宽节省；SPORT-A变体实现51.6%的能耗节省；DRAM漏电功耗降低48.72%，读写能耗降低超过36%；系统总延迟为9.33ms，满足VR舒适度要求。

Conclusion: SPORT框架通过在VR显示路径中实施基于凝视的位截断（bit-truncation），在满足WS-PSNR视觉质量约束的同时，显著降低了内存能耗和带宽需求，并验证了其在硬件实现中的有效性。

Abstract: Memory bandwidth accounts for 30-40% of total power consumption in standalone virtual reality (VR) headsets, yet existing systems typically store the entire 360-degree frame at a uniform resolution regardless of viewer gaze. This paper presents SPORT (Spherical-PSNR Optimized tRuncaTion), a bit-truncation framework that reduces display-path memory power by storing only the most significant bits of pixels outside the user's field of view (FoV). Specifically, a new bit-truncation framework is developed to use weighted-to-spherically-uniform PSNR (WS-PSNR) directly in the optimization constraint, eliminating the metric inconsistency that arises when standard PSNR is used for a WS-PSNR quality target. Also, gaze-predictive tile classification compensates for the 9.33 ms end-to-end pipeline latency, reducing boundary misclassifications by 5.2 percentage points at a cost of only 0.01 ms. In addition, the developed SPORT-B variant, which keeps the FoV lossless, achieves 47.9% memory power saving and 47.9% bandwidth reduction across different 4K video sequences while satisfying all three per-region WS-PSNR thresholds and maintaining SSIM = 1.000 in the attended region. The full adaptive variant SPORT-A reaches 51.6% power saving, 3.1percentage points more than a PSNR-based optimizer at equal measured quality. SPORT is validated on the TrunMEM360 flexible SRAM Application-Specific Integrated Circuit (ASIC) fabricated in SkyWater 130 nm CMOS, confirming byte-exact silicon-software agreement, with WS-PSNR and SSIM matching within 0.1 dB and 0.001. CACTI-based analysis confirms 48.72% DRAM leakage reduction and 36.4%/36.7% read/write energy reduction. The total motion-to-photon latency of 9.33 ms satisfies the 20 ms VR comfort budget with a 53.3% safety margin.

</details>


### [179] [SafeGen: LLM-Driven Assertion Generation and Fault Criticality Evaluation for Functional Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25296)
*Xuanyi Tan,Arjun Chaudhuri,Rubin Parekhji,Krishnendu Chakrabarty*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SafeGen 是一个结合大语言模型与形式化验证的汽车芯片故障临界性评估框架，通过构建超知识图谱实现断言的自动化生成与可追溯的语义化故障分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于仿真的故障分析在模块级过于保守，难以准确反映故障对系统整体安全的实际影响，且缺乏足够的语义解释性。

Method: 提出名为 SafeGen 的框架：1. 构建包含 FMEDA 指南和 RTL 信息的超知识图谱（HyperKG）；2. 利用 LLM 基于 HyperKG 生成语义对齐的功能安全断言（FSAs）；3. 采用门级到 RTL 的故障映射机制，结合形式化属性验证（FPV）实现故障临界性评估。

Result: 在 FOC 系统验证中，SafeGen 生成的断言质量优于现有的 LLM 方法，并且在故障临界性评估中提供了比传统仿真方法更强的语义解释力。

Conclusion: SafeGen 证明了结合 LLM 与形式化验证（FPV）的方法能够有效提升车规芯片功能安全评估的准确性、可追溯性及语义解释性，优于传统的仿真方法。

Abstract: With advances in autonomous driving and electric vehicle technologies, functional safety has become a critical requirement in automotive chip design. Traditional simulation-based fault analysis is often overly conservative at the module level and fails to accurately reflect fault criticality. This paper presents SafeGen, an LLM-driven, formal-verification-assisted framework for functional-safety-oriented fault criticality assessment. SafeGen leverages large language models (LLMs) and a document-level Hyper Knowledge Graph (HyperKG) that incorporates Failure Modes, Effects, and Diagnostic Analysis (FMEDA) guidelines to extract verifiable specifications from design and safety documents and evaluate their relevance to overall system safety. The HyperKG is further enriched with register-transfer-level (RTL) information to guide the generation of Functional Safety Assertions (FSAs) that are both semantically grounded and design-aware. Each assertion is linked to its corresponding specification, enabling traceable reasoning throughout the assessment process. A gate-to-RTL fault-mapping mechanism supporting both stuck-at and bridging faults, combined with formal property verification (FPV), enables semantic-level fault criticality grading based on specification-linked assertion violations. A digital-physical co-simulation platform for a field-oriented control (FOC) system is developed to validate SafeGen. Experimental results demonstrate that SafeGen generates higher-quality assertions than existing LLM-based assertion generation frameworks while providing greater semantic interpretability in fault criticality assessment compared with traditional simulation-based approaches.

</details>


### [180] [Cache-Resident LLM Inference in GB-Scale Last-Level Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25353)
*Wanning Zhang,Tongzhou Gu,Marco Canini,Ceyu Xu,Jian Weng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对内存墙问题，提出了一种新型缓存驻留推理执行模型，通过解耦模型权重与KV缓存管理并优化子算子级别的同步机制，在通用CPU上实现了显著的LLM推理加速（最高达13.9倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型推理受限于内存带宽瓶颈，3D堆叠缓存技术虽提供了GB级末级缓存，但在利用该技术时面临流水线加深带来的KV缓存占用增加以及算子间同步开销过大等挑战。现有的执行方式无法充分挖掘片上存储的性能潜力。

Method: 提出了一种面向层级内存集群系统的“缓存驻留推理模型”，核心策略包括：1. 权重与KV缓存的分离架构，支持权重的缓存长驻与KV缓存的独立扩展；2. 执行调度从算子边界细化至子算子依赖级别，降低了同步开销。通过在多插槽CPU集群上的原型系统验证，结合局部性感知安置与专用静态运行时实现。

Result: 在Llama-3.2-3B和Llama-2-7B模型上，相比llama.cpp实现了2.04x至11.51x的TPOT（每个输出token时间）加速；在分析模型验证下，TPOT加速最高可达13.9x。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过以缓存驻留为核心的执行模型、解码状态管理以及依赖感知协作，通用CPU配合GB级末级缓存能够实现极高效率的LLM推理，显著缓解了内存带宽瓶颈。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference is increasingly dominated by data movement across the memory hierarchy. Recent 3D-stacked cache technologies have enabled GB-scale last-level caches in modern server CPUs, making it possible to keep reusable model weights on chip and exploit cache bandwidth and latency. Achieving this regime is not straightforward: deeper pipelining for weight residency increases in-flight requests and KV-cache footprint, while cache-resident operators make operator-boundary synchronization a visible bottleneck.
  We present a cache-resident execution model for inference on hierarchical-memory clustered systems. The model separates weight-centric operators from attention and KV-cache management into dedicated resource domains, keeping reusable weights cache-resident while scaling KV capacity independently of pipeline depth. It also relaxes synchronization from operator boundaries to true sub-operator dependencies, reducing coordination overhead in the cache-resident regime.
  We instantiate this model on a multi-socket CPU cluster with a weight-attention decoupled architecture, locality-aware placement, and a specialized static runtime. The prototype substantially outperforms equally provisioned llama.cpp. On deployed Llama-3.2-3B and Llama-2-7B configurations, it achieves 2.04x-11.51x speedup on time-per-output-token (TPOT). Under a validated analytical model, it further reaches up to 13.9x TPOT speedup across model sizes, context lengths, and batch sizes. These results show that commodity CPUs with GB-scale last-level caches can support efficient LLM inference when execution is organized around cache residency, decoupled state management, and dependency-aware coordination.

</details>


### [181] [Energy-Efficient CNN Acceleration with MSDF Digit-Serial Arithmetic on FPGA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25562)
*Muhammad Usman,Yousef Sadegheih,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种用于FPGA的合并乘加（MMA）架构，通过融合级联运算减少延迟，显著提升了U-Net图像分割加速器的吞吐量和能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的数字串行（尤其是MSDF）算术在级联操作中会产生累积的初始延迟，导致硬件利用率和吞吐量下降。

Method: 提出了合并乘加（MMA）架构，将乘法和加法操作整合至单一流水线中，以消除传统级联算术产生的冗余启动延迟，并结合了并行处理空间输入深度的设计。

Result: FPGA实现达到了15.14 GOPS/W的能效，远超CPU（1.93 GOPS/W），且相比传统的基于MSDF的FPGA实现，能耗降低了约9倍。

Conclusion: 提出的合并乘加（MMA）架构显著提升了U-Net硬件加速的性能与能效，证明了该方法在资源受限且对延迟敏感的边缘计算（如医学影像）应用中的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents an energy-efficient hardware acceleration of the convolutional layers in the U-Net architecture for image segmentation, implemented on FPGA. While digit-serial arithmetic, particularly most-significant-digit-first (MSDF) techniques, offers a compact hardware footprint, it suffers from initial latency before producing the first output digit. This delay accumulates in cascaded operations like multiplication followed by addition, where each unit introduces its own startup overhead. To overcome this, we propose a merged multiply-add (MMA) architecture that fuses these operations into a unified pipeline. Instead of incurring separate delays, the MMA introduces a single streamlined latency per iteration, shorter than the combined latency of conventional cascaded units, resulting in enhanced throughput and efficiency. The MMA units are designed to process spatial input depths in parallel, achieving significantly higher performance than both standalone MSDF-based and conventional designs. We evaluate the proposed design using U-Net as a target application. Despite operating at a lower frequency than a CPU, the FPGA-based accelerator achieves up to an order of magnitude higher energy efficiency, delivering up to $15.14$ GOPS/W compared to $1.93$ GOPS/W for CPU-based inference. The design also shows approximately $9\times$ reduction in energy consumption compared to MSDF-based FPGA implementations. These results highlight the efficacy of the merged arithmetic approach for resource-constrained, latency-sensitive edge applications in medical imaging and computer vision.

</details>


### [182] [Croc: Training the Next Generation Chip Designers on Domain-Specific End-to-End Open Source Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25673)
*Enrico Zelioli,Philippe Sauter,Thomas Benz,Hannah Pochert,Luisa Wüthrich,Beat Muheim,Frank K. Gürkaynak,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的开源SoC设计与流片流程（Croc平台），通过使用开源EDA工具和PDK，成功解决了传统VLSI教学中工具受限的问题，并经实践证明了开源流程在教学与实际芯片制造中的有效性与高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前VLSI教育严重依赖昂贵且受限的商业EDA工具、私有PDK和闭源硬件，导致学生难以获得端到端的芯片设计与流片实战经验，无法满足当下领域专用SoC设计人才的需求。

Method: 基于完全开源的电子设计自动化（EDA）工具链、130nm开源PDK，以及开源RISC-V平台“Croc”。该流程支持指令集扩展、加速器集成及外设定制，涵盖了从架构规格到芯片流片的完整设计闭环。

Result: 在教学实践中，65名学生完成了33个项目，其中30个达到可制造标准，18个入选流片，5个最终成功流片。基准芯片验证结果显示，其功能和指标与使用商业工具链设计的同类产品相当。

Conclusion: 开源工具链与PDK结合开源硬件平台（如Croc）完全能够支持VLSI教学与芯片流片，实现与商业闭源方案相当的性能与功能水平，有效降低了芯片设计的准入门槛。

Abstract: The demand for domain-specific systems-on-chip (SoCs) in artificial intelligence, robotics, and automotive systems is increasing the need for engineers with hands-on expertise on very-large-scale integration (VLSI) design from architecture specification to fabricated silicon. Yet, most VLSI courses rely on restrictively licensed electronic design automation tools and process design kits (PDKs), as well as closed-source hardware designs. We present an end-to-end open-source domain-specific SoC design and fabrication flow built around Croc, a highly customizable RISC-V platform. Built from open-source SystemVerilog intellectual property blocks and integrated with an end-to-end open-source design flow in a 130nm open PDK, Croc enables tapeout projects supporting multiple domain customization options: instruction-set extensions, accelerator co-processors, and peripherals. In our first open-source course experience using Croc, 65 students completed 33 projects, 30 of which produced manufacturable layouts. 18 designs were selected as tapeout candidates, and five were fabricated. A first baseline chip has already been successfully characterized in silicon, demonstrating microcontroller-class functionality and implementation metrics comparable to those of products with similar functional complexity completed with closed-source toolchains and PDKs.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Scheduling with Testing: Competitive Algorithms for Minimizing the Total Weighted Completion Time in the Adversarial Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25166)
*Felix Buld,Andreas S. Schulz*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本论文研究了带测试功能的加权调度问题，提出了首个针对权重作业的常数竞争比算法，在单机和并行机环境下均刷新了最优竞争比记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对抗性调度模型中，现有研究往往集中在无权场景，缺乏针对具有不同权重（代表重要性）作业的调度算法，尤其是在带“测试”环节（以揭示更短执行时间）的情况下。

Method: 采用确定性算法以及随机化算法的变体，并结合列表调度（List Scheduling）策略，针对单机与并行机环境进行优化。

Result: 单机模型下，确定性算法达到 2.3166 的竞争比，随机化算法达到 2.1523；并行机模型下，通过列表调度分别实现 2.7763 和 2.5110 的竞争比，均优于现有最优解。

Conclusion: 本研究为带测试的调度问题（最小化加权完成时间）提供了首个常数竞争比算法，显著提升了单机及并行机模型下的性能评价指标，并在加权场景下达到了与无权场景相当甚至更优的竞争力水平。

Abstract: We study scheduling with testing on a single machine and on identical parallel machines to minimize the total \emph{weighted} completion time in the adversarial model. In this setting, each job is equipped with a weight, an upper bound on its processing time, and a testing time. An algorithm can either execute a job for an amount of time equal to the upper bound or test it first to reveal a potentially lower processing time used to schedule the job later. We establish the first constant-competitive algorithms for this problem with job-dependent weights that reflect each job's relative importance. For single-machine scheduling, we present a deterministic algorithm with a competitive ratio of 2.3166 and show that a randomized variant has a competitive ratio of 2.1523. These guarantees match the best-known upper bounds in the unweighted setting. Combining these algorithms with list scheduling yields competitive ratios of 2.7763 and 2.5110 for identical-parallel-machine scheduling, improving the previously best-known bounds even in the unweighted case.

</details>


### [184] [Multi-Source Reachability in Near-Optimal Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25612)
*Shimon Kogan,Merav Parter*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多源可达性问题的近最优确定性算法，在稠密图中显著优化了计算时间，突破了前人的超二次时间限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决多源可达性问题（即从给定的顶点子集计算可达集合），并克服现有算法在处理大规模图数据时的超二次时间复杂度问题。

Method: 提出了一种确定性算法，其计算复杂度为 $\tilde{O}(n^{\omega(\sigma)})$，其中 $\omega(\sigma)$ 是矩形矩阵乘法指数（将 $n^\sigma \times n$ 矩阵与 $n \times n$ 矩阵相乘）。该方法利用了矩阵乘法在图处理中的高效性。

Result: 对于具有 $n$ 个顶点的有向图，当源集合大小 $|S|=n^\sigma$ 时，该算法实现了 $\tilde{O}(n^{\omega(\sigma)})$ 的运行时间。对于稠密图，当源数量达到 $n^{0.32}$ 时，实现了近线性时间复杂度，超越了 Elkin 和 Trehan 提出的 $n^{1+2/3\omega(\sigma)}$ 随机算法。

Conclusion: 该研究在处理多源可达性问题上取得了显著进展，给出的确定性算法在理论复杂度上优于现有的随机化算法，达到了近乎最优的性能，并突破了稠密图在特定源数量下的超二次时间复杂度限制。

Abstract: The multi-source reachability problem asks to compute the reachable sets from a given subset of source vertices. For $n$-vertex digraphs $G=(V,E)$ and a subset of sources $S \subseteq V$ with $|S|=n^σ$ for some $σ\in [0,1]$, we present a near-optimal deterministic algorithm that solves this problem in $\tilde{O}(n^{ω(σ)})$ time, where $ω(σ)$ is the rectangular matrix multiplication exponent for multiplying an $n^σ\times n$ matrix by an $n \times n$ matrix. For dense graphs, this yields reachability from up to $n^{0.32}$ sources in near-linear time, breaking the super-quadratic time barrier and improving over the state-of-the-art $n^{1+2/3ω(σ)}$-time randomized algorithm of Elkin and Trehan [arXiv:2401.05628, 2024].

</details>


### [185] [Space-Efficient Language Generation in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25777)
*Nicolas Flammarion,Chirag Pabbaraju,Hristo Papazov,Miltiadis Stouras,Ola Svensson*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了资源受限下的语言生成理论，证明了从多项式空间到指数空间的学习能力存在尖锐转变，并在有限内存下给出了生成间隙的最优界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的情况下（如内存受限的流式学习），研究从正例流中学习目标语言的可行性与边界，特别是解决如何在大规模有限自动机（DFA）假设空间中实现高效学习的问题。

Method: 1. 框架构建：定义了“极限语言生成”并引入资源限制。
2. 算法设计：提出了一种多项式空间流式算法，通过DFA假设类进行学习。
3. 理论分析：通过通信复杂性归约（Communication Complexity Reduction）证明了下界，并分析了生成间隙（Generation Gap）与内存使用之间的数量关系。

Result: 1. 在指数空间 regime 下，学习者可以精确识别目标语言 K。
2. 在多项式空间 regime 下，开发出一种使用 poly(s, k) 空间的算法，其生成间隙 Δ 为 O(k^{2s-2})，且能覆盖所有长度至少为 2s-1 的字符串。
3. 证明了下界：若要实现 Δ ≤ k^{(1-ε)s} 的间隙，必须消耗 k^{Ω(εs)} 的内存。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了语言生成任务中多项式空间近似学习与指数空间精确学习之间的尖锐边界（Sharp Transition）。证明了在有限内存限制下，学习算法可以在多项式空间内实现有界的生成间隙，并给出了相应的下界。

Abstract: We initiate a resource-aware theory of \textit{language generation in the limit} under the minimal constraint of space efficiency. In our framework, a learner observes an adversarial positive stream from a target language $K$ and must eventually output a hallucination-free hypothesis language $L \subseteq K$ while omitting at most $Δ$ strings of $K$. We focus on $\mathcal{C}_{s,k}$, the collection of languages recognized by DFAs with at most $s$ states over an alphabet of size $k$, as the natural hypothesis class for memory-bounded learners. In the exponential-space regime, we prove that a learner can exactly identify the target $K$. Under a stricter memory budget, we characterize the strongest possible generation guarantees. In particular, we present a streaming algorithm using $\mathrm{poly}(s,k)$ space that converges to a hypothesis with generation gap $Δ= O(k^{2s-2})$. Moreover, the learned hypothesis captures every string in $K$ of length at least $2s-1$. We complement this result with a near-matching lower bound through a reduction from a standard communication complexity problem. Specifically, achieving generation gap $Δ\le k^{(1-\varepsilon)s}$ requires $k^{Ω(\varepsilon s)}$ memory. Together, these results reveal a sharp transition between polynomial-space generation and exponential-space exact identification.

</details>


### [186] [Paths and Intersections: Recognizing Outerplanar Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25827)
*Yu Chen,Zihan Tan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文解决了外平面图中的距离实现问题，证明其缺乏局部刻画，并提出了一个以终端数为多项式时间复杂度的求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究给定度量空间 $D$ 是否能由一个包含终端集 $T$ 的外平面图来实现，并探索此类问题是否存在局部刻画。

Method: 通过将图视为路径及其交集的组合来分析图结构，并设计了一种针对外平面图距离实现问题的多项式时间算法。

Result: 证明了该问题不存在局部刻画，且提出了一种运行时间关于终端集大小 $|T|$ 为多项式的算法。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了外平面图距离实现问题不存在局部刻画，并提供了一种基于路径及其交集分析图结构的高效多项式时间算法。

Abstract: We study the following distance realization problem: given a metric $D$ on a set $T$ of terminals, does there exist an (edge-weighted) outerplanar graph $G$, such that $T\subseteq V(G)$, and for every pair $t,t'\in T$, $\textsf{dist}_G(t,t')=D(t,t')$? We first prove that there is no ``local characterization'', forming a contrast with trees and Okamura-Seymour instances. Our main result is an efficient algorithm for this problem whose running time is polynomial in $|T|$.
  Both our proof and our algorithm utilize a recent new approach of analyzing graph structures, by viewing graphs as paths and their intersections, which we believe is of independent interest.

</details>


### [187] [Parameterized Complexity of Power Network Design: Coordinating Cable Placement is Hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25859)
*Thekla Hamm,Bart M. P. Jansen,Faezeh Motiei*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究了具有电源负载平衡约束和挖沟成本共享的Steiner树变体，证明了大多数在终端数参数下是W[1]-hard的，并针对特定场景提供了XP算法及FPT转换策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决实际电力网络设计中，由于电力负载限制（多树结构）及地下电缆铺设成本共享机制（共同沟渠）导致的Steiner树问题的复杂变体设计。

Method: 使用参数化复杂度分析；针对低压网络在平面图上采用了基于解子图树宽结构界限的XP算法；利用Grid Tiling规约证明了复杂度的下界（基于ETH）；并在特定成本模型下进行了变体转化以实现FPT。

Result: 大部分所研究的变体在以终端数为参数时是W[1]-hard的；针对低压网络，给出了平面图下的XP算法；对于高压网络，即使在平面图上证明了W[1]-hard；在修改后的成本共享模型下，原先的困难问题变得可处理（FPT）。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了电力网络设计中变体问题的计算复杂性，展示了在不同拓扑限制和成本模型下，大多数问题为W[1]-hard，但在特定成本共享模型下可实现固定参数可处理性（FPT）。

Abstract: We study generalizations of the Steiner Tree problem motivated by the design of power networks. While Steiner Tree asks for a single minimum-cost tree connecting given terminal vertices, a power network typically consists of multiple trees, each connecting a subset of the terminals, to avoid electrical overloads. The cost of installing depends on both the cable lengths and the cost of digging underground trenches for putting the cables where the digging costs can be shared. These leads to variants of Steiner Tree where the goal is to compute a minimum-cost set of Steiner trees with a common root, that together connect all terminals while balancing the power demand of the terminals in each tree. Two important variants arise depending on whether the network is intended for low-voltage or high-voltage power. In the low-voltage case, power loss imposes a bound on the maximum depth of each tree, while no such restriction applies in the high-voltage case. We study the parameterized complexity of several power network design problems, parameterized by the number of terminals. While Steiner Tree is fixed-parameter tractable under this parameterization, most of our variants are W[1]-hard. For low-voltage networks, we present an XP-algorithm for planar inputs based on structural bounds on the treewidth of solution subgraphs. We also give a reduction from Grid Tiling showing tightness under ETH. The XP-algorithm extends to the high-voltage setting and general graphs, albeit at a cost in the running time. For high-voltage networks, we show the problem remains W[1]-hard even on planar graphs. Finally, we explore a variant of the cost model for sharing digging costs in which both problems become fixed-parameter tractable.

</details>


### [188] [A Stronger Conditional Running-Time Lower Bound for Global Label Min-Cut](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25875)
*Yuanhao Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文通过确定性的归约证明，在指数时间假设（ETH）下，将边标注图算法的运行时间复杂性下界从 $(np)^{o\left(\frac{\log n}{(\log\log n)^2}\right)}$ 提高到了 $(np)^{o\left(\frac{\log n}{\log\log n}\right)}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了加强在指数时间假设（ETH）下，关于边标注图（具有 $n$ 个顶点和 $p$ 个标签）相关算法运行时间下界的理论紧密度。

Method: 提出了一种确定性的归约方法（deterministic reduction），通过更严密的构造论证了该问题在算法复杂性下界上的改进。

Result: 成功证明了在该问题上不存在时间复杂度优于 $(np)^{o\left(\frac{\log n}{\log\log n}\right)}$ 的确定性算法（基于 ETH）。

Conclusion: 通过确定性的归约方法，将边标注图相关的计算问题的条件运行时间下界从 $(np)^{o\left(\frac{\log n}{(\log\log n)^2}\right)}$ 提升至 $(np)^{o\left(\frac{\log n}{\log\log n}\right)}$（在指数时间假设 ETH 下有效）。

Abstract: Let $n$ and $p$ denote the numbers of vertices and labels, respectively, in an undirected edge-labeled graph. Previous work showed that, under the Exponential Time Hypothesis (ETH), there is no deterministic algorithm with running time \[ (np)^{o\left(\frac{\log n}{(\log\log n)^2}\right)}. \] In this paper, we give a deterministic reduction that strengthens this conditional running-time lower bound to \[ (np)^{o\left(\frac{\log n}{\log\log n}\right)}. \]

</details>


### [189] [On the Parameterized Complexity of Bounded-Density Vertex Deletion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26012)
*Jakob Raupach,Tom-Lukas Breitkopf,Anton Herrmann,André Nichterlein*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了“有界密度点删除”问题的参数化复杂度，证明了其在 treewidth 等常见参数下是 W[1]-困难的，并在特定条件下给出了 FPT 算法，完善了计算复杂度画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 Bazgan 等人（JCSS, 2025）留下的关于 BDVD 在 treewidth 参数下复杂度的公开问题，并完善该问题的参数化复杂度分析。

Method: 使用参数化复杂度分析工具，证明了BDVD在 treedepth、feedback vertex number 和 treewidth 参数下是 W[1]-困难的；在 max leaf number 和 vertex integrity 参数下是 FPT 的；当目标密度为常数时，在 cliquewidth 参数下是 FPT 的。

Result: BDVD 在 treewidth 等参数下是 W[1]-困难的，但在某些更强参数（如 vertex integrity）或特定密度约束下是 FPT 的。

Conclusion: 本文刻画了“有界密度点删除”（BDVD）问题的参数化复杂度图景，明确了不同结构参数下该问题的可解性边界（FPT与W[1]-困难）。

Abstract: We explore the parameterized complexity of Bounded Density Vertex Deletion (BDVD): given a graph $G$, an integer budget $k$, and a target density $τ_ρ$, the task is to determine whether the density (i.e. number of edges divided by number of vertices) of the densest subgraph of $G$ can be reduced to at most $τ_ρ$ by deleting at most $k$ vertices. Our primary focus is on structural graph parameters related to treewidth, as the parameterized complexity of BDVD with respect to treewidth was left as open question by Bazgan et al. [JCSS, 2025]. We resolve this question by showing W[1]-hardness with respect to various parameters, including treedepth and feedback vertex number. These results imply W[1]-hardness with respect to treewidth. We obtain positive results for parameters larger than treedepth and feedback vertex number, namely we show BDVD is in FPT parameterized by the max leaf number or vertex integrity. Under the assumption that the target density $τ_ρ$ is a fixed constant the parameterized complexity landscape of BDVD changes drastically, allowing a fixed-parameter tractable algorithm even for parameters smaller than treewidth, namely cliquewidth. Altogether, our results provide a refined complexity landscape for Bounded Density Vertex Deletion, sharply distinguishing between tractable and intractable parameter regimes under structural parameterizations.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Agentic evolution of physically constrained foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25532)
*Jiangwei Zhang,Wen Sun,Chong Wang,Shiyao Li,Cheng Che,Chunjing Han,Dan Meng,Jian Yang,Yu Wang,Rui Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种具备物理约束的 AI 多智能体框架，利用进化知识图谱成功自动设计出高性能的硬件感知大模型压缩算法，实现了在资源受限环境下的超大规模模型高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大模型通用智能体缺乏物理基础（physical grounding），在进行硬件设计时常产生幻觉，导致生成的设计无法在实际硬件上运行。

Method: 提出了一种物理锚定的多智能体发现引擎，利用“进化知识图谱”（Evolutionary Knowledge Graph）结构化历史科学创新，并提取“算法思维链”（algorithmic Chain-of-Thought）以将随机搜索转化为定向结构进化，同时引入带宽高效的敏感度配置文件（Sensitivity Profile）来满足硬件约束。

Result: 成功进化出两种优于人类启发式方法的硬件感测压缩算法（Q-Enhance 和 MoE-Salient-AQ）；在超低位（sub-3-bit）机制下性能超过现有最优人工设计 3.7%；实现将 235B 参数模型部署在双 A100 服务器上，内存占用降低 75%，精度损失仅 0.64%。

Conclusion: 通过将无约束的组合搜索转化为知识驱动的自主探索，该研究建立了一种在严格物理边界内实现机器驱动发现的可扩展软硬件协同设计范式。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence increasingly drives automated scientific discovery, yet contemporary generalist agents lack physical grounding, frequently hallucinating hardware-incompatible designs. Here, we present a physically grounded, multi-agent discovery engine that autonomously architects hardware-compliant computing systems. Anchored by an Evolutionary Knowledge Graph structuring past scientific innovations, the framework extracts an "algorithmic Chain-of-Thought" to transform blind stochastic search into directed structural evolution. Applied to the extreme testbed of foundation model deployment, the engine evolved two hardware-aware compression methodologies surpassing human-engineered heuristics: Q-Enhance mitigates long-context accuracy loss in dense models, and MoE-Salient-AQ outperforms state-of-the-art manual sparse Mixture-of-Experts designs by 3.7% at sub-3-bit regimes. Utilizing a bandwidth-efficient Sensitivity Profile, we successfully deployed a massive 235-billion-parameter model onto a constrained dual-A100 server, reducing memory requirements by 75% with a marginal 0.64% accuracy degradation. By transforming unconstrained combinatorial search into knowledge-driven autonomy, this establishes a scalable hardware-software co-design paradigm for machine-driven discovery within strict physical boundaries.

</details>


### [191] [The Hitchhiker's Guide to Agentic AI: From Foundations to Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24937)
*Haggai Roitman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本书是构建自主智能体AI系统的全栈实战指南，系统性地串联了从LLM模型基础、推理对齐到智能体记忆、多智能体协作及生产部署的完整技术链条。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为开发者提供一本关于构建自主智能体AI系统的综合指南，解决当前智能体开发中分散学习、缺乏系统化全栈认知的问题。

Method: 采用系统工程的自底向上方法，组织结构上涵盖了：1. LLM基座（Transformer、微调、推理优化）；2. 对齐与推理层（RLHF、DPO、思维链）；3. 智能体核心设计（RAG、记忆系统、设计模式）；4. 多智能体协作（通信协议、拓扑结构）；5. 开发框架与生产部署。

Result: 提供了一套完整的智能体开发参考知识体系，涵盖了理论基础、代码实践、评估方法及生产环境部署的全生命周期指导。

Conclusion: 构建高性能智能体系统需要掌握从底层LLM基座到高层多智能体协同的全技术栈，而非单一层面。该书为开发实践提供了理论与工程相结合的完整参考框架。

Abstract: The Hitchhiker's Guide to Agentic AI is a comprehensive practitioner's reference for building autonomous AI systems. The book covers the full stack from first principles to production deployment, organized around a central thesis: building great agentic systems requires understanding every layer of the pipeline, not just one. The book opens with the LLM substrate -- transformer architecture, GPU systems, training and fine-tuning (SFT,LoRA, MoE), model compression, and inference optimization -- treated as essential foundations rather than the primary focus. It then develops the alignment and reasoning layer: reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), PPO, DPO and its variants, GRPO, reward modeling, and RL for large reasoning models including chain-of-thought and test-time scaling. The second half is devoted to agentic AI proper. Topics include agentic training and trajectory-based RL, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG and Agentic RAG), memory systems (in-context, external, episodic, and semantic), agent harness design and context management, and a taxonomy of agent design patterns. Inter-agent coordination is covered in depth: the Model Context Protocol (MCP), agent skills and tool use, the Agent-to-Agent (A2A) communication protocol, and multi-agent architectures spanning centralized, decentralized, and hierarchical topologies. The book concludes with agent development frameworks, agentic UI design, evaluation methodology for agentic tasks, and production deployment. Each chapter pairs rigorous theoretical foundations with implementation guidance, code examples, and references to the primary literature.

</details>


### [192] [Critique of Agent Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23991)
*Eric Xing,Mingkai Deng,Jinyu Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了AI“代理”的本质，通过定义代理的五个维度，区分了外挂式“代理系统”和内生式“自主代理系统”，并提出了一种名为GIC的架构以实现通用的自主智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于LLM的“代理”系统激增且伴随着对潜在巨大影响的担忧，学界缺乏对“代理”本质的清晰定义。研究旨在界定自动化与自主性（Agency）的边界，区分工具性系统与真正具备自主能力的系统，从而明确系统构建的方向并评估相关风险。

Method: 借鉴笛卡尔关于代理的哲学观点及科幻作品中的自主存在概念，从目标、身份、决策、自我调节和学习五个维度分析AI代理架构。提出了一种新型架构方案——目标-身份-配置器（Goal-Identity-Configurator, GIC）。

Result: 定义了评估代理的五个核心维度。提出了GIC模型，该模型整合了分层目标分解、身份演化、基于世界模型的模拟推理、自我调节机制及自我导向学习，旨在实现更具自主性的智能体行为。

Conclusion: 区分了“代理（agentic）系统”（能力源于外部脚手架）与“自主代理（agentive）系统”（能力源于系统内部）。提出了GIC架构以实现真正的自主性，并论述了在保持人类监督的前提下，对具备高度自主性的系统进行审计、控制和安全治理的重要性。

Abstract: What is an agent? What constitutes agency? With the rise of Large Language Model (LLM) systems marketed as ``coding agents'', ``AI co-scientists'', and other ``agentic" tools that promise to drive up productivity, and at the same time, ``existential" concerns such as AI escaping human control with destructive power under a speculative ``machine agency" against humans, it has become essential to clarify where automation ends and agency begins, both for building capable systems and for understanding whether and what to fear. Drawing on Descartes' grounding of agency in independent thought, and on portrayals of autonomous beings in science fiction, we survey the current landscape of AI agents, and analyze agent architectures along five dimensions: goal, identity, decision-making, self-regulation, and learning. Specifically, we argue that genuine agency requires these structures to be \emph{internalized within the system itself} rather than assembled through external scaffolding. This distinction between \emph{agentic} systems, whose competence resides in engineered workflows, and \emph{agentive} systems, whose capabilities (including social interaction) arise endogenously, defines the boundary between systems designed for prescribed tasks, and those capable of operating in the open world with true autonomy. Building on this analysis, we propose the Goal-Identity-Configurator (GIC) architecture for a general-purpose agent model, combining hierarchical goal decomposition, identity evolution, simulative reasoning grounded in a separately trained world model, learned self-regulation, and self-directed learning from both real and simulated experience. Furthermore, we share insight on the auditability, controllability, and safety of agentive systems that possess greater autonomy and ``agency", but remain under human oversight.

</details>


### [193] [Ensemble Feature Selection and Harris Hawks Optimization for Explainable Mental Health Risk Prediction in Female Sex Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24047)
*Ahnaf Atef Choudhury,Md. Parvej Hoque Palash,Shahriar Siddique Ayon,Ramkrishna Saha,Abdullah Al Mamun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者提出了一种集成特征选择与哈里斯鹰优化逻辑回归的混合模型，有效预测了女性性工作者的抑郁风险，并通过XAI技术识别出关键创伤因素，为该群体的精准干预提供了科学依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 女性性工作者（FSW）面临严重的心理健康风险（如抑郁），但现有机器学习模型难以有效捕捉该群体复杂、高维的风险特征。

Method: 提出了一种混合预测模型：采用基于ANOVA和互信息的集成特征选择策略，并结合哈里斯鹰优化算法（HHO）调整逻辑回归（LR）参数，同时集成了可解释人工智能（XAI）方法以分析特征影响。

Result: 在3,005名FSW的数据集上，模型表现优异，准确率达95.78%，F1分数达95.77%，AUC值为0.96；成功识别出创伤后应激、客户相关暴力及职业因素为导致抑郁的主要贡献因子。

Conclusion: 该研究成功弥合了传统方法与机器学习在心理健康预测中的差距，提出的XAI工具不仅能提升预测精度，还能通过量化关键风险因素（如创伤后应激、暴力和职业因素），为弱势群体的早期干预、循证心理社会护理及健康规划提供有力支持。

Abstract: One of the significant mental health issues affecting female sex workers (FSWs) is mental disorders, especially depression. Exposure to violence, stigma, and economic hardship further increases their psychological risk. Current machine learning (ML) models are typically ineffective at capturing the high-dimensional and complex risk patterns that exist in this marginalized group. This paper suggests a hybrid predictive model that merges an ensemble feature selection strategy using ANOVA and mutual information and Harris Hawks optimization-tuned logistic regression and represents a new application of swarm intelligence to predict mental health in vulnerable groups. The explainable AI (XAI) methods can be used to understand the factors of trauma associated with model predictions. When applied to a group of 3,005 FSWs, it can be seen that the proposed model is more effective than traditional classifiers, with an accuracy of 95.78%, an F1 score of 95.77%, and an AUC of 0.96, and identifying post-traumatic stress, client-related violence, and occupational factors as major contributors to depression. This work bridges the gaps between conventional and ML approaches to develop an XAI tool that enables vulnerable groups to receive early assistance, evidence-based targeted psychosocial care, and health planning.

</details>


### [194] [Reasonable Motion: A General ASP Foundation for Environment Constrained Movement Trajectory Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25626)
*Julius Monsen,Jakob Suchan,Mehul Bhatt,Lars Karlsson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于答案集编程（ASP）的混合推理方法，通过将运动行为建模为稳定模型，实现了自动驾驶中复杂轨迹的可解释计算与验证，优于传统的纯数据驱动方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有处理动态目标运动的方法（特别是纯机器学习方法）缺乏可解释性和可验证性，难以在需要严格逻辑遵循的复杂动态领域（如自动驾驶）中提供明确的推理依据。

Method: 提出了一种基于答案集编程（ASP）的混合定量-定性方法。通过对环境图（environment graph）进行约束遍历，将几何可行的运动行为枚举为稳定模型（stable models），每个模型代表一种包含事件序列、拓扑结构和领域规范的轨迹模式。

Result: 在 Argoverse 2 大型自动驾驶基准数据集上的实证评估表明，该方法能够有效地计算出受限的分支轨迹模式，并能清晰溯源至其底层的逻辑稳定模型。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种具有可解释性和可验证性的计算框架，能够克服纯机器学习方法在处理动态环境轨迹预测时的“黑盒”局限，在自动驾驶等领域展现了应用潜力。

Abstract: We present a general answer set programming based hybrid quantitative-qualitative method for computing constrained branching trajectory modes for moving objects in real-world settings. The method performs constrained traversal of an environment graph, enumerating geometrically admissible motion behaviours as stable models, each constituting a distinct trajectory mode characterised by both domain-dependent and independent factors such as derived event sequence, map topology, and domain norms. The hybrid trajectory computation method is generally applicable across motion characteristics typically encountered in diverse dynamic domains with moving objects, e.g., autonomous driving. We demonstrate applicability and highlight how computed trajectories are traceable to their underlying stable model, thereby affording verifiable interpretability that purely learned approaches cannot provide. We also perform an empirical evaluation with Argoverse 2, a large-scale real-world autonomous driving benchmark representative of the class of dynamic domains within the scope of the proposed method.

</details>


### [195] [PHANTOM: A Large-Scale Dataset of Multimodal Adversarial Attacks for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24388)
*Simone Gallivanone,Hossein Khodadadi,Mauro Dore,Mauro Medda,Nicola Franco*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者发布了一个大规模、开源的VLM对抗性攻击数据集，旨在解决生成攻击样本算力成本高的问题，促进VLM安全性、模型鲁棒性评估及防御机制的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于对抗性样本的生成过程计算成本高且复杂，为了推动VLM安全性研究，研究者需要开放且高质量的数据集以进行系统性的模型鲁棒性和安全性评估。

Method: 采用了多种前沿的对抗性攻击策略，整合并扩展了多个现有基准测试，涵盖了10个大类和55个细分领域的有害意图，最终构建包含7,826个意图共47,524个对抗性样本的数据集。

Result: 成功构建并发布了一个包含47,524个对抗样本的开放数据集，覆盖了广泛的有害意图，并提供了比现有单一基准更广的覆盖范围。

Conclusion: 该大规模开源数据集为视觉语言模型（VLM）的鲁棒性和安全性评估提供了一套标准化、全面的资源，降低了对抗性研究的门槛，有助于开发更安全、更健壮的AI模型。

Abstract: We introduce a large-scale, open-source dataset of pre-generated adversarial attacks for vision-language models (VLMs). The dataset is designed to be diverse, representative, and practical, extending existing benchmarks by covering 10 high-level categories and 55 subcategories of harmful intents. Our primary goal is to make adversarial data accessible to the research community, given the computational cost and complexity of generating large numbers of attacks. The dataset comprises 47 524 adversarial samples, generated using state-of-the-art attack strategies from recent literature. Our work complements existing efforts by consolidating and extending prior benchmarks from multiple established sources, resulting in 7 826 intents, and introduce an additional category to broaden coverage. This provides realistic evaluation resources for studying model robustness and alignment. Our dataset intends to enable researchers and practitioners to systematically evaluate the robustness and safety of VLMs, fine-tune attack-generation models, and develop or stress-test defensive guardrails under diverse adversarial conditions. By releasing this resource, we aim to lower the barrier to adversarial research and foster more reproducible, comprehensive, and comparable evaluations of VLM safety.

</details>


### [196] [ASALT: Adaptive State Alignment for Lateral Transfer in Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24601)
*Anurag Akula,Satheesh K. Perepu,Abhishek Sarkar,Kaushik Dey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ASALT 框架，利用适配器技术解决 MARL 中跨维度异构任务的策略迁移问题，提升了迁移效率并抑制了负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体强化学习（MARL）迁移学习方法大多要求源域与目标域的观测空间和全局状态空间维度一致，这限制了其在实际异构环境中的应用。作者旨在解决状态空间维度不匹配导致无法进行高效知识迁移的问题。

Method: 提出了 ASALT (Adapter-based State-space Alignment for MARL Transfer) 方法。通过在观察层和状态层引入映射适配器（Adapters），将不同维度的目标域数据映射到统一的潜空间（Shared Embedding Space），实现智能体策略在异构域间的知识迁移。

Result: 在标准基准环境下，ASALT 在协作任务中的样本效率和全局收益均超过了现有基线模型，并表现出良好的抗负迁移能力。研究发现，该方法的有效性与源域和目标域之间的匹配程度正相关。

Conclusion: ASALT 通过引入观察值和全局状态适配器，成功克服了多智能体强化学习中跨异构域（状态空间维度不匹配）的迁移挑战，在提升样本效率和收益的同时，有效缓解了负迁移现象。其性能上限受限于任务间差异度。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) addresses the problem of training multiple agents that pursue collaborative, competitive, or mixed objectives. Prior work has investigated transfer learning between source and target domains in MARL; however, the majority of existing approaches impose the constraint that the dimensionalities of the observation space and the global state space must be identical across domains. In this paper, we introduce a method that explicitly accommodates mismatched state-space dimensionalities between source and target domains. The proposed approach, ASALT, incorporates both observation-level and state-level adapters that map the target-domain observations and global states into a shared embedding space, thereby enabling more effective transfer of knowledge across both actors and critics. These adapters can generate embeddings that support efficient strategy transfer across heterogeneous domains. Experimental results on multiple configurations in standard benchmark environments demonstrate that ASALT surpasses existing baselines in terms of sample efficiency and global return in cooperative settings, but its effectiveness depends on the degree of mismatch between source and target domains. Furthermore, our findings indicate that ASALT mitigates negative transfer, which frequently constitutes a major obstacle when transferring policies between domains with differing observation and action spaces.

</details>
